### [1] [Source-to-Source Transformations for GPU Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13864)
*Julien de Castelnau,Thomas Koehler,Arthur Charguéraud,Clément Pit-Claudel*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: OptiGPU是一个通过证明保留编译技术实现GPU安全编程的系统，能够在保证数据竞争自由、死锁自由和全功能正确性的同时，最小化对底层控制力和额外工作量的权衡。它将优化的GPU程序通过源到源变换精化自已验证的CPU程序，从而避免直接验证复杂GPU程序的证明负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU在现代高性能计算中不可或缺，但其并发编程因同步原语和数据移动的显式管理而极易出现微妙的并发错误。现有安全GPU编程方法在三个维度存在权衡：保证的强度、允许的底层控制程度、以及实现安全保证所需的额外工作量。需要一种能在这三个维度上最小化权衡的方案。

Method: 将**证明保留编译**（proof-preserving compilation）应用于GPU编程：通过精化（refinement）已验证的简单CPU程序，逐步应用源到源变换来推导出优化的GPU程序，这些变换自动保留原有证明的正确性。实现基于OptiTrust框架扩展，支持核函数启动、共享内存、同步屏障等GPU核心特性。

Result: 通过矩阵转置和树形并行归约两个案例研究，证明OptiGPU能够推导出与手写参考实现相匹配的CUDA代码，同时保留了强安全保证。

Conclusion: OptiGPU证明了证明保留编译在GPU编程领域的可行性，在提供强安全保证（数据竞争自由、死锁自由、全功能正确性）的同时，有效减轻了直接验证复杂GPU程序的证明负担，并在底层控制力和额外工作量之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: GPUs have become essential in modern high performance computing, but programming them correctly remains a significant challenge. This difficulty arises from subtle concurrency bugs that result from the explicit management of synchronization primitives and data movement across intricate hierarchies of memory and parallel threads. At the same time, the ability to control these aspects explicitly is at the core of the performance gains granted by GPUs. These challenges have motivated interest in safe GPU programming: tools and languages that can prevent or detect such bugs statically. However, existing approaches make tradeoffs in three dimensions: the strength of their guarantees, the degree of low-level control they allow, and the amount of additional effort required to achieve these safety guarantees. This thesis presents OptiGPU, a system for GPU programming with strong safety guarantees-data race freedom, deadlock freedom, and full functional correctness-that minimizes tradeoffs in the other two dimensions compared to previous approaches. OptiGPU applies proof-preserving compilation to GPU programming, allowing verification of low-level, optimized GPU programs through refinement of simple, verified CPU programs. An OptiGPU user thus avoids the substantial proof burden of directly verifying complex optimized GPU programs, instead directing this refinement with source-to-source transformations that automatically preserve proofs. OptiGPU is implemented as a set of extensions to OptiTrust, an existing framework for proof-preserving compilation on CPUs. OptiGPU models essential GPU programming features, including kernel launches, shared memory, and synchronous barriers, and produces both device and host-side code. We evaluate OptiGPU on two case studies, matrix transpose and tree-based parallel reduction, showing it can derive CUDA code matching techniques found in handwritten references.

</details>


### [2] [Quantitative Symbolic Patch Impact Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13885)
*Laboni Sarker,Abdus Satter,Tevfik Bultan*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出定量部分等价分析（Quantitative Partial Equivalence Analysis），通过符号分析量化原始代码与补丁代码之间的行为差异，识别导致行为一致或不同的输入条件，并基于范围搜索启发式方法给出等价性的可靠下界。在90个CVE补丁和Juliet测试集上验证了效果，并在EqBench基准中发现5个被误标为等价的程序对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统等价性检查只能将程序分类为等价或不等价，无法满足补丁影响分析等任务的需求——在这些任务中，补丁版本与原始版本预期不等价。当两个程序版本不等价时，确定它们在什么条件下存在差异以及受影响的输入比例仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 采用符号分析技术，识别补丁前后程序表现出相同或不同行为的输入条件；针对数值域的高效定量分析，提出基于范围的搜索启发式方法，提供等价性的可靠下界。

Result: 在90个来自Linux、Qemu、FFmpeg等开源项目的CVE补丁以及基于Juliet Test Suite的CWE数据集上验证了有效性；在EqBench基准中发现5个被误标为等价的C程序对，并识别出它们行为差异的输入条件。

Conclusion: 定量部分等价分析能有效表征和量化补丁影响，为补丁评估提供比传统二元等价性判断更丰富的信息。

Abstract: Traditional equivalence checking classifies programs as equivalent or non-equivalent, providing insufficient information for tasks like patch impact analysis where it is expected the patched version of the program to be non-equivalent to the original program. When two program versions are non-equivalent, determining under what conditions they differ and what percentage of inputs are affected remains an open challenge. In this work, we introduce quantitative partial equivalence analysis, an approach for assessing software patches by quantifying behavioral differences between the original (vulnerable) code and the patched code. Using symbolic analysis, we identify input conditions under which patched and original programs exhibit identical or divergent behaviors. Our approach refines non-equivalence by measuring the extent of behavioral divergence across the input domain. For efficient quantitative analysis of numerical domains, we propose a range-based search heuristic that provides a sound lower bound on equivalence. We demonstrate our approach on 90 CVE patches from widely used open-source projects (Linux, Qemu, FFmpeg), as well as on a Juliet Test Suite-based dataset containing programs with CWEs. Our results show that quantitative partial equivalence analysis effectively characterizes and quantifies patch impact. Additionally, experiments on the EqBench benchmark reveal five C program pairs that are mislabeled as equivalent, and we identify the input conditions under which their behaviors diverge.

</details>


### [3] [Linear-Time T-Gate Optimization via Random Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13929)
*Aws Albarghouthi*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TZAP的T门优化工具，其核心是基于随机化静态分析的线性时间复杂度相位折叠算法。该静态分析不追踪符号表达式，而是传播固定宽度的比特串，从而能够以任意高概率可靠地逼近可达量子态集合。TZAP比现有最先进工具（如PyZX、VOQC、Feynman）快数个数量级，在标准基准测试中达到相近的T门缩减效果，且能在笔记本电脑上数秒内优化包含数百万门的电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算实现优势需要容错，而容错方案中T门无法横向实现，需依赖代价昂贵的魔法态蒸馏协议，导致T门计数成为大规模量子计算资源瓶颈。现有T门优化工具无法扩展到量子优势所需的电路规模。

Method: 提出线性时间随机化相位折叠算法，基于新型随机化静态分析：不追踪符号表达式，而是传播固定宽度比特串，以任意高概率可靠逼近可达量子态集合。

Result: 实现工具TZAP，比PyZX、VOQC、Feynman等最先进工具快数个数量级，在标准基准测试中达到相近的T门缩减效果，能在笔记本电脑上数秒内优化含数百万门的电路。

Conclusion: 提出的随机化静态分析方法有效解决了T门优化在大规模电路上的可扩展性问题，为通往量子优势扫清了T计数瓶颈障碍。

Abstract: Quantum computers promise exponential speedups for problems in cryptography, chemistry, and optimization. Realizing this promise requires fault tolerance: physical qubits are noisy, so logical qubits must be encoded redundantly across many physical ones using quantum error-correcting codes. In most practical fault-tolerance schemes, T gates cannot be implemented transversally and instead require costly magic-state distillation protocols involving a complex set of operations. As a result, T-gate count can dominate the resource budget of large-scale quantum computations, making T-count minimization a central bottleneck on the path to quantum advantage. Existing T-count optimization tools, however, do not scale to the circuits that quantum advantage demands.
  We present theoretical and practical results on T-gate optimization. On the theoretical side, we give a linear-time randomized algorithm for phase folding, based on a novel randomized static analysis. Our static analysis soundly approximates the set of reachable quantum states with an arbitrarily high probability. Our key insight is a static analysis that does not track symbolic expressions, but propagates constant-width bitstrings down the circuit. On the practical side, our implementation, TZAP, is multiple orders of magnitude faster than state-of-the-art tools -- such as PyZX, VOQC, and Feynman -- closely matches their T-count reductions on standard benchmarks, and within seconds on a laptop computer can optimize circuits with millions of gates.

</details>


### [4] [Mat2Boundary: Treating User-Defined Boundary Condition as SpMV for Distributed PDE Solvers on Block-Structured Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14780)
*Yanzheng Cai,Mingzhe Zhang,Shengqi Chen,Haoyuan Song,Wenguang Chen*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Mat2Boundary是一个将边界条件处理抽象为仿射稀疏线性算子的DSL和编译器，在结构化/块结构化网格上支持多种BC类型，通过多阶段编程和多面体分析生成高效无矩阵内核，在浅水方程求解器和HPCG上实现最高7.6倍加速、减少70%以上代码量，并扩展到1344核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界条件处理是PDE求解器在结构化和块结构化网格上复杂性的主要来源，尤其是在高阶方法和分布式内存执行场景下，现有方法缺乏统一且高效的抽象。

Method: 将边界条件建模为仿射稀疏线性算子，统一了halo拷贝、循环/对称映射、零填充、块边界同步和用户定义插值；结合多阶段编程和多面体分析生成无矩阵内核（结构化情况），支持用户定义稀疏矩阵（非规则情况），消除冗余边界计算，并为分布式执行合成可复用的通信调度。

Result: 在立方球网格上的两个浅水方程求解器和HPCG基准测试中，BC内核加速最高达7.6倍，BC代码量减少超过70%，可扩展到1344个CPU核心，并行效率为72%-88%。

Conclusion: Mat2Boundary通过统一的仿射稀疏线性算子抽象和编译优化，显著降低了边界条件处理的复杂性和代码量，同时提升了性能和可扩展性，证明该抽象在广谱BC类型上高效可行。

Abstract: Boundary-condition (BC) handling is a major source of complexity in PDE solvers on structured and block-structured grids, especially for high-order methods and distributed-memory execution. We present Mat2Boundary, a DSL and compiler for boundary computations that models a broad class of boundary-conditions as affine sparse linear operators. This abstraction unifies halo copying, circular and symmetric mappings, zero padding, block-edge synchronization, and user-defined interpolation, while exposing a modular basic sub-matrix interface for declarative composition. To make this representation efficient, Mat2Boundary combines multi-stage programming and polyhedral analysis to generate matrix-free kernels for structured cases, support user-defined sparse matrices for irregular cases, eliminate redundant boundary work, and synthesize reusable communication schedules for distributed execution. Evaluated on two shallow-water equation solvers on cubed-sphere grids and HPCG, Mat2Boundary achieves up to 7.6$\times$ BC-kernel speedup, reduces BC code by over 70%, and scales to 1,344 CPU cores with 72%-88% efficiency.

</details>

### [5] [Neural Code Translation of Legacy Code: APL to C#](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13896)
*Abdulrahman Ramadan,Hanen Borchani,Iben Lilholm,Mikkel Almind,Allan Peter Engsig-Karup*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用大语言模型将APL（一种语法简洁、特殊的编程语言）自动翻译为C#的方法，提出了三种引导策略（自然语言描述中介、检索增强和迭代优化），并通过自动评估流水线验证了翻译质量。实验结果表明，该方法能有效弥合APL与C#之间的差距，且引入额外上下文和指导能显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程语言之间的自动翻译仍具挑战性，尤其是当源语言（APL）语法极为简洁且专门化时。APL的稀疏语法、大规模平行语料库的稀缺以及解读APL程序所需的专业知识，使得翻译任务尤为困难。

Method: 提出了一个APL到C#翻译的框架，对比了三种引导策略：自然语言描述中介（通过自然语言描述作为中间表示）、检索增强（利用外部知识库检索相关信息）和迭代优化（逐步改进翻译结果），并与基线直接翻译模型进行比较。构建了多个不同复杂度的功能等价代码对数据集，并开发了自动评估流水线，验证生成C#代码的语法编译和功能执行正确性。

Result: 实验表明，神经代码翻译能够成功弥合APL与C#之间的差距，适用于广泛的程序；引入额外上下文和指导策略能显著提升模型翻译性能。

Conclusion: 基于大语言模型的引导式翻译方法能够有效解决APL到C#的翻译问题，为处理高度专业化语言之间的代码转换提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automatic translation between programming languages remains a challenging problem, particularly when the source language is highly concise and specialized. This paper investigates the translation of APL into C# using large language models. The task is difficult due to APL's sparse syntax, the scarcity of large-scale parallel corpora, and the requirement for specialized knowledge to interpret APL programs. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel framework for APL-to-C# translation by comparing three guided strategies, namely natural language description-mediated, retrieval-augmented, and iterative refinement, against a baseline direct translation model. We constructed multiple datasets of functionally equivalent code pairs spanning various levels of complexity, and to rigorously assess translation quality, we developed an automated evaluation pipeline that verifies both syntactic compilation and functional execution of the generated C# code. Our results demonstrate that neural code translation can successfully bridge the gap between APL and C# for a wide range of programs, and that incorporating additional context and guidance significantly improves model performance.

</details>


### [6] [Bidirectional Empowerment of Metamorphic Testing and Large Language Models: A Systematic Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13898)
*Zheng Zheng,Zenghui Zhou,Yinwang Xu,Daixu Ren,Tsong Yueh Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了93篇论文，提出了元测试（MT）与大语言模型（LLM）双向赋能框架：一方面用MT测试LLM的幻觉、公平性、鲁棒性等问题；另一方面用LLM自动发现元关系、生成测试输入、实现可执行测试和闭环测试，为构建更严谨、可扩展、可信的AI质量保障方法提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的生成性、概率性和开放性给软件质量保障带来巨大挑战，传统测试方法因预言难题而受限。元测试通过检查多轮执行间的必要关系来应对这一问题，但其传统阶段人工密集。同时LLM的语义理解与代码生成能力为自动化MT提供了新可能，因此需要系统梳理二者的双向赋能关系。

Method: 对93篇相关研究进行系统性文献综述，提出双向赋能分类体系：（1）MT for LLMs：验证LLM在幻觉、公平性、鲁棒性、代码可靠性、检索增强生成、对话系统和自主智能体等方面的表现；（2）LLMs for MT：利用LLM自动发现元关系、实现输入变换与合成、生成可执行测试、构建智能体闭环测试。

Result: 建立了MT与LLM双向赋能的结构化分类体系，覆盖了两个方向共多个子领域的研究现状。

Conclusion: MT与LLM之间存在互惠互利的双向赋能关系，未来方向包括构建更严谨、可扩展和可信的AI质量保障方法论。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have introduced substantial challenges to software quality assurance due to their generative, probabilistic, and open-ended nature, which intensifies the oracle problem and limits the applicability of traditional testing methods. Metamorphic testing (MT), which checks necessary relations among multiple related executions rather than relying on exact expected outputs, has emerged as a promising approach for testing LLMs and other oracle-deficient systems. At the same time, the strong semantic understanding, reasoning, and code generation capabilities of LLMs create new opportunities to automate the traditionally labor-intensive phases of MT. This survey systematically reviews 93 primary studies and characterizes this reciprocal relationship as the bidirectional empowerment of MT and LLMs. We propose a taxonomy spanning two complementary directions: MT for LLMs, which uses MT to verify, validate, assess, and understand LLMs and LLM-based systems across issues such as hallucination, fairness, robustness, code reliability, retrieval-augmented generation, dialogue, and autonomous agents; and LLMs for MT, which leverages LLMs to support metamorphic relation discovery, input transformation and synthesis, executable test implementation, and agentic closed-loop testing. By synthesizing these developments, this survey provides a structured foundation for understanding the evolving synergy between MT and LLMs and highlights future directions for building more rigorous, scalable, and trustworthy AI quality assurance methodologies.

</details>


### [7] [A Non-Destructive Methodological Framework for Modernizing Legacy Clinical Reporting Systems for AI-Driven Pharmacoinformatics: A SAS Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13905)
*Jaime Yan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种非破坏性方法论框架，通过元数据层（桥接映射、类型化中间表示IR、编排器）包裹遗留临床报告系统，在不修改原有代码的前提下实现AI就绪，在558个组件的SAS报告库中验证，现代化核心专有代码减少92%，Phase III研究14种报告类型平均单元格一致性达82.7%，CDISC基准测试达100%一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物开发和药物警戒受困于遗留临床报告管线，这些单体系统编码了监管级逻辑但产生不透明输出，缺乏机器可读的中间层，阻碍AI集成。现有现代化方法要么完全重写，要么增量重构但保留结构性障碍。

Method: 提出非破坏性方法论框架，包含三个元数据层组件：桥接映射（bridge map）、类型化中间表示（IR）和编排器（orchestrator）。该层包裹现有组件，将其输出重新暴露为LLM可消费的结构化数据，并支持可选增量整合，用元数据配置的核心例程替换遗留组件。

Result: 在558个组件、37.3万行代码的SAS报告库上验证：共存模式下即时AI就绪，产生机器可读输出；现代化核心专有代码减少92%；Phase III研究14种报告类型中11个达到80%以上单元格一致性（均值82.7%，最佳99.2%）；CDISC CDISCPilot01数据5个报告达100%一致性；LLM实验证实IR支持自动化药物警戒、表格摘要和试验配置生成。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一条符合监管要求的路径，实现AI集成的临床报告，加速药物开发而不中断监管提交流程。

Abstract: Drug development and pharmacovigilance are frequently bottlenecked by legacy clinical reporting pipelines. These monolithic systems encode regulatory-grade logic but resist AI integration by producing opaque output with no machine-readable intermediate layer. Existing modernization approaches force a choice between full rewrites and incremental refactoring that preserves structural barriers. We present a non-destructive methodological framework achieving AI-driven pharmacoinformatics readiness without altering legacy source code. A metadata layer--comprising a bridge map, a typed Intermediate Representation (IR), and an orchestrator--wraps existing components and re-exposes their outputs as structured data consumable by LLMs. It enables optional incremental consolidation, replacing selected legacy components with metadata-configured core routines while the remainder operates unchanged. Validated on a 558-component SAS reporting library (373,000 lines of code), the framework demonstrated immediate AI-readiness under coexistence mode, yielding machine-readable output. Where consolidation was elected, the modernized core achieved a 92% reduction in proprietary code. Parity validation on 14 report types from a Phase III study achieved cell-level parity of 80% or above on 11 reports (mean 82.7%, best 99.2%). A benchmark using CDISC CDISCPilot01 data achieved 100% parity across 5 reports. LLM experiments confirmed the IR enables automated pharmacovigilance, table summarization, and trial configuration generation. The framework offers a regulation-aware path to AI-integrated clinical reporting, accelerating drug development without interrupting regulatory submissions.

</details>


### [8] [CA2: Code-Aware Agent for Automated Game Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13918)
*Valliappan Chidambaram Adaikkappan,Vincent Martineau,Joshua Romoff,David Meger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Code Aware Agent (CA2)，利用调用栈信息增强强化学习游戏测试，相比不利用代码信号的方法取得了更优的覆盖率和测试效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化游戏测试成本高、耗时长，手动测试易遗漏边界情况，现有自动化方法难以实现全覆盖，而先前的强化学习方法未利用调用栈等内部代码信号。

Method: 提出CA2框架，让智能体在游戏状态基础上同时接收当前函数调用轨迹（call stack），学习到达特定目标函数的测试策略，构建了State-based和Image-based两种环境并支持高效调用栈提取。

Result: CA2相比不利用调用栈信息的基线方法取得了一致的性能提升，表明代码信号能增强游戏测试的有效性和针对性。

Conclusion: 融入调用栈等代码信号能够使游戏测试更加高效和有目标性，为自动化游戏测试提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Automated game testing is important for verifying game functionality, but it remains a costly and time-consuming process. Manual testing often misses edge cases, and current automated methods struggle to provide full code coverage. Prior work has explored reinforcement learning (RL) for game testing, but without leveraging internal code signals such as the call stack. We present Code Aware Agent (CA2), which uses call stack information to learn effective testing strategies. The agent receives the current function call trace along with the game state and learns to reach specific target functions. We instrument two types of environments, 1) State-based and 2) Image-based, with support for efficient call stack extraction. Through experimental evaluation, we find that CA2 achieves consistent improvement over the non-code aware baselines, which does not leverage call stack information. Our results show that incorporating code signals like the call stack enables more effective and targeted game testing.

</details>


### [9] [CRANE: Constrained Reasoning Injection for Code Agents via Nullspace Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14084)
*Mingzhi Zhu,Michele Merler,Raju Pavuluri,Stacy Patterson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CRANE是一种无需训练的模型参数编辑方法，通过噪声过滤、保守泰勒门控和渐进S型投影等技术，将Thinking模型的推理优势注入Instruct模型，在代码智能体任务中同时提升推理能力和工具使用纪律性，显著优于任一单独模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码智能体需要兼顾长程推理和工具使用纪律性，而Instruct模型（简洁、工具纪律好）和Thinking模型（强规划与恢复能力，但过度推理降低性能）的能力互补却存在对齐冲突，现有模型无法同时具备两种优势。

Method: CRANE包含三个核心步骤：1) 幅度阈值去噪，从Thinking-Instruct参数差值中筛选候选推理编辑方向；2) 保守泰勒门控，筛选对推理迁移和工具使用保持都有益的编辑；3) 渐进S型投影，抑制破坏工具格式的关键更新方向。

Result: Roo-Eval上Qwen3-30B-A3B pass1达66.2%（+19.5%），Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B达81.5%（+8.7%）；SWE-bench-Verified在两个尺度上分别多解决14个问题（122/500和180/500）；Terminal-Bench v2上pass1/pass5提升最高2.3%/7.8%，三个基准一致优于其他合并策略。

Conclusion: CRANE通过零训练的参数编辑方法成功融合Instruct和Thinking模型优势，兼顾推理增强与工具使用纪律保持，为代码智能体领域提供了一种高效、实用的模型能力整合方案。

Abstract: Code agents must both reason over long-horizon repository state and obey strict tool-use protocols. In paired Instruct/Thinking checkpoints, these capabilities are complementary but misaligned. The Instruct model is concise and tool-disciplined, whereas the Thinking model offers stronger planning and recovery behavior but often over-deliberates and degrades agent performance. We present CRANE (Constrained Reasoning Injection for Code Agents via Nullspace Editing), a training-free parameter-editing method that treats the Thinking-Instruct delta as a directional pool of candidate reasoning edits for the Instruct backbone. CRANE combines magnitude thresholding to denoise the delta, a Conservative Taylor Gate to retain edits that are jointly beneficial for reasoning transfer and tool-use preservation, and Graduated Sigmoidal Projection to suppress format-critical update directions. By merging paired Instruct and Thinking checkpoints, CRANE delivers strong gains over either individual model while preserving Instruct-level efficiency: on Roo-Eval it achieves pass1 of 66.2% (+19.5%) for Qwen3-30B-A3B and 81.5% (+8.7%) for Qwen3-Next-80B-A3B; on SWE-bench-Verified it resolves up to 14 additional instances at both scales (122/500 and 180/500); and on Terminal-Bench v2 it improves pass1/pass5 by up to 2.3%/7.8%, reaching 7.6%/17.9% and 14.8%/30.3%, respectively, consistently outperforming alternative merging strategies across all three benchmarks.

</details>


### [10] [LLM-Based Robustness Testing of Microservice Applications: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14202)
*Hrushitha Goud Tigulla,Marco Vieira*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了使用LLM对微服务API进行鲁棒性测试时，不同模型规模和提示策略对测试多样性与故障覆盖的影响。实验涉及3个开源LLM（14B-70B参数）、7种提示策略和两个架构不同的微服务系统（Java单语言6服务9种故障模式；多语言27服务14种故障模式），共产生38次有效运行和663个测试用例。结果表明：提示策略比模型规模对多样性影响更大；结构化提示完全破坏多样性；单一模型使用三种不同提示策略可在一个系统上实现完全故障覆盖，超越任何固定提示下的多模型集成。提出的GuidedFewShot策略在两个系统上均达到最高单次运行覆盖率（5/9和8/14），同时保持低跨模型相似度。关键教训：仅有分类规则不足，LLM若无具体示例无法区分key-absent与value-empty突变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务API中畸形、缺失或边界值输入可能级联传播威胁系统可靠性，鲁棒性测试通过系统性地注入此类输入来暴露服务端故障，但如何生成多样化且有效的测试用例仍具挑战。LLM可从API规范生成测试，但不同模型和提示策略产生的故障集是多样化还是趋同于相同故障尚不清楚。

Method: 控制实验方法：对3个开源LLM（14B-70B参数）应用7种提示策略，针对两个架构不同的微服务系统（Java单语言6服务9故障模式、多语言27服务14故障模式）进行测试，共38次有效运行产生663个测试用例。引入Guided和GuidedFewShot两种策略，将先前鲁棒性测试研究的突变分类法嵌入为领域上下文。

Result: 提示策略比模型规模对多样性影响更大；结构化提示完全消除多样性；单一模型使用三种提示策略可在一个系统上实现完全故障覆盖，超越任何固定提示下的多模型集成。GuidedFewShot在单系统上达最高单次运行覆盖率（5/9和8/14），同时保持低跨模型相似度。

Conclusion: 仅有分类规则不足以指导LLM生成多样化测试；LLM若无具体示例无法区分key-absent与value-empty突变。研究结果在两个系统上均可复现。

Abstract: Malformed, missing, or boundary-value inputs in microservice APIs can cascade across dependent services, threatening reliability. Robustness testing systematically exercises such inputs to expose server-side failures, but generating diverse, effective tests remains challenging. Large Language Models can generate such tests from API specifications; however, it is unknown whether different models and prompt strategies produce diverse failure sets or converge on the same failures. We report a controlled experiment applying 7 prompt strategies to 3 open-source LLMs (14B-70B parameters) targeting 2 architecturally distinct microservice systems: one Java monolingual (6 services, 9 failure modes) and one polyglot (27 services, 14 failure modes), yielding 38 valid runs and 663 generated tests. We find that prompt strategy explains more variation in diversity than model size: a Structured prompt collapses diversity entirely, while a single model varied across three prompt strategies achieves complete failure-mode coverage on one system, outperforming any multi-model ensemble under a fixed prompt. We introduce two strategies, Guided and GuidedFewShot, that embed a mutation taxonomy from prior robustness testing research as domain context. GuidedFewShot achieves the highest single-run coverage on both systems (5 of 9 and 8 of 14 failure modes) while maintaining low cross-model similarity. A key lesson is that taxonomy rules alone are insufficient: LLMs cannot distinguish key-absent from value-empty mutations without concrete examples. Findings replicate across both systems.

</details>


### [11] [Failure-Guided Fuzzing for Hybrid Quantum-Classical Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14219)
*Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对混合量子-经典（HQC）程序的测试问题，提出了基于失败引导的模糊测试方法，通过先搜索非收敛种子、再对种子附近电路参数进行局部模糊测试的两阶段策略，在VQE和QAOA实例上验证了该方法相比随机测试的有效性，并发现失败引导的局部模糊测试是主要改进来源，而符号化种子发现的收益依赖于具体工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典算法（如VQE和QAOA）在近期的量子计算中至关重要，但测试这些算法非常困难。基于采样的模糊测试在现实执行预算下可能无法覆盖失败易发区域，因此需要更有效的测试策略来发现混合输入（经典优化器超参数和量子电路参数）中的故障或非收敛配置。

Method: 提出失败引导的模糊测试方法：将混合输入建模为经典优化器超参数与量子电路参数对；采用两阶段策略，首先搜索非收敛种子，然后在种子附近对电路参数进行局部模糊测试。比较了五种预算策略：随机混合测试、无模糊的经典枚举、随机种子局部模糊、枚举种子局部模糊和符号化种子局部模糊。在Qiskit平台上对VQE实例和QAOA MaxCut实例进行了实现。

Result: 失败引导的局部模糊测试是相对于随机测试的主要改进来源；符号化种子发现在VQE上提供了额外收益，但在QAOA上不够稳定。

Conclusion: 利用失败信息进行HQC测试是一个有前景的方向，但符号化种子发现的价值依赖于具体的工作负载。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical (HQC) algorithms, such as the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) and the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA), are central to near-term quantum computing but remain challenging to test. Sampling-based fuzzing can expose faulty or non-convergent configurations, but under realistic execution budgets, it may miss failure-prone regions in the joint space of classical optimizer settings and quantum circuit parameters.
  This paper studies failure-guided fuzzing for HQC programs. It models a hybrid input as a pair of classical optimizer hyperparameters and quantum circuit parameters, and evaluates a two-phase strategy that first searches for non-convergent seeds and then locally fuzzes circuit parameters around those seeds. To understand where the gains come from, five budgeted strategies are compared: random hybrid testing, classical enumeration without fuzzing, random-seed local fuzzing, enumeration-seed local fuzzing, and concolic-seed local fuzzing. The study is implemented on a VQE instance and a QAOA MaxCut instance in Qiskit. The results show that failure-guided local fuzzing is the main driver of improvement over random testing, while concolic seed discovery provides additional benefits on VQE but is less stable on QAOA. These findings suggest that reusing failure information is a promising direction for HQC testing, but that the value of concolic seed discovery is workload-dependent.

</details>


### [12] [Making OpenAPI Documentation Agent-Ready: Detecting Documentation and REST Smells with a Multi-Agent LLM System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14312)
*Rayfran Rocha Lima,Davi G. Assunção Pinheiro,Thiago Medeiros de Menezes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了在微服务架构中，将现有REST API通过MCP协议暴露给AI Agent时，OpenAPI文档存在大量缺陷（检测到2450个smells/600个端点），导致Agent系统化失败。为此开发了多Agent LLM系统Hermes进行自动化文档质量检测，并基于证据调整了组织的API治理策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业广泛采用AI Agent和MCP协议，促使组织将现有REST API暴露为Agent可消费的工具，但早期实验发现Agent在任务规划、工具选择和参数构建上存在系统性失败，需要从根本上评估OpenAPI文档的质量问题，而非简单归因于模型能力。

Method: 开发了Hermes系统——一个基于多Agent LLM的框架，在端点级别检测文档相关的REST smells，并生成可解释的诊断报告。对包含约600个端点的16个生产API进行了大规模实证评估。

Result: 在600个端点中识别出2450个smells，所有被分析的操作都存在缺陷。从业者验证确认了检测结果的高度一致性，同时也揭示了修复决策中的上下文权衡。

Conclusion: 微服务环境中的结构有效性并不能保证Agent消费的语义就绪性。基于此证据，组织调整了采纳策略：优先选择性端点适配、重新定义文档标准、将自动化文档评估集成到API治理流程中。系统性的制品级评估可作为战略决策支持机制，降低技术风险，引导工业软件生态中基于证据的AI采纳。

Abstract: The growing adoption of AI agents and the Model Context Protocol (MCP) has motivated organizations to expose existing REST APIs as agent-consumable tools. In our industrial context, this initiative targeted an ecosystem of 16 production APIs comprising approximately 600 endpoints. Although these APIs were stable and widely used within a microservice architecture, early proof-of-concept experiments revealed systematic failures in task planning, tool selection, and payload construction when accessed through MCP-based agents. Rather than attributing these failures to model limitations alone, we conducted an ecosystem-scale empirical assessment of the underlying OpenAPI documentation. We developed Hermes, a multi-agent LLM-based system that detects documentation and REST-related smells at the endpoint level and generates explainable diagnostic reports. The large-scale evaluation identified 2,450 smells across 600 endpoints, with deficiencies present in all analyzed operations. Practitioner validation confirmed high agreement with the detected issues while also revealing contextual trade-offs in remediation decisions. The findings suggested that structural validity within microservice environments does not guarantee semantic readiness for agent-based consumption. Based on this evidence, the organization revised its adoption strategy, prioritizing selective endpoint adaptation, redefining documentation standards, and integrating automated documentation assessment into API governance workflows. This case illustrates how systematic artifact-level evaluation can function as a strategic decision-support mechanism, reducing technological risk and guiding evidence-based AI adoption in industrial software ecosystems.

</details>


### [13] [SWE-Chain: Benchmarking Coding Agents on Chained Release-Level Package Upgrades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14415)
*Man Ho Lam,Chaozheng Wang,Hange Liu,Jingyu Xiao,Haau-sing Li,Jen-tse Huang,Terry Yue Zhuo,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-Chain是一个用于评估大模型驱动的编码智能体在连锁包级别版本升级任务中表现的基准测试，包含12个真实Python包的升级链、155个版本转换和1660个升级需求。实验表明，当前最优智能体（Claude-Opus-4.7）在Build+Fix机制下仅达到60.8%的解决率和68.5%的F1分数，说明现有智能体在连锁包升级中仍难以在保持功能完整性的同时正确完成升级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试大多针对孤立问题修复，无法捕捉软件真实维护中包版本级别连续变更（变更被打包、发布并被后续版本继承）的场景，因此需要一个新的基准来评估智能体在连锁版本升级中的表现。

Method: 设计了一个分治合成流水线（divide-and-conquer synthesis pipeline），将发布说明与每个版本转换的代码差异进行对齐，生成基于实际代码变更、信息丰富且可实现的升级规范。构建了包含12个升级链、9个真实Python包、155个版本转换和1660个升级需求的基准测试SWE-Chain。

Result: 在9种前沿智能体-模型配置下，智能体在Build+Fix机制下平均解决率44.8%、精确率65.4%、F1分数50.2%。Claude-Opus-4.7（Claude Code）表现最佳，解决率60.8%、精确率80.6%、F1分数68.5%。

Conclusion: SWE-Chain具有可行性和区分度，揭示了当前智能体在连锁包级别版本升级中仍存在显著困难，尤其是在不破坏现有功能的前提下完成正确升级。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by large language models are increasingly expected to perform realistic software maintenance tasks beyond isolated issue resolution. Existing benchmarks have shifted toward realistic software evolution, but they rarely capture continuous maintenance at the granularity of package releases, where changes are bundled, shipped, and inherited by subsequent versions. We present SWE-Chain, a benchmark for evaluating agents on chained release-level package upgrades, where each transition builds on the agent's prior codebase. To produce upgrade specifications, we design a divide-and-conquer synthesis pipeline that aligns release notes with code diffs for each version transition, ensuring the requirements are grounded in actual code changes, informative to agents, and feasible to implement. SWE-Chain contains 12 upgrade chains across 9 real Python packages, with 155 version transitions and 1,660 grounded upgrade requirements. Across nine frontier agent-model configurations, agents achieve an average of 44.8% resolving, 65.4% precision, and 50.2% F1 under the Build+Fix regime, with Claude-Opus-4.7 (Claude Code) leading at 60.8% resolving, 80.6% precision, and 68.5% F1. These results show that SWE-Chain is both feasible and discriminative, and reveal that current agents still struggle to make correct upgrades across chained package releases without breaking existing functionality.

</details>


### [14] [FuzzAgent: Multi-Agent System for Evolutionary Library Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14431)
*Yunlong Lyu,Peng Chen,Fengyi Wu,Junzhe Yu,Kit Long Hon,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FuzzAgent是一个多智能体系统，将库模糊测试转变为迭代进化过程，通过专业化智能体团队协作，基于运行时证据逐步优化测试套件，在20个真实C/C++库上超越四种基线方法，覆盖179619个分支，发现102个真实库漏洞（其中78个已被确认修复）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有库模糊测试在规模化采用时成本高昂，环境搭建复杂、测试桩难以满足API约束、无法区分真正的库漏洞与测试桩引发的崩溃。基于LLM的系统通常是一步式代码生成器，忽略运行时反馈，限制了代码覆盖深度和漏洞报告的有效性。

Method: 提出FuzzAgent多智能体系统，将库模糊测试建模为进化过程：多个专业化智能体（如测试桩生成、覆盖分析、崩溃分类等）在整个模糊测试生命周期中协作，每个决策基于具体的运行时证据（覆盖率、崩溃信息等），跨轮次逐步优化测试套件。

Result: 在20个真实C/C++库上无需人工干预完成完整模糊测试生命周期，覆盖179619个分支，比OSS-Fuzz、PromptFuzz、PromeFuzz和OSS-Fuzz-Gen分别高出45.1%、73.2%、92.1%和191.2%。发现102个真实库漏洞，其中78个已被上游维护者确认并修复。

Conclusion: 基于运行时反馈的迭代式多智能体协作方法能显著提升库模糊测试的覆盖深度和漏洞发现能力，验证了将模糊测试视为进化过程而非一次性生成任务的有效性。

Abstract: Library fuzzing is essential for hardening the software supply chain, but adopting it at scale remains expensive. Practitioners still spend substantial effort on environment setup, struggle to generate harnesses that respect intricate API constraints, and lack reliable means to tell genuine library bugs from harness-induced crashes. Recent LLM-based systems automate parts of this pipeline, yet they typically operate as one-shot code generators that ignore runtime feedback, which limits both the depth of code they reach and the validity of the bugs they report. We argue that effective library fuzzing is iterative by nature: each campaign exposes new coverage bottlenecks and crashes, and the next campaign should evolve from these signals rather than restart from scratch. Building on this insight, we present FuzzAgent, a multi-agent system that turns library fuzzing into an evolutionary process, in which a team of specialized agents collaborates over the full fuzzing lifecycle and grounds every decision in concrete runtime evidence, so that the harness suite is successively refined toward deeper coverage and higher-fidelity crash analysis across rounds.
  We evaluate FuzzAgent on 20 real-world C/C++ libraries against four state-of-the-art baselines (OSS-Fuzz, OSS-Fuzz-Gen, PromptFuzz, and PromeFuzz). FuzzAgent completes the full fuzzing lifecycle for all 20 libraries without human intervention and reaches 179619 branches, exceeding OSS-Fuzz, PromptFuzz, PromeFuzz, and OSS-Fuzz-Gen by 45.1%, 73.2%, 92.1%, and 191.2%, respectively. FuzzAgent also identifies 102 genuine library bugs, 78 of which have already been acknowledged and fixed by upstream maintainers.

</details>


### [15] [When Retrieval Hurts Code Completion: A Diagnostic Study of Stale Repository Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14478)
*Haojun Weng,Qianqian Yang,Hao Fu,Haobin Pan,Xinwei Lv*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 检索增强的代码生成中，过时的仓库上下文不仅会移除有用证据，还会主动诱导模型生成与当前状态不兼容的代码，表现为“无害噪声→主动偏置”的质变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究检索增强代码生成中，检索到的跨文件仓库上下文若来自过时的项目状态，是仅作为无害噪声，还是会主动诱导生成与当前状态不兼容的代码。

Method: 在5个Python仓库的17个生产级辅助函数签名变更样本上，通过当前-only、过时-only、无检索、当前/过时混合四种检索条件进行受控诊断实验，提示信息中隐藏提交新鲜度和期望的当前签名。

Result: 在信息中立的提示下，过时-only检索在Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct上诱导15/17样本引用过时辅助函数（较当前-only增加88.2个百分点），在gpt-4.1-mini上为13/17（增加76.5个百分点）。无检索产生零次过时引用但仅1/17通过编译。两模型在过时触发样本上Jaccard重叠度为75.0%，混合条件表明添加有效当前证据能大幅挽救过时-only的失败。

Conclusion: 检索到的仓库上下文的时间有效性是代码检索增强生成（Code RAG）鲁棒性的一个独立诊断变量：过时上下文会主动将模型偏置到过时的仓库状态，而不仅仅是移除有用证据。

Abstract: Context: Retrieval-augmented code generation relies on cross-file repository context, but retrieved snippets may come from obsolete project states.
  Objectives: We study whether temporally stale repository snippets act as harmless noise or actively induce current-state-incompatible code.
  Methods: We conduct a controlled diagnostic study on a curated 17-sample set of production-helper signature changes from five Python repositories. For each sample, we compare current-only, stale-only, no-retrieval, and mixed current/stale retrieval conditions under prompts that hide commit freshness and expected current signatures.
  Results: Under neutralized prompts, stale-only retrieval induces stale helper references on 15/17 Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct samples and 13/17 gpt-4.1-mini samples, corresponding to 88.2 and 76.5 percentage-point increases over current-only retrieval. No retrieval produces zero stale references but only 1/17 passing completions. The two models share 75.0% Jaccard overlap among stale-triggering samples, and mixed conditions show that adding valid current evidence largely rescues stale-only failures.
  Conclusion: Temporal validity of retrieved repository context is a distinct diagnostic variable for Code RAG robustness: stale context can actively bias models toward obsolete repository state rather than merely removing useful evidence.

</details>


### [16] [Viverra: Text-to-Code with Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14972)
*Haoze Wu,Rocky Klopfenstein,Keith Farkas,Nina Narodytska*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Viverra是一个能自动为LLM生成的C代码附带形式化验证断言（assertions）的系统，通过组合有界模型检查器进行验证，在18个任务和400+用户研究中证明其能提升代码理解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-to-Code生成代码缺乏正确性保证，人工审查LLM生成代码耗时且可能抵消AI编程工具带来的效率增益，因此需要一种自动提供正确性保障的方法。

Method: 给定自然语言任务描述，Viverra先提示LLM合成C程序并生成候选断言（表达安全性和正确性属性），然后通过组合式、尽力而为的方式使用有界模型检查器（bounded model checkers）对断言进行验证。

Result: 在18个不同编程任务上，Viverra能高效生成带有已验证断言的代码；在超过400名参与者的用户研究中，这些断言显著提升了用户在代码理解任务上的表现。

Conclusion: 自动为LLM生成代码附加形式化验证断言是可行的，并且能有效帮助开发者理解代码，弥补Text-to-Code在正确性保障方面的根本性缺陷。

Abstract: A fundamental limitation of Text-to-Code is that no guarantee can be obtained about the correctness of the generated code. Therefore, to ensure its correctness, the generated code still has to be reviewed, tested, and maintained by developers. However, parsing through LLM-generated code can be tedious and time-consuming, potentially negating the productivity gains promised by AI-coding tools. To address this challenge, we present Viverra, a system that automatically produces formally verified annotations alongside generated code to aid user's understanding of the generated program. Given a natural-language task description, Viverra prompts an LLM to synthesize a C program together with candidate assertions expressing safety and correctness properties. It then verifies those assertions in a compositional and best-effort manner via a portfolio of bounded model checkers. Evaluation on 18 diverse programming tasks suggests that Viverra can efficiently generate code with verified assertions, and that these assertions improve users' performance on code-comprehension tasks in a user study with more than 400 participants.

</details>


### [17] [Not All RAGs Are Created Equal: A Component-Wise Empirical Study for Software Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14503)
*Qiang Ke,Yanjie Zhao,Hongjin Leng,Shengming Zhao,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对RAG流水线在软件工程任务中的组件进行了系统性实证研究，评估了21种模型和方法，发现检索器侧组件（尤其是检索算法）对系统性能的影响大于生成器模型的选择，且经典词法检索器BM25在多种任务中表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RAG被越来越多地用于将LLM与软件制品结合，但其组件的最佳配置在软件工程任务中仍无系统性指导，导致实践者依赖昂贵且随机的实验。

Method: 采用组件级实证研究方法，系统隔离并评估了RAG流水线中的4种查询处理技术、7种检索模型（稀疏、稠密和混合范式）、4种上下文精炼方法和6种生成器，在代码生成、摘要和修复3个核心SE任务上进行测试。

Result: 检索器侧组件（特别是检索算法的选择）对最终系统性能的影响通常大于生成器模型的选择；经典词法检索器BM25在多种任务中展现出异常稳健的性能。

Conclusion: 为在软件工程上下文中构建有效的RAG系统提供了基于数据驱动的实用路线图和优化优先级指导。

Abstract: While Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is increasingly adopted to ground Large Language Models (LLMs) in software artifacts, the optimal configuration of its components remains an open question for software engineering (SE) tasks. The lack of systematic guidance forces practitioners into costly, ad-hoc experimentation. This paper presents a comprehensive, component-wise empirical study that dissects the RAG pipeline, evaluating over 21 distinct models and methods. Our study systematically isolates and evaluates 4 query processing techniques, 7 retrieval models spanning sparse, dense, and hybrid paradigms, 4 context refinement methods, and 6 distinct generators. We test these components on a suite of 3 core SE tasks: code generation, summarization, and repair. Our empirical findings reveal a crucial insight: the retriever-side components, particularly the choice of the retrieval algorithm, often exert a more significant influence on final system performance than the selection of the generator model. Strikingly, the classic lexical retriever BM25 demonstrates exceptionally robust performance across diverse tasks. Our analysis provides a practical, data-driven roadmap for researchers and practitioners, offering clear guidance on prioritizing optimization efforts when constructing effective RAG systems for software engineering contexts.

</details>


### [18] [Mining Subscenario Refactoring Opportunities in Behaviour-Driven Software Test Suites: ML Classifiers and LLM-Judge Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14568)
*Ali Hassaan Mughal,Noor Fatima,Muhammad Bilal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一套自动化框架，用于在BDD（行为驱动开发）测试套件中发现并分类可重构的重复步骤子序列（slices），利用 paraphrase-robust 聚类、XGBoost 分类器将重复模式映射到三种已知重构模式，并在 339 个仓库的大规模语料上验证。分类器 F1=0.891，优于规则基线（0.836）和 LLM 评判（0.728）。结果显示 75.0%、59.5%、11.7% 的场景分别包含 within-file Background、within-repo reusable-scenario 和 cross-organisational shared-step 候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BDD 测试套件中积累了重复的步骤子序列，已有三种重构模式但缺乏自动化方法来判断哪些重复子序列值得提取以及应用哪种机制。

Method: 使用滑动窗口（L∈[2,18]）从 339 个仓库的 Gherkin 语料中提取连续步骤子序列；利用 SBERT + UMAP + HDBSCAN 进行 paraphrase-robust 聚类；三位作者依据书面评分标准对分层抽样的 200 个片段进行标注；使用 XGBoost 在 5 折交叉验证下训练提取价值分类器，并与规则基线和两个开源 LLM 评判进行对比。

Result: 挖掘出 5,382,249 个切片，归并为 692,020 个重复模式。三位作者的 Fleiss' κ 在 extraction-worthy 上为 0.56，在 mechanism 上为 0.79。分类器 out-of-fold F1=0.891（95% CI [0.852, 0.927]），优于规则基线（F1=0.836, p=0.017）和最佳 LLM 评判（F1=0.728, p<1e-4）。75.0%、59.5%、11.7% 的场景分别包含三种重构模式候选。

Conclusion: Paraphrase-robust 子场景发现方法能在大规模语料上生成 BDD 重构机会的全景普查；管道、分类器预测、标注池和评分标准均已以 Apache-2.0 许可开源。

Abstract: Context. Behaviour-Driven Development (BDD) software test suites accumulate duplicated step subsequences. Three published refactoring patterns are available (within-file Background, within-repo reusable-scenario invocation, cross-organisational shared higher-level step), but no prior work automates which recurring subsequences are worth extracting or which mechanism applies. Objective. Rank recurring step subsequences ("slices") by refactoring suitability (extraction-worthy), pre-map each to one of the three patterns, and quantify prevalence across the public BDD ecosystem. Method. Every contiguous L-step window (L in [2, 18]) in a 339-repository / 276-upstream-owner Gherkin corpus is keyed by paraphrase-robust cluster identifiers and counted under three scopes. Sentence-BERT (SBERT) / Uniform Manifold Approximation and Projection (UMAP) / Hierarchical Density-Based Clustering (HDBSCAN) recovers paraphrase-equivalent slices. Three authors label a stratified 200-slice pool against a written rubric. An eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) extraction-worthy classifier trained under 5-fold cross-validation is compared with a tuned rule baseline and two open-weight Large Language Model (LLM) judges. Results. The miner produces 5,382,249 slices collapsing to 692,020 recurring patterns. Three-author Fleiss' kappa = 0.56 (extraction-worthy) and 0.79 (mechanism). The classifier reaches out-of-fold F1 = 0.891 (95% CI [0.852, 0.927]), outperforming both the rule baseline (F1 = 0.836, p = 0.017) and the better LLM judge (F1 = 0.728, p < 1e-4). 75.0%, 59.5%, and 11.7% of scenarios carry a within-file Background, within-repo reusable-scenario, or cross-organisational shared-step candidate. Conclusion. Paraphrase-robust subscenario discovery yields a corpus-wide census of BDD refactoring opportunities; pipeline, classifier predictions, labelled pool, and rubric are released under Apache-2.0.

</details>


### [19] [In-IDE Toolkit for Developers of AI-Based Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14612)
*Yaroslav Sokolov,Yury Khudyakov,Lenar Sharipov,Andrei Gasparian,Parth Tiwary,Artem Trofimov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍JetBrains IDE的AI Toolkit插件，通过IDE原生的追踪与评估功能，帮助非ML背景的软件工程师在Run/Debug循环中测试、调试和复现LLM与agentic工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM和agentic workflows的AI功能难以测试、调试和复现，尤其对没有机器学习背景的产品软件工程师而言挑战巨大。

Method: 通过混合方法研究识别三个核心需求（可重复评估、执行时追踪、最小化配置与上下文切换），设计并实现了IDE原生工作流：运行时触发追踪捕获、即时层次化检查、一键从追踪添加数据集、基于单元测试的可插拔评估。

Result: PyCharm首发版本显示积极信号：Run时推广转化率高、追踪用户持续使用、流失率低，表明IDE原生可观测性降低了激活门槛。

Conclusion: 将AI可观测性与评估集成到日常IDE工作流中，能使非ML专家也能访问现代AI开发，同时保留软件工程实践。

Abstract: AI-enabled features built on LLMs and agentic workflows are difficult to test, debug, and reproduce, especially for product-focused software engineers without a machine learning background. We present the AI Toolkit plugin for JetBrains IDEs, which brings tracing and evaluation directly into the Run/Debug loop. A mixed methods study with practitioners presents three consistent needs: (1) make evaluation regular and repeatable, (2) expose traces at the moment of execution, and (3) minimize setup and context switching. Guided by these needs, the AI Toolkit introduces an IDE-native workflow: run-triggered trace capture; immediate, hierarchical inspection; one-click "Add to Dataset" from traces; and unit-test-like evaluations with pluggable metrics. The first release in PyCharm shows promising early signals - strong conversion when promoted at Run, sustained usage among those who capture traces, and low churn - suggesting that IDE-native observability lowers activation energy and helps developers adopt disciplined practices. We detail the design and implementation of the AI Agents Debugger and AI Evaluation, report initial adoption telemetry, and outline next steps to broaden framework coverage and scale evaluations. Together, these results indicate that integrating AI observability and evaluation into everyday IDE workflows can make modern AI development accessible to non-ML specialists while preserving software-engineering practices.

</details>


### [20] [Agentic AI in Industry: Adoption Level and Deployment Barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14675)
*Spyridon Alvanakis Apostolou,Jan Bosch,Helena Holmström Olsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对12家公司16位从业者的访谈研究，发现工业界AI智能体采纳存在一个"能力-部署验证鸿沟"：四家公司具备高级实验性AI能力但无法集成到生产流程，主要受限于LLM上下文窗口、专有语言性能不足、非确定性不符合资格标准以及数据保密性四大障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管智能体AI系统正进入软件工程工作流，但关于工业组织如何实际采纳这些系统的经验证据仍然匮乏，亟需实证研究填补这一空白。

Method: 对12家不同规模和领域公司的16位从业者进行定性访谈研究，采用改编自成熟AI驱动组织的六层成熟度框架来表征当前智能体AI采纳状态。

Result: 7家公司处于L1（AI助手），4家处于L2（AI补偿器），仅1家达到L3（多智能体编排）。四家公司虽有高级实验性AI能力但缺乏输出验证机制无法投入生产，形成了能力-部署验证鸿沟，该鸿沟由上下文窗口限制、专有语言表现欠佳、非确定性与资格标准冲突、数据保密性四个障碍塑造，并呈现出信息不对称和资格缺失两个相互依存的维度。

Conclusion: 工业级智能体集成的核心开放问题是能力-部署验证鸿沟，其根源在于信息不对称与资格缺失两个相互依存的维度，需要建立可信的输出验证机制来弥合实验能力与生产部署之间的差距。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are entering software engineering workflows, yet empirical evidence on how industrial organizations actually adopt them remains sparse. We present a qualitative interview study with sixteen practitioners across twelve companies of varying size and domain. This study characterizes the current agentic AI adoption state of these companies, employing a six-level maturity framework adapted from established AI-driven organizations. The findings reveal that seven companies operate at Level~1 (AI Assistants), four companies at Level~2 (AI Compensators), and only one in Level~3 (Multi-Agent Orchestration), with large and safety-regulated organizations among the most advanced adopters. The primary finding is a capability-deployment verification gap, four companies demonstrated higher-level experimental AI capabilities but cannot integrate them into production workflows because adequate output verification mechanisms are absent, leaving human-in-the-loop as the only trusted verification mechanism. This gap is shaped by four recurring barriers: context window of LLMs constraints especially when diverse knowledge aggregation is needed, under-performance on proprietary programming languages and protocols, non-determinism incompatible with qualification standards, and data confidentiality concerns. Two interdependent dimensions of this gap emerge from these findings (information asymmetry and qualification absence) framing a core open problem for industrial agentic integration.

</details>


### [21] [Towards In-Depth Root Cause Localization for Microservices with Multi-Agent Recursion-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14866)
*Lingzhe Zhang,Tong Jia,Kangjin Wang,Chiming Duan,Minghua He,Rongqian Wang,Xi Peng,Meiling Wang,Gong Zhang,Renhai Chen,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RCLAgent，一种面向微服务系统的多智能体递归思维与并行推理根因定位框架，通过沿追踪图分解诊断过程、为每个span分配专用智能体并按图拓扑递归并行组织，显著提升定位精度与推理效率，在多个公开基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代微服务系统因动态交互和演化运行环境导致故障频发，现有根因定位方法中，传统机器学习和深度学习可解释性差且跨部署迁移能力弱；基于大语言模型的方法则存在上下文爆炸（稀释关键证据、降低定位精度）和串行推理结构（阻碍深度因果探索、降低推理效率）两大根本局限。

Method: RCLAgent框架包含以下核心设计：(1) 沿追踪图将诊断过程分解，为每个span分配专用智能体（Dedicated Agent）；(2) 根据图拓扑结构以递归和并行方式组织智能体；(3) 通过综合根级诊断报告（Root-Level Diagnosis Report）和全局证据图（Global Evidence Graph）获得最终诊断结果。

Result: 在多个公开基准上的大量实验表明，RCLAgent在定位精度和推理效率上均持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: RCLAgent通过多智能体递归思维与并行推理机制，有效解决了现有LLM方法在微服务根因定位中的上下文爆炸和串行推理瓶颈问题，为复杂系统可靠性保障提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: As modern microservice systems grow increasingly complex due to dynamic interactions and evolving runtime environments, they experience failures with rising frequency. Ensuring system reliability therefore critically depends on accurate root cause localization (RCL). While numerous traditional machine learning and deep learning approaches have been explored for this task, they often suffer from limited interpretability and poor transferability across deployments. More recently, large language model (LLM)-based methods have been proposed to address these issues. However, existing LLM-based approaches still face two fundamental limitations: context explosion, which dilutes critical evidence and degrades localization accuracy, and serial reasoning structures, which hinder deep causal exploration and impair inference efficiency. In this paper, we conduct a comprehensive study of both how human SREs perform root cause localization in practice and why existing LLM-based methods fall short. Motivated by these findings, we introduce RCLAgent, an in-depth root cause localization framework for microservice systems that realizes multi-agent recursion-of-thought with parallel reasoning. RCLAgent decomposes the diagnostic process along the trace graph by assigning each span to a Dedicated Agent and organizing agents recursively and in parallel according to the graph topology, with the final diagnosis obtained by synthesizing the Root-Level Diagnosis Report and the Global Evidence Graph. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks demonstrate that RCLAgent consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both localization accuracy and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [22] [Veritas: A Semantically Grounded Agentic Framework for Memory Corruption Vulnerability Detection in Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15097)
*Xinran Zheng,Alfredo Pesoli,Marco Valleri,Suman Jana,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Veritas是一个语义驱动的二进制内存损坏漏洞检测框架，结合静态切片、双视图LLM检测器和多智能体验证器，在真实二进制漏洞基准上达到90%召回率，零误报（经穷举验证），并发现了一个此前未知的Apple CVE漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在剥离二进制文件中检测内存损坏漏洞面临三大挑战：恢复对象语义、过程间传播以及从低级有损表示中提取可行触发条件。现有方法（包括纯LLM方法）缺乏对内存相关语义和运行时可行性证据的可靠支撑。

Method: Veritas由三部分组成：(1) 基于RetDec提升的LLVM IR的静态切片器，重建值流关系并生成紧凑的见证流对象；(2) 双视图LLM检测器，结合反编译C代码和选择性LLVM IR进行逐步推理；(3) 多智能体验证器，通过引导式调试、断点检查和内存检测预言机来确认或拒绝候选漏洞。

Result: 在真实二进制漏洞基准上达到90%召回率。穷举验证623个检测候选无假阳性，额外审计中发现2个确认的假阳性。在实际应用中发现了此前未知的Apple漏洞并获得CVE编号。

Conclusion: 语义化基础（semantic grounding）可作为实用化二进制漏洞检测的操作性设计原则，能有效提升检测的准确性和可信度。

Abstract: Detecting memory corruption vulnerabilities in stripped binaries requires recovering object semantics, interprocedural propagation, and feasible triggers from low-level, lossy representations. Recent LLM-based approaches improve code understanding, but reliable detection still requires grounding in memory-relevant semantics and runtime feasibility evidence. We present Veritas, a semantically grounded framework for binary memory corruption vulnerability detection. Veritas combines a static slicer over RetDec-lifted LLVM IR, a dual-view LLM detector that reasons step by step over grounded flows using decompiled C and selective LLVM IR, and a multi-agent validator that checks hypotheses against debugger-visible artifacts and runtime evidence. The slicer reconstructs value-flow relations from LLVM-IR facts, including def-use, calls, returns, globals, and pointer operations, and emits compact witness-backed flow objects. The detector uses these artifacts to reason about control flow, bounds, and object correspondence without rediscovering whole-binary propagation. The validator confirms or rejects candidates through guided debugging, breakpoint inspection, and memory-checking oracles. We implement Veritas as a modular pipeline and evaluate it on a curated benchmark of real-world binary vulnerability cases. Veritas achieves 90\% recall. For false-positive assessment, we exhaustively validate and manually verify 623 detector candidates and audit additional candidates from larger cases. The exhaustive subset produces no false positives, while the additional audit identifies two confirmed false positives. In a real-world application, Veritas discovered a previously unknown Apple vulnerability that was confirmed and assigned a CVE. These results support semantic grounding as an operational design principle for practical binary vulnerability detection.

</details>

### [23] [A foundational characterization of Hoare Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13944)
*Daniel Leivant*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文证明，关于迭代程序的部分正确性断言在Hoare逻辑中可证，当且仅当它在标准二阶逻辑（限定于一阶谓词的理解）中可证。这一等价关系曾两次被声称但均有错误证明，且似乎是Hoare逻辑的首个基础性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Hoare逻辑提供严格的基础性刻画，纠正此前两次有缺陷的证明，建立Hoare逻辑与标准二阶逻辑之间的精确等价关系。

Method: 通过形式化证明，建立Hoare逻辑中部分正确性断言的可证性与标准二阶逻辑（理解公理限定于一阶谓词）中可证性之间的等价关系。

Result: 证明了Hoare逻辑中关于迭代程序的部分正确性断言可证，当且仅当其在限定于一阶谓词理解的二阶逻辑中可证。

Conclusion: 该等价关系是Hoare逻辑首个基础性刻画，为理解Hoare逻辑的证明能力提供了精确的逻辑基础。

Abstract: We show that a partial-correctness assertion about an iterative program is provable in Hoare Logic iffit is provable in standard second-order logic with comprehension restricted to first-order predicates. This equivalence was claimed twice in the past, both with faulty proofs, and seems to be the first foundational characterization of Hoare Logic.

</details>


### [24] [Proof Nets for PiL (Full Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14476)
*Matteo Acclavio,Giulia Manara*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文为PiL（π-演算线性逻辑扩展）设计了一种证明网，提供了正确性判据、串行化程序和证明翻译算法，并证明该证明网能规范表示规则置换下的相继式演算推演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在逻辑系统中更自然地编码π-演算中的进程，需要扩展线性逻辑并为其提供一种不依赖于规则置换的规范表示形式，从而为进程演算提供更简洁的语法分析工具。

Method: 在一阶乘法加法线性逻辑基础上引入新算子得到PiL，为该逻辑设计证明网，并发展出正确性判据、串行化过程和证明翻译算法。

Result: 建立了PiL的证明网理论，包含正确性判据、串行化程序和证明翻译算法，并证明了证明网能够规范表示相继式演算推演（模规则置换）。

Conclusion: 该证明网为PiL提供了不依赖于语法细节的规范表示，有助于连接线性逻辑与进程演算，为π-演算的进程行为分析提供了逻辑基础。

Abstract: We introduce proof nets for PiL, an extension of first-order multiplicative additive linear logic with new operators allowing a shallow encoding of processes in the π-calculus as formulas. We provide correctness criterion, sequentialization procedure, and a proof translation algorithm. We show that proof nets provide a canonical representation of sequent calculus derivations modulo rule permutations.

</details>

Method: 在合理的拓扑假设下，将参数化族的安全验证问题简化为一组局部证明的组合式方案，并提出相应的验证算法并实现为工具。

Result: 通过多个不同拓扑上的基准测试，证明了所提方法在证明参数化程序安全性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的组合式验证方法能够自动生成全称量化的归纳不变量，有效解决无限状态参数化并发程序的安全验证问题。

Abstract: This paper investigates the algorithmic safety verification problem of infinite-state parameterized concurrent programs over a rich set of communication topologies. The goal is to automatically produce a proof of correctness in the form of a universally quantified inductive invariant, where the quantification is over the nodes in the topology. We illustrate that under reasonable assumptions on the underlying topology, the problem can be reduced to and solved as a compositional scheme, that is, the verification of the parameterized family is reduced to a set of local proofs, in a complete manner. We propose a verification algorithm, which is implemented as a tool, and demonstrate through a set of benchmarks over several different topologies that our approach is effective in proving parameterized programs safe.

</details>


### [26] [Refactoring-as-Propositions: Proved Refactoring of Hybrid Systems via Proved Refinements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15001)
*Enguerrand Prebet,André Platzer*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出"将重构视为命题"原则，利用微分精化逻辑(dRL)证明混合系统重构后仍保留所需安全属性，将全局验证简化为局部变更的模块化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息物理系统因软件与物理世界连接而具有固有复杂性，迭代设计虽降低了复杂度，但每次变更后都需要重新全面检查系统安全性，这一过程耗时且容易出错。现有方法缺乏一种能够同时严谨地引用系统属性以及重构系统与原系统之间关系的统一框架。

Method: 提出"重构即命题"(refactoring-as-propositions)原则，将重构表示为命题形式；基于微分精化逻辑(dRL)，同时严谨地指称系统属性和重构版本与原版本之间的关系；利用精化(refinement)统一表达不同类型的混合系统重构（包括引入辅助变量的重构）；通过沿相应修改传递证明来保留所需属性。

Result: 展示了这些重构可以自动证明，并且/或者简化为仅关注局部变更的模块化证明，而非整个系统的全局证明。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了信息物理系统迭代设计中安全属性验证的效率，将全局验证任务分解为局部模块化证明，降低了验证复杂度。

Abstract: Cyber-physical systems are inherently complex due to their connection between software and the physical world. Iterative design reduces their complexity, but increases the need to repeatedly recheck their safety in full after every change. We introduce the refactoring-as-propositions principle in which refactorings are represented as propositions along with a method for proving that system refactorings preserve their required properties by transferring the proof along the respective modification. It is based on differential refinement logic (dRL), with which one can simultaneously and rigorously refer to properties of the systems and the relation between a refactored system and its original version. Refinements represent a uniform way of expressing different types of hybrid system refactorings, including those that introduce auxiliary variables. Furthermore, we show how these refactorings can be proved automatically, and/or reduce to a modular proof solely about the local change rather than about the whole system.

</details>


### [27] [Extending CDCL to disjunctions of parity equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15002)
*Paul Beame,Glenn Sun*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了CDCL($\oplus$)，一种将CDCL扩展到XNF公式的泛化框架，证明了其与Res($\oplus$)证明系统的双向多项式模拟关系，并通过实现原型求解器Xorcle展示了其在原生XNF公式和Tseitin公式上的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDCL仅产生Resolution证明，而某些问题（如包含奇偶约束的问题）在Resolution下是指数困难的，但在更强的证明系统Res($\oplus$)下可以有指数级更短的证明。现有求解器（如CryptoMiniSAT）虽然能处理单独的奇偶方程，但对更一般的线性子句（XNF公式）的推理尚不成熟，缺乏统一的CDCL框架。

Method: 1) 将CDCL泛化到XNF公式，支持在任意奇偶方程上分支、线性代数单元传播和通过冲突分析学习线性子句。2) 为Res($\oplus$)设计了一组新的推理规则，用于翻译基于Resolution的子程序（如1-UIP子句学习）。3) 证明了CDCL($\oplus$)与Res($\oplus$)的双向联系：CDCL($\oplus$)既产生Res($\oplus$)证明，又在非确定性决策和重启下多项式模拟Res($\oplus$)。4) 实现了原型求解器Xorcle，包含适配XNF公式的CDCL启发式以及扩展的LRUP($\oplus$)证明记录系统。

Result: 在原生XNF公式的基准测试中，Xorcle优于Kissat和CryptoMiniSAT等现有求解器。在CNF编码的Tseitin公式上，即使没有预处理，Xorcle的运行时间几乎呈多项式规模扩展。

Conclusion: CDCL($\oplus$)成功地将CDCL的推理能力从Resolution扩展到Res($\oplus$)，弥合了SAT求解与更强证明系统之间的鸿沟，为处理包含奇偶约束的复杂问题提供了统一的理论框架和实用求解器。

Abstract: Because CDCL produces proofs in the Resolution proof system, problems provably hard for Resolution are also provably hard for CDCL. Exponentially shorter proofs can sometimes be found using stronger proof systems such as $\text{Res}(\oplus)$, a generalization of Resolution to XNF formulas, whose constraints are disjunctions of parity equations ("linear clauses") such as $(x \oplus y) \lor \lnot (y \oplus z)$. While some modern solvers like CryptoMiniSAT reason on Boolean clauses with separate parity equations, reasoning about more general linear clauses is less explored.
  We present $\text{CDCL}(\oplus)$, a generalization of CDCL to XNF formulas, and prove a bidirectional connection with $\text{Res}(\oplus)$: $\text{CDCL}(\oplus)$ not only produces $\text{Res}(\oplus)$ proofs, but also polynomially simulates $\text{Res}(\oplus)$ given nondeterministic decisions and restarts, mirroring the classical relationship between CDCL and Resolution. Our key technical tool is a new set of inference rules for $\text{Res}(\oplus)$ that helps us translate Resolution-based subroutines such as 1-UIP clause learning. Altogether, $\text{CDCL}(\oplus)$'s parity reasoning includes branching on arbitrary parity equations, linear-algebraic reasoning during unit propagation, and learning linear clauses through conflict analysis.
  We provide a proof-of-concept implementation of $\text{CDCL}(\oplus)$ called Xorcle, which includes adaptations of existing CDCL heuristics to XNF formulas and an extension of LRUP proof logging that we call $\text{LRUP}(\oplus)$. On a selected suite of benchmarks focusing on native XNF formulas, Xorcle outperforms existing solvers such as Kissat and CryptoMiniSAT. Additionally, on Tseitin formulas written in CNF, even without preprocessing, Xorcle's running time appears to scale nearly polynomially.

</details>


### [28] [The Guarded Fragment with Nested Equivalences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15072)
*Oskar Fiuk*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究了带嵌套等价关系的守卫片段（GF），证明了无等词情况下有限模型性质和可判定性，给出了TOWER-完全和(K+2)-ExpTime-完全复杂度界，并指出破坏嵌套条件或引入等词会导致不可判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 守卫片段（GF）是已建立的可判定一阶逻辑片段，但带等价关系的扩展尚未被充分研究。本文旨在探索嵌套等价关系（即一族逐层加粗的等价关系）对GF可判定性和复杂度的影响。

Method: 通过定义带嵌套等价关系的GF语法，利用模型论和复杂性分析方法，证明无等词情况下的有限模型性质（FMP）和可判定性；并通过归约论证刻画复杂度下界（TOWER-完全和(K+2)-ExpTime-完全），通过反例构造证明不可判定性结果。

Result: 1) 无等词的GF带嵌套等价关系具有FMP且可判定；2) 一般情形TOWER-完全，固定K个关系时(K+2)-ExpTime-完全；3) 违反嵌套条件（即使仅两个等价关系）或引入等词（即使仅一个等价关系）会导致不可判定。

Conclusion: 嵌套等价关系下的GF在无等词时具备良好的可判定性和紧致复杂度界，但该扩展对嵌套条件和等词高度敏感，二者缺一不可，否则将跃至不可判定。

Abstract: The Guarded Fragment (GF) is a well-established decidable fragment of first-order logic. We study an extension of GF with nested equivalence relations, namely a family of distinguished binary predicates $E_1, E_2, \dots$ interpreted as equivalence relations such that $E_{k+1}$ is coarser than $E_k$ for every $k$. We show that the equality-free GF with nested equivalence relations enjoys the finite model property and has a decidable satisfiability problem. Moreover, we establish tight complexity bounds for satisfiability: TOWER-completeness in general, and $(K{+}2)$-ExpTime-completeness when the number of distinguished predicates is fixed to $K$. Finally, we show that satisfiability becomes undecidable if either the nesting condition is dropped (already with two equivalence relations) or equality is admitted (already with a single equivalence relation).

</details>


### [29] [Eliminating reversals from cubical type theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15080)
*Evan Cavallo,Christian Sattler*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文证明，在自对偶区间理论的立方类型论中，添加反转算子（将区间端点互换的对合算子）是保守扩张，并利用“扭转构造”首次给出具有反转算子的理论在无反转的立方集范畴中的模型，其同伦理论对应拓扑空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立方类型论基于抽象单位区间定义路径类型以表示相等关系，区间上不同操作对应不同类型论。反转算子是交换区间端点的对合算子。研究添加反转算子是否保守扩张，以及能否在无反转的立方集范畴中构造模型，对于理解立方类型论的理论性质及其与拓扑空间同伦理论的对应关系具有重要意义。

Method: 核心工具是“扭转构造”：将一个区间与其对偶区间的乘积构造为新的区间，其中反转算子由坐标交换实现。利用该构造证明对自对偶区间理论（如最小两端点理论或有界分配格理论）添加反转算子是保守扩张。同时，在无反转的立方集范畴中利用相同构造建立带反转算子的严格立方类型论的模型。

Result: 证明了在自对偶区间理论（最小两端点理论、有界分配格理论）的立方类型论中，添加反转算子是保守扩张。首次在无反转的立方集范畴中构造了带反转算子的立方类型论模型，该模型的同伦理论对应拓扑空间的同伦理论。

Conclusion: 反转算子可保守地添加到自对偶区间理论的立方类型论中，且可通过扭转构造在无反转的立方集范畴中实现模型，从而建立了带反转算子的立方类型论与拓扑空间同伦理论之间的对应关系。

Abstract: Cubical type theories are designed around an abstract unit interval from which types of paths, used to represent equalities, are defined. Varying the operations available on this interval yields different type theories. A reversal is an involutive operator on the interval that swaps its two endpoints. We show that for cubical type theories with self-dual interval theories, such as the minimal theory of two endpoints or the theory of a bounded distributive lattice, the extension of the theory with a reversal that internalizes the duality is a conservative extension. The key tool is a "twist construction": the product of an interval and its dual is again an interval with a reversal given by swapping coordinates.
  Our conservativity result applies to "opaque" cubical type theories, without strict equations reducing the filling operator at concrete type formers or eliminators from higher inductive types at path constructors. Using the same twist construction, we also construct models of strict cubical type theory with reversals in categories of cubical sets without reversals. We thereby give the first model of a theory with reversals whose homotopy theory corresponds to that of topological spaces.

</details>


### [30] [Loop Termination and Generalized Collatz Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15094)
*Mishel Carelli*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究线性约束循环的终止性问题，发现单变量整数循环与广义Collatz序列紧密相关。在特定猜想成立的前提下，单变量循环的终止性可在多项式时间内判定；反之，任何判定程序可解决该猜想的未解决问题。此外，单变量循环若有循环迹，则长度至多为2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性约束循环是程序分析中的基本问题，其终止性判定在整数、有理数和实数域上的可判定性至今未解决，作者希望探索整数域上该问题的可判定性边界。

Method: 将单变量线性约束整数循环的终止性问题映射到广义Collatz序列的终止性问题上，利用Collatz猜想相关理论进行分析。

Result: 1) 在广义Collatz猜想成立的前提下，单变量线性约束循环的终止性可在多项式时间内判定；2) 任何该类判定程序可反过来解决广义Collatz猜想的特定未解决问题；3) 单变量循环若存在循环迹，则循环迹长度不超过2。

Conclusion: 单变量线性约束整数循环的终止性问题与广义Collatz猜想之间存在紧密的双向联系，该问题的完全解决依赖于一个长期未解决的数论猜想。

Abstract: Linear-constraint loops are programs whose transition relation is specified by a system of linear inequalities. The termination problem asks, given a loop, whether it admits an infinite computation. Decidability of termination remains open for linear-constraint loops over integers, rationals, and reals. We focus on loops over integers and show that they are tightly connected to generalized Collatz sequences - integer sequences generated by maps that are linear on each residue class modulo a fixed natural number. We prove that termination of one-variable linear-constraint loops is decidable in polynomial time, provided a long-standing conjecture about generalized Collatz sequences holds. Conversely, we show that any decision procedure for one-variable loops would prove or refute specific instances of this conjecture, which remain open. Moreover, we show that if a one-variable loop has a cyclic trace, then it also has a cyclic trace of length at most two.

</details>

Method: 本文在构造性元理论中建立了类型理论的高层（higher sheaf）模型框架，通过构造性的方式构建了上述合成数学系统的具体模型。

Result: 成功构造了单纯同伦类型理论、合成代数几何和合成Stone对偶性等系统的构造性模型。

Conclusion: 本文为合成数学提供了构造性的模型理论基础，使得这些形式系统在构造性元理论中得以确立。

Abstract: There have recently been several developments in synthetic mathematics using extensions of dependent type theory with univalence and higher inductive types: simplicial homotopy type theory, synthetic algebraic geometry and synthetic Stone duality. We provide a foundation of higher sheaf models of type theory in a constructive metatheory and, in particular, build constructive models of these formal systems.

</details>


### [32] [Guises and Perspectives: An Intentional and Hyperintensional Sketch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15144)
*Juan J. Colomina-Alminana*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文基于Castañeda的内部主义观点，发展了一种以" guise "（属性束+意向）为基本语义对象的意向性逻辑，包含莱布尼茨内涵语义、意向算子和模态层，建立了元理论结果，并与多种经典语义理论进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Castañeda继承莱布尼茨的内部主义关系观，认为关系不是外部因果联系而是意向内部结构。现有形式框架（Montague、Bealer、Fine等）未能充分捕捉这种基于" guise "的意向性视角，因此需要一种新的形式逻辑来严格化这一哲学立场。

Method: 本文采用形式逻辑方法：构建了包含语法、模型论和证明论的意向性逻辑系统。系统整合了三个核心组件：(i) 莱布尼茨式包含语义处理单称真值；(ii) 意向算子捕获 guise 之间的内部关系；(iii) 模态层处理可能性和必然性（最大一致闭包）。

Result: 建立了核心元定理（可靠性和典范模型完全性证明概要），分析了超内涵现象（意向语境中的替换失败、准索引性、de se指称）。与Montague内涵语义、Bealer属性理论、Fine超内涵逻辑、Barwise & Perry情景语义学以及莱布尼茨概念演算纲领进行了系统比较。

Conclusion: 该形式框架自洽地证明了：关系不是外部因果联系，而是通过 agents 和 objects 被构想所依赖的 guise 编码的意向内部结构——即关系即视角。

Abstract: This paper develops a formal logic for guises based on the work of Héctor-Neri Castañeda, who understood relations from an internalist viewpoint, following Leibniz. We introduce a syntax, model theory, and proof theory for an intensional logic in which guises (taken as bundles of properties equipped with intention) serve as primary semantic objects. The system integrates (i) a Leibnizian containment semantics for singular truths, (ii) an intentional operator that captures internal relations among guises, and (iii) a modal layer for possibility and necessity modeled as maximally consistent closures. We establish core metatheoretic results (e.i. soundness and canonical-model completeness sketches) and analyze hyperintensional phenomena such as substitution failure in intentional contexts, quasi-indexicality, and de se reference. We compare the framework to classical intensional semantics (Montague), property theory (Bealer), hyperintensional logics (Fine), situation semantics (Barwise and Perry), and to the Leibniz program for a calculus of concepts. The result is a selfcontained formal framework that demonstrates that relations are not external causal links but intentional internal structures encoded in the guises through which agents and objects are conceived: i.e., they are perspectives.

</details>
