### [50] [Towards Robotic Dexterous Hand Intelligence: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13925)
*Weiguang Zhao,Xihao Guo,Tian Liang,Rui Zhang,Irwin King,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从硬件设计、控制与学习方法、数据集与评估规范四个互补维度，系统综述了机器人灵巧手研究，梳理了关键权衡与发展脉络，并总结了当前局限与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手研究在硬件构型、感知配置、任务设定、训练数据和评估协议等方面假设各异，缺乏系统性比较，模糊了领域发展轨迹。本文旨在通过多维度整合分析，提供结构化理解并厘清关键开放挑战。

Method: 从四个互补方面进行综述：1）硬件层面分析驱动、传动、感知及代表性设计；2）方法论层面按主要范式归类控制与学习方法并追踪时序演化；3）整理数据集、模态设计与评估规范，将方法进展与训练、基准测试及评估方式关联分析；4）讨论当前局限并总结未来方向。

Result: 揭示了灵巧手在力控能力、柔顺性、带宽、集成度与系统复杂性之间的关键权衡；梳理了控制与学习方法的范式演进路径；整合了数据集与评估实践，使方法论进展可与训练和评估方式相互印证。

Conclusion: 通过连通硬件分析、方法发展、数据资源和评估体系，本文为灵巧手研究提供了结构化认知框架，明确了当前最关键的开放挑战，为后续研究提供了系统化的参考和方向指引。

Abstract: Robotic dexterous hands are central to contact-rich manipulation, with rapid progress driven by advances in hardware, sensing, control, simulation, and data generation. However, existing studies are often developed under different assumptions regarding hand embodiments, sensory configurations, task settings, training data, and evaluation protocols, making systematic comparison difficult and obscuring the developmental trajectory of the field. This survey provides a holistic review of dexterous hand research from four complementary aspects. First, we present a hardware-level analysis covering actuation, transmission, perception, and representative hand designs, highlighting the key trade-offs in force capability, compliance, bandwidth, integration, and system complexity. Furthermore, we review control and learning methods for dexterous manipulation from a methodological perspective, grouping representative works by major paradigms and tracing their evolution in chronological order. In addition, we consolidate datasets, modality design, and evaluation practices, which enables methodological progress to be interpreted together with the ways in which it is trained, benchmarked, and assessed. Finally, we discuss the major limitations of current dexterous hand research and summarize the corresponding future directions. By connecting hardware analysis, methodological development, data resources, and evaluation, this survey aims to provide a structured understanding of dexterous hand research and to clarify the most important open challenges for future study.

</details>


### [51] [Ergodic Imitation for Adaptive Exploration around Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13996)
*Ziyi Xu,Cem Bilaloglu,Yiming Li,Sylvain Calinon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应遍历模仿学习方法，通过从演示中构建目标分布，在轨迹跟踪与探索之间自适应插值，解决模仿学习中训练与部署条件不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习中，训练与部署条件存在不匹配（如环境变化、观测与控制不完美），导致机器人遵循名义轨迹时可能卡住而无法完成任务，需要自适应的在线探索策略。

Method: 提出自适应遍历模仿方法：从检索到的演示几何中构建目标分布，利用该分布在检索的滚动时域框架中生成轨迹，在跟踪与探索之间自适应插值；将遍历控制从传统的区域覆盖搜索扩展至演示驱动的自适应模仿。

Result: 未在摘要中明确报告具体实验结果。

Conclusion: 该方法通过扩展遍历控制到演示驱动的滚动时域框架，实现了自适应模仿，能够应对训练与部署条件不匹配的情况。

Abstract: In robotics, a common challenge in imitation learning is the mismatch between training and deployment conditions, caused, for example, by environmental changes or imperfect observation and control. When a robot follows a nominal trajectory under such mismatch, it may become stuck and fail to complete the task. This calls for adaptive online exploration strategies that remain grounded in demonstrations. To this end, we propose an adaptive ergodic imitation approach that constructs a target distribution from the geometry of the retrieved demonstrations and uses it to generate trajectories that adaptively interpolate between tracking and exploration. Our method extends ergodic control beyond its traditional role in area-coverage and search by incorporating demonstrations into a retrieval-based receding-horizon framework for adaptive imitation.

</details>


### [52] [Motion Planning for Autonomous Vehicles using Optimization over Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14199)
*Matheus Wagner,Antônio Augusto Fröhlich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究基于凸集图（GCS）的优化方法在自动驾驶场景中近似非线性最优控制问题的能力。通过将自由空间表示为有向凸区域图、使用贝塞尔曲线参数化路径、结合简化动力学模型，该方法在保持凸性的同时生成无碰撞且动力学可行的轨迹，计算效率优于非线性规划，且对初始化不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性最优控制方法虽能提供高保真解，但计算成本高且对初始值敏感；几何规划方法可扩展性强，但常将路径选择与轨迹优化解耦。现有方法难以在保持凸性、计算效率和求解质量之间取得平衡。

Method: 将自由空间表示为有向凸区域图（GCS），通过离散连接决策处理非凸几何，同时在每个区域内保持凸轨迹约束；使用贝塞尔曲线参数化空间路径，多项式时间缩放函数处理时间演化；基于小滑移和线性轮胎假设的简化动力学自行车模型，通过对轨迹导数的凸约束近似实现动力学可行性。

Result: 在CommonRoad场景（静态障碍物避让和换道）中测试，GCS方法能生成与非线性规划高度匹配的无碰撞且动力学一致的轨迹，计算效率更优，对初始化敏感性更低。

Conclusion: GCS提供了一种结构化的非线性运动规划问题近似方法，能够在连续松弛中保持凸性的同时捕捉主要的几何和动力学效应，在自动驾驶运动规划中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Motion planning for autonomous vehicles requires generating collision-free and dynamically feasible trajectories in complex environments under real-time constraints. While nonlinear optimal control formulations provide high-fidelity solutions, they are computationally demanding and sensitive to initialization, whereas geometric planning methods scale well but often decouple path selection from trajectory optimization. This paper studies the extent to which optimization over Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS) can approximate solutions of nonlinear optimal control problems in the context of autonomous driving. The free space is represented as a finite union of convex regions organized as a directed graph, allowing nonconvex geometry to be handled through discrete connectivity decisions while maintaining convex trajectory constraints within each region. Vehicle motion is parameterized using Bezier curves for the spatial path and a polynomial time-scaling function for temporal evolution. Under small-slip and linear tire assumptions, a simplified dynamic bicycle model enables approximate enforcement of dynamic feasibility through convex constraints on trajectory derivatives. The approach is evaluated in CommonRoad scenarios involving static obstacle avoidance and lane-changing maneuvers, and is compared against a nonlinear discrete-time optimal control formulation. The results indicate that the GCS-based method generates collision-free and dynamically consistent trajectories that closely match those obtained from the nonlinear program, while exhibiting improved computational efficiency and reduced sensitivity to initialization. These findings suggest that GCS provides a structured approximation of nonlinear motion planning problems, capturing dominant geometric and dynamic effects while preserving convexity in the continuous relaxation.

</details>


### [53] [Behavior Cloning for Active Perception with Low-Resolution Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14106)
*Anthony Bilic,Chen Chen,Ladislau Bölöni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究在结构化物体寻找任务中，行为克隆是否足以产生主动感知能力。使用低成本机械臂和腕部RGB相机，通过预测相对关节增量而非绝对位置，从低分辨率图像直接预测关节命令，在闭环控制下成功完成了重新定位部分可见植物的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究行为克隆这一简单方法是否能够在结构化物体寻找任务中产生主动感知行为（即通过动作改善未来观察的能力），验证低成本和简单方法的可行性。

Method: 使用低成本机械臂配备腕部安装的自我中心RGB相机，模型直接从低分辨率RGB图像预测关节命令，在闭环控制下执行任务。比较了预测绝对关节位置与预测相对关节增量两种策略。

Result: 低分辨率自我中心视觉足以可靠完成任务；预测相对关节增量显著优于预测绝对关节位置。

Conclusion: 视觉基础的主动感知可以从行为克隆中涌现，且该方法具有可重复性。

Abstract: We investigate whether behavior cloning is sufficient to produce active perception in a structured object-finding task. A low-cost robot arm equipped with a wrist-mounted egocentric RGB camera must reposition to center a partially visible plant before triggering a grasp signal, requiring actions that improve future observations. The model predicts joint commands directly from low-resolution RGB images under closed-loop control. We show that low-resolution egocentric vision is sufficient for reliable task completion and that predicting relative joint deltas substantially outperforms absolute joint position prediction in our setting. These results demonstrate that visually grounded active perception can emerge from behavior cloning in a reproducible setting.

</details>


### [54] [MAPLE: Latent Multi-Agent Play for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14201)
*Rajeev Yasarla,Deepti Hegde,Hsin-Pai Cheng,Shizhong Han,Yunxiao Shi,Meysam Sadeghigooghari,Hanno Ackermann,Litian Liu,Pranav Desai,Fatih Porikli,Mohammad Ghavamzadeh,Hong Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAPLE提出了一种在VLA模型隐空间中执行反应式多智能体闭环展开的框架，通过两阶段训练（监督微调+强化学习）实现鲁棒的端到端自动驾驶，无需外部模拟器，在Bench2Drive上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型作为端到端运动规划器在闭环评估中表现脆弱，因为它们在传统模仿学习框架下训练；现有的闭环监督方法缺乏可扩展性且无法完全建模反应式环境。

Method: MAPLE包含两个训练阶段：(1) 基于真值轨迹进行隐空间展开的监督微调；(2) 使用全局与智能体特定奖励（安全性、进展、交互真实性）以及多样性奖励进行强化学习。自车与周围交通智能体在多步时间窗口上独立控制并相互反应，实现闭环训练。

Result: 在Bench2Drive上取得了最先进的驾驶性能，证明了可扩展的闭环多智能体交互对于鲁棒端到端自动驾驶系统的有效性。

Conclusion: MAPLE是一种无需外部模拟器、可扩展的闭环训练框架，通过隐空间反应式多智能体展开和两阶段训练显著提升VLA模型在闭环场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are effective as end-to-end motion planners, but can be brittle when evaluated in closed-loop settings due to being trained under traditional imitation learning framework. Existing closed-loop supervision approaches lack scalability and fail to completely model a reactive environment. We propose MAPLE, a novel framework for reactive, multi-agent rollout of a dynamic driving scenario in the latent space of the VLA model. The ego vehicle and nearby traffic agents are independently controlled over multi-step horizons, while being reactive to other agents in the scene, enabling closed-loop training. MAPLE consists of two training stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning on the latent rollouts based on ground-truth trajectories, followed by (2) reinforcement learning with global and agent -specific rewards that encourage safety, progress, and interaction realism. We further propose diversity rewards that encourage the model to generate planning behaviors that may not be present in logged driving data. Notably, our closed-loop training framework is scalable and does not require external simulators, which can be computationally expensive to run and have limited visual fidelity to the real-world. MAPLE achieves state-of-the-art driving performance on Bench2Drive and demonstrates scalable, closed-loop multi-agent play for robust E2E autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [55] [Reactive Planning based Control for Mobile Robots in Obstacle-Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14232)
*Li Tan,Junlin Xiong,Yan Wang,Wei Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对部分环境信息下的移动机器人避障运动控制问题，提出了一种基于反应式规划的控制策略（RPCS），通过连接初始与目标位置形成参考轨迹，结合反应式规划策略（RPS）进行局部避障修改，以及自适应跟踪控制策略（ATCS）进行轨迹跟踪，最终通过数值算例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在障碍物密集环境中仅有部分环境信息时，如何从初始位置无碰撞地到达目标位置是一个挑战性问题，现有方法在实时性和适应性方面存在不足。

Method: 首先连接初始与目标位置生成参考轨迹；然后提出反应式规划策略（RPS），基于部分环境信息对参考轨迹进行局部修改以实现避障；接着提出自适应跟踪控制策略（ATCS），利用离散化技术跟踪可能带有局部修改的参考轨迹；最后将RPS和ATCS结合为RPCS。

Result: 通过数值算例验证了所提RPCS策略的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: RPCS能够在部分环境信息条件下实现移动机器人的无碰撞运动控制，兼顾了避障反应能力和轨迹跟踪精度。

Abstract: This paper addresses the motion control problem for mobile robots in obstacle-cluttered environments. The mobile robot has partial environment information only, and aims to move from an initial position to a target position without collisions. For this purpose, a reactive planning based control strategy (RPCS) is proposed. First, the initial and target positions are connected as a reference trajectory. Then, a reactive planning strategy (RPS) is developed to ensure the collision avoidance by modifying the reference trajectory locally based on the partial environment information. Next, an adaptive tracking control strategy (ATCS) is proposed to track the reference trajectory with potentially local modifications via the discretization techniques. Finally, the RPS and ATCS are combined to establish the RPCS, whose efficacy and advantages are illustrated by numerical examples.

</details>


### [56] [SeaVis: Modeling and Control of a Remotely Operated Towed Vehicle for Seabed Visualization and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14683)
*Abdelhakim Amer,Aske Alstrup,Frederik Rasmussen,Yury Brodskiy,Andriy Sarabakha,Erdal Kayacan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对SeaVis型遥控拖曳式水下机器人提出了一种增益调度线性二次型调节器（LQR），用于深度和姿态的鲁棒控制。在高保真仿真中，与传统PID控制器相比，LQR在扰动鲁棒性、控制效率和舵面驱动方面表现更优，且增益调度确保了全速域有效性。仿真环境与控制器已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率海底测绘需要水下机器人具备稳定且精确的定位能力，而传统控制方法在复杂海床环境中难以兼顾鲁棒性与控制效率。

Method: 建立SeaVis型遥控拖曳式水下机器人的新型数学模型，设计增益调度线性二次型调节器（LQR），并在高保真仿真环境中与PID控制器进行对比验证。

Result: LQR控制器在扰动鲁棒性、控制效率上显著优于PID，且大幅降低了舵面驱动幅度；增益调度机制保证了全工作速度范围内的控制有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的增益调度LQR方法能够为拖曳式水下机器人提供更鲁棒、更高效的控制方案，适用于复杂海底地形的高分辨率测绘任务。

Abstract: High-resolution seafloor mapping necessitates stable and precise positioning for underwater robots. This paper introduces a novel mathematical model for SeaVis remotely operated towed vehicles (ROTVs) and develops a gain-scheduled linear-quadratic regulator (LQR) for robust depth and attitude control. We validate the approach in a high-fidelity simulation, benchmarking the LQR against a conventional PID controller over a challenging seabed profile. The presented results demonstrate the LQR's superior performance, with significantly enhanced robustness to disturbances, greater control efficiency, and substantially reduced flap actuation. The gain scheduling also confirms the controller's effectiveness across the full operational velocity range. The complete simulation environment and controller are open-sourced.

</details>


### [57] [Distill: Uncovering the True Intent behind Human-Robot Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14262)
*Ting Li,David Porfirio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Distill方法，通过去除冗余步骤、泛化步骤含义、放松顺序约束，从用户初始任务规约中提炼真实意图，以解决人机交互中自然语言模糊性和编程过度具体化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机交互方式（自然语言和终端用户编程）无法充分捕捉用户真实意图：自然语言存在不精确和歧义性，而终端用户编程又过于具体。理解用户真实意图成为人机通信系统的核心挑战。

Method: 提出Distill方法，包含三个核心操作：(1) 去除不必要的步骤；(2) 泛化单个步骤背后的含义；(3) 放宽步骤之间的顺序约束。并在Web界面上实现该系统。

Result: 通过众包研究验证，Distill能够从初始任务规约中有效提炼和精炼用户意图。

Conclusion: Distill为改善人机交互中的意图理解提供了有效途径，弥合了自然语言的歧义性与编程的过度具体化之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: As robots become increasingly integrated into everyday environments, intuitive communication paradigms such as natural language and end-user programming have become indispensable for specifying autonomous robot behavior. However, these mechanisms are ineffective at fully capturing user intent: natural language is imprecise and ambiguous, whereas end-user programming can be overly specific. As a result, understanding what users truly mean when they interact with robots remains a central challenge for human-AI communication systems. To address this issue, we propose the Distill approach for human-robot communication interfaces. Given a task specification provided by the user, Distill (1) removes unnecessary steps; (2) generalizes the meaning behind individual steps; and (3) relaxes ordering constraints between steps. We implemented Distill on a web interface and, through a crowdsourcing study, demonstrated its ability to elicit and refine user intent from initial task specifications.

</details>


### [58] [A Prototyping Framework for Distributed Control of Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15049)
*Junaid Ahmed Memon,Allan Andre Do Nascimento,Kostas Margellos,Antonis Papachristodoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于SPMD范式的多机器人分布式控制原型框架，能够在单台计算机上模拟分布式控制，并通过四旋翼位置交换任务验证了该框架在不同动力学层级（点质量模型、高保真模型、硬件实验平台）上的有效性，为分布式算法的验证提供了低成本、易访问的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合分布式优化算法在理论研究和实际测试之间的鸿沟，提供一个低成本、易访问的原型框架来验证多机器人系统的分布式控制算法。

Method: 采用单程序多数据（SPMD）范式，在单台计算机上利用多核处理器模拟分布式控制，每个核心运行相同的算法但使用本地状态和邻居间通信；使用非合作博弈论分布式算法，在四个层级（点质量模型、高保真四旋翼模型、Crazyflie硬件实验平台）上测试四旋翼位置交换任务。

Result: 该框架在不同动力学层级上均能有效运行，计算时间和轨迹数据的一致性表明框架可以低成本地验证分布式算法，并且结果与硬件实验吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该原型框架为多机器人分布式控制算法的验证提供了一种低成本、易访问的解决方案，能够有效连接理论算法与实际应用。

Abstract: This paper presents a prototyping framework for distributed control of multi-robot systems, aimed at bridging theory and practical testing of distributed optimization algorithms. Using the Single Program, Multiple Data (SPMD) paradigm, the framework emulates distributed control on a single computer, with each core running the same algorithm using local states and neighbour-to-neighbour communication. We demonstrate the framework on a four-quadrotor position-swapping task using a non-cooperative game-theoretic distributed algorithm. Computational time and trajectory data are compared across the supported dynamics levels: a point-mass model, a high-fidelity quadrotor model, and an experimental hardware testbed using Crazyflie quadcopters. The results show that the framework provides a low-cost and accessible approach for validating distributed algorithms.

</details>


### [59] [Behavioral Data-Driven Optimal Trajectory Generation for Rotary Cranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14944)
*Iskandar Khemakhem,Manuel Zobel,Johannes Schüle,Oliver Sawodny,Naoki Uchiyama,Abdallah Farrage*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于行为数据驱动的框架，用于生成旋转起重机的开环回转轨迹，在抑制负载摇摆的同时减少操作时间和能量消耗。该方法基于Willems基本引理及其推广，绕过显式系统建模，直接利用测量输入输出数据，通过凸优化生成平滑最优轨迹。在实验室起重机平台上验证，与经典模型方法相比，负载摇摆减少35%，跟踪误差减少43%，行程时间减少50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑行业增长和全球熟练劳动力短缺使得起重机控制自动化对安全高效操作日益重要。经典模型控制方法依赖精确动力学模型和专家调参，难以满足安全和精度要求；而基于学习的方法需要大量数据和计算资源。因此需要一种既能减少负载振荡又能降低操作时间和能耗的数据驱动方法。

Method: 基于Willems基本引理及其推广的行为数据驱动框架，绕过显式系统建模，直接从测量输入输出数据中识别系统的非参数化表示，利用凸优化生成平滑最优的开环回转轨迹。

Result: 在实验室起重机平台上验证，与经典模型方法相比：负载摇摆减少35%，跟踪误差减少43%，行程时间减少50%。

Conclusion: 该方法在不足驱动动力学特性下，利用有限数据和凸优化即可生成最优轨迹，减少了对专家知识的依赖，在摇摆抑制、跟踪精度和操作效率方面均优于传统模型方法。

Abstract: With the growth of the construction industry and the global shortage of skilled labor, the automation of crane control has become increasingly important for safe and efficient operations. A central challenge in automatic crane control is the reduction of load oscillations during motion, which is primarily addressed through appropriate slewing trajectories. In this context, classical model-based control methods rely on accurate dynamical models and expert tuning, and often struggle to meet safety and precision requirements, while many learning-based approaches require large data sets and significant computational resources. This paper proposes a behavioral data-driven framework for generating open-loop slewing trajectories for rotary cranes that suppress load sway while reducing operation time and energy consumption. The approach builds on Willems' fundamental lemma and its generalizations, to bypass explicit system modeling and operate directly on measured input-output data. A practical workflow is presented in this paper to reduce the need for expert knowledge. Despite the underactuated nature of the crane dynamics, the method identifies a nonparametric representation of the system behavior and generates smooth, optimal trajectories using limited data and convex optimization. The proposed trajectory generation method is validated on a laboratory crane setup and compared against an established model-based approach, achieving up to 35% reduction in load sway, 43% reduction in tracking error, and 50% reduction in travel time.

</details>


### [60] [Energy-Efficient Quadruped Locomotion with Compliant Feet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14411)
*Pramod Pal,Shishir Kolathaya,Ashitava Ghosal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究在四足机器人足部集成柔性元件，通过强化学习训练不同刚度的足部策略，发现适中的弹簧刚度可降低约17%的步行能耗，表明适当足部柔性可在不牺牲稳定性的前提下提升运动效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四足机器人使用刚性足部虽控制简单，但限制了冲击吸收和弹性储能能力，导致运动能耗较高。本文旨在探索足部柔性能否通过强化学习控制器提升步行效率。

Method: 在仿真中训练了8种不同弹簧刚度对应的RL运动控制策略，并在实际四足机器人上进行交叉评估，以每米机械能耗作为效率度量指标。

Result: 中等刚度的弹簧足部相比极硬或极软足部，在实际实验中能耗降低约17%，仿真结果呈现相似趋势。

Conclusion: 选择合适的足部柔顺性可以在不导致运动失稳的前提下有效提升四足机器人的运动效率。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are often designed with rigid feet to simplify control and maintain stable contact during locomotion. While this approach is straightforward, it limits the ability of the legs to absorb impact forces and reuse stored elastic energy, leading to higher energy expenditure during locomotion. To explore whether compliant feet can provide an advantage, we integrate foot compliance into a reinforcement learning (RL) locomotion controller and study its effect on walking efficiency. In simulation, we train eight policies corresponding to eight different spring stiffness values and then cross-evaluate their performance by measuring mechanical energy consumed per meter traveled. In experiments done on a developed quadruped, the energy consumption for the intermediate stiffness spring is lower by ~ 17% when compared to a very stiff or a very flexible spring incorporated in the feet, with similar trends appearing in the simulation results. These results indicate that selecting an appropriate foot compliance can improve locomotion efficiency without destabilizing the robot during motion.

</details>


### [61] [Before the Body Moves: Learning Anticipatory Joint Intent for Language-Conditioned Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14417)
*Haozhe Jia,Honglei Jin,Yuan Zhang,Youcheng Fan,Shaofeng Liang,Lei Wang,Shuxu Jin,Kuimou Yu,Zinuo Zhang,Jianfei Song,Wenshuo Chen,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DAJI是一个层次化框架，通过学习语言生成与闭环控制之间的前瞻性联合意图接口，实现人形机器人流式全身控制。它包含DAJI-Act（通过学生驱动rollout从未来感知教师中蒸馏出可部署的扩散动作策略）和DAJI-Flow（从语言和意图历史中自回归生成未来意图块）。在HumanML3D和BABEL基准上分别达到94.42%的rollout成功率和0.152的子序列FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言条件人形机器人系统存在局限：要么生成运动学参考后由低级跟踪器被动修复，要么使用隐式动作策略无法显式编码即将发生的接触变化、支撑转移和平衡准备。需要一种既能即时执行又能预判物理状态转换的控制表示方法。

Method: 提出DAJI层次化框架：(1) DAJI-Act：通过学生驱动rollout，从未来感知的教师策略中蒸馏出可部署的扩散动作策略；(2) DAJI-Flow：从语言指令和意图历史中自回归生成未来意图块，构建语言生成与闭环控制之间的前瞻性联合意图接口。

Result: 在HumanML3D风格生成任务上达到94.42%的rollout成功率，在BABEL任务上达到0.152的子序列FID，在单指令生成和流式指令跟随任务上也表现优异。

Conclusion: DAJI通过前瞻性联合意图接口有效连接了语言生成与闭环控制，实现了人形机器人的流式全身控制，在多个基准上取得领先结果。

Abstract: Natural language is an intuitive interface for humanoid robots, yet streaming whole-body control requires control representations that are executable now and anticipatory of future physical transitions. Existing language-conditioned humanoid systems typically generate kinematic references that a low-level tracker must repair reactively, or use latent/action policies whose outputs do not explicitly encode upcoming contact changes, support transfers, and balance preparation. We propose \textbf{DAJI} (\emph{Dynamics-Aligned Joint Intent}), a hierarchical framework that learns an anticipatory joint-intent interface between language generation and closed-loop control. DAJI-Act distills a future-aware teacher into a deployable diffusion action policy through student-driven rollouts, while DAJI-Flow autoregressively generates future intent chunks from language and intent history. Experiments show that DAJI achieves strong results in anticipatory latent learning, single-instruction generation, and streaming instruction following, reaching 94.42\% rollout success on HumanML3D-style generation and 0.152 subsequence FID on BABEL.

</details>


### [62] [Let Robots Feel Your Touch: Visuo-Tactile Cortical Alignment for Embodied Mirror Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14571)
*Tianfang Zhu,Ning An,Rui Wang,Jiasi Gao,Qingming Luo,Anan Li,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Mirror Touch Net，通过视觉与触觉表征的语义、分布和几何对齐，使机器人具备镜像触觉能力——能从RGB图像预测机器人手上1140个触觉点的毫米级触觉信号，并泛化到观测人体触觉刺激产生反射式响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过视觉-触觉共振（镜像触觉）支持共情与社会感知，其神经基础在于视觉皮层与躯体感觉皮层之间的结构对应关系。然而现有机器人系统缺乏实现这一原则的计算框架，限制了机器人触觉感知的预测能力和人机交互的共情水平。

Method: 提出Mirror Touch Net，通过三级约束（语义对齐、分布对齐、几何对齐）实现视觉与触觉表征间的跨模态对齐。利用流形分析验证约束将视觉表征重塑为与触觉流形一致的几何结构，降低了跨模态映射的复杂度。该方法从RGB图像预测机器人手上1140个触觉点的触觉信号，并扩展至跨域人体手部观测。

Result: 该方法能从RGB图像预测毫米级精度的触觉信号（覆盖1140个触觉点）；流形分析表明多级约束使视觉表征与触觉流形几何一致；方法可泛化至观测人体手部触觉刺激并产生反射式响应。

Conclusion: 将视觉-触觉共振的神经原则与机器人感知相连接，为预测性触觉和共情人机交互提供了可解释的技术路径。

Abstract: Observing touch on another's body can elicit corresponding tactile sensations in the observer, a phenomenon termed mirror touch that supports empathy and social perception. This visuo-tactile resonance is thought to rely on structural correspondence between visual and somatosensory cortices, yet robotic systems lack computational frameworks that instantiate this principle. Here we demonstrate that cortical correspondence can be operationalized to endow robots with mirror touch. We introduce Mirror Touch Net, which imposes semantic, distributional and geometric alignment between visual and tactile representations through multi-level constraints, enabling prediction of millimetre-scale tactile signals across 1,140 taxels on a robotic hand from RGB images. Manifold analysis reveals that these constraints reshape visual representations into geometry consistent with the tactile manifold, reducing the complexity of cross-modal mapping. Extending this alignment framework to cross-domain observations of human hands enables tactile prediction and reflexive responses to observed human touch. Our results link a neural principle of visuo-tactile resonance to robotic perception, providing an explainable route towards anticipatory touch and empathic human-robot interaction. Code is available at https://github.com/fun0515/Mirror-Touch-Net.

</details>


### [63] [SR-Platform: An Agentic Pipeline for Natural Language-Driven Robot Simulation Environment Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14700)
*Ben Wei Lim,Minh Duc Le,Thang Truong,Thanh Nguyen Canh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SR-Platform 是一个生产级智能体系统，可将自然语言描述自动转换为可执行的 MuJoCo 机器人仿真环境，通过四阶段流水线（编排、资产生成、布局规划、场景桥接）在约50秒内生成包含五个物体的场景，显著降低仿真环境构建的手工成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成机器人仿真环境是仿真驱动机器人学习的主要瓶颈，构建一个可直接用于训练的 MuJoCo 场景通常需要3D资产建模、MJCF规范、空间布局、碰撞避免和机器人模型集成等多方面专业知识，手工操作繁琐且效率低下。

Method: 系统采用四阶段流水线架构：(1) LLM编排器将用户意图转化为结构化场景规划；(2) 资产锻造模块通过LLM-to-CadQuery合成或缓存检索生成3D几何体；(3) 布局架构师分配物体姿态并验证工业约束；(4) 桥接层组装最终MJCF场景并融合机器人模型。系统以九服务Docker栈部署，集成WebSocket进度流、MinIO网格存储、Qdrant语义资产检索、Redis作业状态和InfluxDB遥测。

Result: 基于30天生产遥测数据（611次成功LLM调用），系统生成五物体场景的中位端到端延迟约50秒，缓存加速场景约30-40秒。资产锻造模块首次尝试重试率为11.3%并具有自动恢复能力，缓存资产检索可消除对已生成物体类型的逐物体LLM调用。

Conclusion: 智能体式场景合成能够显著减少创建多样化机器人训练环境所需的手工劳动，用户可在不到一分钟内通过自然语言提示生成可执行的MuJoCo场景，验证了该方法的实用性和高效性。

Abstract: Generating robot simulation environments remains a major bottleneck in simulation-based robot learning. Constructing a training-ready MuJoCo scene typically requires expertise in 3D asset modeling, MJCF specification, spatial layout, collision avoidance, and robot-model integration. We present SR-Platform, a production-deployed agentic system that converts free-form natural language descriptions into executable, physically valid MuJoCo environments. SR-Platform decomposes scene synthesis into four stages: an LLM-based orchestrator that converts user intent into a structured scene plan; an asset forge that retrieves cached assets or generates new 3D geometry through LLM-to-CadQuery synthesis; a layout architect that assigns object poses and verifies industrial constraints; and a bridge layer that assembles the final MJCF scene and merges the selected robot model. The system is deployed as a nine-service Docker stack with WebSocket progress streaming, MinIO-backed mesh storage, Qdrant-based semantic asset retrieval, Redis job state, and InfluxDB telemetry. Using 30 days of production telemetry covering 611 successful LLM calls, SR-Platform generates five-object scenes with a median end-to-end latency of approximately 50 s, while cache-accelerated scenes complete in approximately 30-40 s. The asset forge shows an 11.3% first-attempt retry rate with automatic recovery, and cached asset retrieval removes per-object LLM calls for previously generated object types. These results show that agentic scene synthesis can reduce the manual effort required to create diverse robot training environments, enabling users to produce executable MuJoCo scenes from plain English prompts in under one minute.

</details>


### [64] [IntentVLA: Short-Horizon Intent Modeling for Aliased Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14712)
*Shijie Lian,Bin Yu,Xiaopeng Lin,Zhaolong Shen,Laurence Tianruo Yang,Yurun Jin,Haishan Liu,Changti Wu,Hang Yuan,Cong Huang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IntentVLA是一种历史条件化的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）框架，通过编码近期视觉观测为紧凑的短时意图表示来条件化动作块生成，解决了机器人模仿数据中的多模态性问题（即相似的观测可能对应不同的动作块），从而减少跨规划步骤的意图重采样导致的块间冲突和不稳定执行。同时引入了AliasBench基准来评估短时观测歧义场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有帧条件化的VLA策略仅依赖当前观测和指令来推断动作块，在部分可观测性下，相邻重规划步骤可能重新采样不同的短时意图，导致块间冲突和执行不稳定。机器人模仿数据本身具有多模态性（相似观测对应不同动作），这一问题亟需解决。

Method: IntentVLA通过编码近期视觉观测序列为紧凑的短时意图表示，并将该意图表示作为条件来生成动作块。具体而言，将历史观测信息压缩为隐式意图表征，以此消除观测歧义，使动作生成更加一致和稳定。

Result: 在AliasBench（12任务歧义感知基准）、SimplerEnv、LIBERO和RoboCasa等多个基准上，IntentVLA相比强VLA基线方法显著提升了 rollout 稳定性，并在各基准上取得了更优性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入历史观测信息的短时意图表示，IntentVLA有效缓解了部分可观测性下的多模态动作冲突问题，提升了机器人动作生成的稳定性和一致性，为处理模仿数据中的歧义问题提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Robot imitation data are often multimodal: similar visual-language observations may be followed by different action chunks because human demonstrators act with different short-horizon intents, task phases, or recent context. Existing frame-conditioned VLA policies infer each chunk from the current observation and instruction alone, so under partial observability they may resample different intents across adjacent replanning steps, leading to inter-chunk conflict and unstable execution. We introduce IntentVLA, a history-conditioned VLA framework that encodes recent visual observations into a compact short-horizon intent representation and uses it to condition chunk generation. We further introduce AliasBench, a 12-task ambiguity-aware benchmark on RoboTwin2 with matched training data and evaluation environments that isolate short-horizon observation aliasing. Across AliasBench, SimplerEnv, LIBERO, and RoboCasa, IntentVLA improves rollout stability and outperforms strong VLA baselines

</details>


### [65] [Exploring Bottlenecks in VLM-LLM Navigation: How 3D Scene Understanding Capability Impacts Zero-Shot VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14801)
*Ziyi Xia,Chaoran Xiong,Litao Wei,Xinhao Hu,Ling Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文量化了3D场景理解能力对零样本视觉-语言导航（VLN）性能的实际影响，提出了基于拓扑映射语义的慢速LLM规划器和基于空间坐标与边界框的快速反应导航器两个核心子系统的统计成功率上限，发现了感知饱和现象——感知精度提升超过阈值后对导航成功的回报递减。研究建议VLN的3D场景理解应从严格像素级精度转向关注导航相关核心词汇和准确的边界框比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前零样本VLN系统中，3D感知模型追求像素级精度，与具身导航严格的计算限制和实时效率要求相冲突，存在关键瓶颈。本文旨在量化3D场景理解能力对VLN性能的实质影响，并探索感知精度与导航成功率之间的关系。

Method: 基于典型的VLM-LLM框架，提出两个核心子系统的统计成功率上限：1) 依赖拓扑映射语义的慢速LLM规划器；2) 利用空间坐标和边界框执行LLM决策的快速反应导航器。使用最先进的3D场景理解模型进行验证。

Result: 验证了所提出的成功率上限，并发现了感知饱和现象：感知精度提升超过某一阈值后，对导航成功的回报呈递减趋势。

Conclusion: 3D场景理解在VLN中应放弃对严格像素级精度的追求，转而优先关注导航相关的核心词汇和准确的边界框比例。

Abstract: Zero-shot vision-and-language navigation (VLN) has gained significant attention due to its minimal data collection costs and inherent generalization. This paradigm is typically driven by the integration of pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and Large Language Models (LLMs), where VLMs construct 3D scene graphs while LLMs handle high-level reasoning and decision-making. However, a critical bottleneck exists in this system: current 3D perception models prioritize pixel-level accuracy, directly conflicting with the strict computational limits and real-time efficiency demanded by embodied navigation. To address this gap, this paper quantifies the actual impact of 3D scene understanding capability on VLN performance. Based on typical VLM-LLM frameworks, we propose statistical success rate (SR) upper bounds for two core subsystems: 1) the slow LLM planner, which relies on topological mapping semantics, and 2) the fast reactive navigator, which utilizes spatial coordinates and bounding boxes to execute LLM decisions. Evaluations using state-of-the-art 3D scene understanding models validate our proposed bounds and reveal a perception saturation phenomenon, indicating that improvements in perception accuracy beyond a certain threshold yield diminishing returns in navigation success. Our findings suggest that 3D scene understanding for VLN should pivot away from strict pixel-level precision, prioritizing instead navigation-relevant core vocabularies and accurate bounding box proportions.

</details>


### [66] [Learning Cross-Coupled and Regime Dependent Dynamics for Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14805)
*Rishabh Dev Yadav,Samaksh Ujjawal,Sihao Sun,Spandan Roy,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结构化编码器-解码器框架，用于空中机械臂的残差动力学自适应学习。该框架通过非线性潜变量编码器捕获交叉耦合和时间依赖性，轻量级线性潜变量解码器实现在线自适应，结合贝叶斯自适应和协方差膨胀，在真实平台上验证了残差预测精度、自适应速度和MPC轨迹跟踪性能的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机械臂在复杂任务如载荷运输中面临强耦合、延迟气动交互和任务依赖的动态变化，导致残差动力学存在交叉耦合、历史依赖和非平稳特性，传统分析模型和纯离线学习模型在部署时性能退化。

Method: 提出结构化编码器-解码器框架：非线性潜变量编码器从状态-输入历史中捕获交叉变量耦合和时间依赖性；轻量级线性潜变量解码器利用线性参数结构实现封闭形式的贝叶斯自适应和一致性驱动的协方差膨胀，支持对瞬态和慢变动态的快速稳定自适应，并与实时MPC兼容。

Result: 在真实空中机械臂平台上，所提方法在残差预测精度、变工况下的自适应速度以及基于MPC的轨迹跟踪性能方面均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 联合建模耦合的时间动态和部署时的非平稳性对于可靠的空中机械臂操作至关重要。

Abstract: Accurate dynamics models are critical for aerial manipulators operating under complex tasks such as payload transport. However, modeling these systems remains fundamentally challenging due to strong quadrotor-manipulator coupling, delayed aerodynamic interactions, and regime-dependent dynamics variations arising from payload changes and manipulator reconfiguration. These effects produce residual dynamics that are simultaneously cross-coupled, history-dependent, and nonstationary, causing both analytical models and purely offline learned models to degrade during deployment. To address these challenges, we propose a structured encoder-decoder framework for adaptive residual dynamics learning in aerial manipulators. The proposed nonlinear latent encoder captures cross-variable coupling and temporal dependencies from state-input histories, while a lightweight linear latent decoder enables online adaptation under regime-dependent nonstationary dynamics. The linear-in-parameter decoder structure permits closed-form Bayesian adaptation together with consistency-driven covariance inflation, enabling rapid and stable adaptation to both transient and slowly varying dynamics changes while remaining compatible with real-time model predictive control (MPC). Experimental results on a real aerial manipulation platform demonstrate improved residual prediction accuracy, faster adaptation under changing operating conditions, and enhanced MPC-based trajectory tracking performance. These results highlight the importance of jointly modeling coupled temporal dynamics and deployment-time nonstationarity for reliable aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [67] [CaMeRL: Collision-Aware and Memory-Enhanced Reinforcement Learning for UAV Navigation in Multi-Scale Obstacle Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14810)
*Hong Hong,Feiyu Liao,Yongheng Liang,Boning Zhang,Haitao Wang,Hejun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无人机避障导航中多尺度障碍物（超小和超大）导致的感知退化问题，提出CaMeRL框架，通过碰撞感知潜在编码保留小障碍物细节，结合时序记忆模块缓解大障碍物遮挡造成的部分可观测性，在超小和超大障碍物场景下成功率分别提升0.48和0.28。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机避障方法多关注障碍物数量或密度，忽视尺度变化带来的挑战；单帧深度图的纯几何特征容易忽略小障碍物，且在大障碍物遮挡下丢失空间上下文，导致多尺度环境中性能显著下降。

Method: 提出CaMeRL强化学习框架：1）碰撞感知潜在表示模块，编码风险敏感深度线索以保留细粒度障碍物结构；2）时序记忆模块，跨帧整合观测以缓解大障碍物遮挡导致的局部可观测性。在包含超小和超大障碍物的多尺度场景中评估。

Result: 在多尺度障碍物场景中，CaMeRL在所有尺度上优于SOTA基线，超小障碍物设置下成功率提升0.48，超大障碍物设置下提升0.28，且在杂乱户外环境中实现可靠导航。

Conclusion: CaMeRL通过碰撞感知编码和时序记忆有效解决了多尺度障碍物导航难题，验证了风险敏感深度表示和跨帧时序集成对提升无人机避障鲁棒性的重要作用。

Abstract: In obstacle avoidance navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), variations in obstacle scale have received strangely less attention than obstacle number or density. Existing methods typically extract purely geometric features from single-frame depth observations. Such representations tend to neglect small obstacles and lose spatial context under occlusions caused by large obstacles, leading to noticeable degradation in environments with multi-scale obstacles. To address this issue, we propose CaMeRL, a Collision-aware and Memory-enhanced Reinforcement Learning framework for UAV navigation. The collision-aware latent representation encodes risk-sensitive depth cues to preserve fine-grained obstacle structures, thereby improving sensitivity to small obstacles. The temporal memory module integrates observations across frames, mitigating partial observability caused by large-obstacle occlusions. We evaluate CaMeRL with multi-scale obstacles, including ultra-small and extra-large obstacle settings. Results show that CaMeRL outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across all scales, with success rate gains of 0.48 and 0.28 in the ultra-small and extra-large settings, respectively. More importantly, CaMeRL achieves reliable navigation in cluttered outdoor environments.

</details>


### [68] [Learning Direct Control Policies with Flow Matching for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14832)
*Marcello Ceresini,Federico Pirazzoli,Andrea Bertogalli,Lorenzo Cipelli,Filippo D'Addeo,Anthony Dell'Eva,Alessandro Paolo Capasso,Alberto Broggi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流匹配的自动驾驶规划器，直接输出由加速度和曲率定义的可执行控制轨迹。模型以鸟瞰图栅格为条件，仅需少量ODE积分步即可生成控制序列，实现低延迟闭环重规划。仅在城市交通模拟器数据上训练，但能泛化到分布外的高速公路和未见城市场景。归因于BEV表示的几何中心特性和流匹配的平滑向量场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶规划方法在分布外场景下泛化能力不足，且推理延迟难以满足实时闭环重规划需求。作者希望设计一种直接输出控制信号的规划器，在保持低延迟的同时具备跨场景的鲁棒泛化能力。

Method: 采用流匹配（Flow Matching）框架，以BEV栅格图像为条件，通过求解常微分方程（ODE）在少量积分步内生成加速度和曲率轮廓控制轨迹。训练数据来源于2D交通模拟器中的城市场景（含真实街道、交叉口、环岛），包含反应式智能体。

Result: 在闭环评估中，模型能可靠泛化到分布外的多车道高速公路和未见城市场景，保持稳定闭环控制并成功完成任务。与训练分布差异显著的环境下仍表现良好。

Conclusion: BEV表示提供几何中心视角，对分布偏移不敏感；流匹配学习平滑向量场，在分布偏移下性能退化优雅。两者结合使模型具备强泛化能力。

Abstract: We present a flow-matching planner for autonomous driving that directly outputs actionable control trajectories defined by acceleration and curvature profiles. The model is conditioned on a bird's-eye-view (BEV) raster of the surrounding scene and generates control sequences in a small number of Ordinary Differential Equations (ODE) integration steps, enabling low-latency inference suitable for real-time closed-loop re-planning. We train exclusively on urban scenarios (real urban city streets, intersections and roundabouts of the city of Parma, Italy) collected from a 2D traffic simulator with reactive agents, and evaluate in closed-loop on both in-distribution and markedly out-of-distribution environments, including multi-lane highways and unseen urban scenarios. Our results show that the model generalizes reliably to these unseen conditions, maintaining stable closed-loop control and successfully completing scenarios that differ substantially from the training distribution. We attribute this to the BEV representation, which provides a geometry-centric view of the scene that is inherently less sensitive to distributional shifts, and to the flow-matching formulation, which learns a smooth vector field that degrades gracefully under distribution shift. We provide video demonstrations of closed-loop behavior at https://marcelloceresini.github.io/DirectControlFlowMatching.

</details>


### [69] [Chrono-Gymnasium: An Open-Source, Gymnasium-Compatible Distributed Simulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14911)
*Bocheng Zou,Harry Zhang,Khailanii Slaton,Jingquan Wang,Derrick Ruan,Huzaifa Mustafa Unjhawala,Radu Serban,Dan Negrut*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Chrono-Gymnasium是一个基于Ray框架的分布式计算平台，它将高保真多体动力学引擎Project Chrono扩展到大规模计算集群，通过标准Gymnasium接口实现与机器学习库的无缝集成。实验证明，该框架在不牺牲物理精度的前提下显著缩短了高保真仿真的实际运行时间，并成功应用于自主机器人导航的强化学习训练和行星着陆器设计的贝叶斯优化两个案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真物理仿真对缩小机器人与复杂机械系统的sim-to-real差距至关重要，但其计算开销限制了在强化学习和全局优化等数据密集型任务中的应用。现有方案难以在高保真度和可扩展性之间取得平衡。

Method: 基于Ray分布式计算框架构建Chrono-Gymnasium，将Project Chrono的高保真多体动力学仿真扩展到大规模集群。提供标准Gymnasium接口以兼容现代机器学习库，并内置同步和消息传递原语以支持分布式执行。

Result: 在两个案例中验证了框架性能：(1) 在复杂地形上的自主机器人导航强化学习训练；(2) 行星着陆器设计参数的贝叶斯优化以保障着陆稳定性。结果表明，Chrono-Gymnasium在不牺牲物理精度的条件下减少了高保真仿真的实际运行时间（wall-clock time）。

Conclusion: Chrono-Gymnasium为复杂机器人系统的设计与控制提供了一条可扩展的高保真仿真路径，有效解决了高保真度与计算效率之间的矛盾，有助于推动仿真到现实的迁移。

Abstract: High-fidelity physics simulation is essential for closing the sim-to-real gap in robotics and complex mechanical systems. However, the computational overhead of high-fidelity engines often limits their use in data-intensive tasks like Reinforcement Learning (RL) and global optimization. We introduce Chrono-Gymnasium, a distributed computing framework that scales the high-fidelity multi-body dynamics of Project Chrono across large-scale computing clusters. Built upon the Ray framework, Chrono-Gymnasium provides a standardized Gymnasium interface, enabling seamless integration with modern machine learning libraries while providing built-in synchronization and messaging primitives for distributed execution. We demonstrate the framework's capabilities through two distinct case studies: (1) the training of an RL agent for autonomous robotic navigation in complex terrains, and (2) the Bayesian Optimization of a planetary lander's design parameters to ensure landing stability. Our results show that Chrono-Gymnasium reduces wall-clock time for high-fidelity simulations without sacrificing physical accuracy, offering a scalable path for the design and control of complex robotic systems.

</details>


### [70] [FU-MPC: Frontier- and Uncertainty-Aware Model Predictive Control for Efficient and Accurate UAV Exploration with Motorized LiDAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14920)
*Jianping Li,Pengfei Wan,Zhongyuan Liu,Yi Wang,Yiheng Chen,Xinhang Xu,Rui Jin,Boyu Zhou,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种配备独立驱动旋转激光雷达的无人机分层探索框架FU-MPC，通过全局拓扑规划与局部滚动时域扫描控制联合优化LiDAR旋转，在保持鲁棒定位的同时提升复杂环境下的探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无人机在未知环境探索中，覆盖扩展与定位质量之间存在耦合矛盾：扩大观测范围往往需要额外的平移或旋转机动，这会降低探索效率并在几何挑战性场景中增加定位退化风险。现有系统很少将激光雷达扫描自由度作为与探索进度和定位质量显式关联的决策变量。

Method: 构建配备独立驱动旋转激光雷达的无人机平台，提出分层探索框架：全局规划器将前沿组织为代表性视点并利用拓扑感知转移代价排序；局部FU-MPC控制器沿预测轨迹优化LiDAR旋转，联合优化前沿感知探索效用与方向相关定位不确定性，通过轻量替代评估实现实时机载执行。

Result: 在复杂环境实验中，与固定模式扫描和仅考虑不确定性的基线方法相比，所提系统在提升探索效率的同时保持了鲁棒的定位性能。

Conclusion: 主动调整LiDAR旋转方向作为额外感知自由度，能够有效解耦探索与运动之间的强耦合关系，在保证定位可靠性的前提下提高探索效率。

Abstract: Efficient UAV exploration in unknown environments requires rapid coverage expansion while maintaining accurate and reliable localization, since safe navigation in complex scenes depends on consistent mapping and pose estimation. However, for conventional LiDAR-equipped UAVs, the observable region is tightly coupled with the UAV pose and motion. Expanding coverage often requires additional translational or rotational maneuvers, which can reduce exploration efficiency and increase the risk of localization degradation in geometrically challenging environments. Motorized rotating LiDARs provide a promising solution by actively adjusting the sensor viewing direction without changing the UAV motion, thereby introducing an additional sensing degree of freedom. Nevertheless, existing exploration systems rarely exploit this scanning freedom as an explicit decision variable linked to both exploration progress and localization quality. To address this gap, we develop a UAV platform equipped with an independently actuated rotating LiDAR and propose a hierarchical exploration framework. The global planner organizes frontiers into representative viewpoints and sequences them using topology-aware transition costs. Built upon this planner, FU-MPC serves as a local receding-horizon scan controller that optimizes LiDAR rotation along the predicted flight trajectory. The controller jointly considers frontier-aware exploration utility and direction-dependent localization uncertainty, while lightweight surrogate evaluation enables real-time onboard execution. Experiments in complex environments demonstrate that the proposed system improves exploration efficiency while maintaining robust localization performance compared with fixed-pattern scanning and uncertainty-only baselines. The project page can be found at https://kafeiyin00.github.io/FU-MPC/.

</details>


### [71] [SOCC-ICP: Semantics-Assisted Odometry based on Occupancy Grids and ICP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15074)
*Johannes Scherer,Sebastian Hirt,Henri Meeß*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOCC-ICP是一种语义辅助的里程计框架，联合进行语义占用网格建图和激光雷达扫描配准，通过统一表示实现与SOTA相当的定位精度，并直接为下游任务提供可用地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激光雷达里程计方法使用的点、面元或NDT地图表示与下游任务（如运动规划）常用的语义占用网格不一致，导致冗余的地图结构。

Method: 提出SOCC-ICP框架，每个地图体素编码几何和语义统计信息，基于局部平面性自适应选择点对点或点对平面ICP，通过射线投射的自由空间更新过滤动态物体，并将语义标签融入地图构建、降采样和对应加权中。

Result: 在多种评估场景中，SOCC-ICP达到与SOTA激光雷达里程计竞争的性能，在几何退化环境中保持鲁棒性；引入语义标签后进一步提升了精度。

Conclusion: 统一里程计与语义占用网格建图为单一表示，消除冗余地图结构，可直接为下游机器人应用提供合适的地图。

Abstract: Reliable pose estimation in previously unseen environments is a fundamental capability of autonomous systems. Existing LiDAR odometry methods typically employ point-, surfel-, or NDT-based map representations, which are distinct from the semantic occupancy grids commonly used for downstream tasks such as motion planning. We introduce SOCC-ICP, a semantics-assisted odometry framework that jointly performs Semantic OCCupancy grid mapping and LiDAR scan alignment. Each map voxel encodes geometric and semantic statistics, enabling adaptive point-to-point or point-to-plane ICP based on local planarity. Further, the occupancy grid naturally filters dynamic objects through raycasting-based free-space updates. Across diverse evaluation scenarios, SOCC-ICP achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art LiDAR odometry and remains robust in geometrically degenerate environments, even in the absence of semantic cues. When semantic labels are available, integrating them into map construction, downsampling, and correspondence weighting yields further accuracy gains. By unifying odometry and semantic occupancy grid mapping within a single representation, SOCC-ICP eliminates redundant map structures and directly provides a map suitable for downstream robotic applications.

</details>


### [72] [CLOVER: Closed-Loop Value Estimation \& Ranking for End-to-End Autonomous Driving Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15120)
*Sining Ang,Yuguang Yang,Canyu Chen,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLOVER提出了一种闭环价值估计与排序框架，通过生成器-评分器架构、伪专家轨迹构建、集合级覆盖监督以及保守闭环自蒸馏方法，解决了端到端自动驾驶规划中模仿学习与基于规则评估之间的不匹配问题，在NAVSIM和nuScenes上取得了SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶规划器通常通过模仿单条驾驶轨迹来训练，但评估时采用基于规则规划指标（安全性、可行性、进度、舒适性），导致训练与评估不匹配——靠近日志轨迹的方案可能违反规则，而偏离演示的有效方案反而得分更高。这一问题在候选-选择型规划器中尤为突出。

Method: CLOVER采用轻量级生成器-评分器架构：(1) 生成器产生多样化候选轨迹，通过评估器过滤构造伪专家轨迹并进行集合级覆盖监督；(2) 评分器预测规划指标子分数以在推理时排序；(3) 进行保守闭环自蒸馏：评分器拟合真实评估子分数，生成器向教师选择的top-k和向量Pareto目标优化，并加入稳定性正则化。

Result: 在NAVSIM上达到94.5 PDMS和90.4 EPDMS，创SOTA；在更具挑战性的NavHard上获得48.3 EPDMS，匹配最强结果；在nuScenes开环评估中实现最低L2误差和碰撞率。

Conclusion: CLOVER通过闭环评分器介入生成器训练，证明只要评分器选择的目标在真实评估下是富集的且更新保持保守，评分器介导的生成器改进是可靠的，有效缓解了模仿学习与规则评估之间的不匹配问题。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving planners are commonly trained by imitating a single logged trajectory, yet evaluated by rule-based planning metrics that measure safety, feasibility, progress, and comfort. This creates a training--evaluation mismatch: trajectories close to the logged path may violate planning rules, while alternatives farther from the demonstration can remain valid and high-scoring. The mismatch is especially limiting for proposal-selection planners, whose performance depends on candidate-set coverage and scorer ranking quality. We propose CLOVER, a Closed-LOop Value Estimation and Ranking framework for end-to-end autonomous driving planning. CLOVER follows a lightweight generator--scorer formulation: a generator produces diverse candidate trajectories, and a scorer predicts planning-metric sub-scores to rank them at inference time. To expand proposal support beyond single-trajectory imitation, CLOVER constructs evaluator-filtered pseudo-expert trajectories and trains the generator with set-level coverage supervision. It then performs conservative closed-loop self-distillation: the scorer is fitted to true evaluator sub-scores on generated proposals, while the generator is refined toward teacher-selected top-$k$ and vector-Pareto targets with stability regularization. We analyze when an imperfect scorer can improve the generator, showing that scorer-mediated refinement is reliable when scorer-selected targets are enriched under the true evaluator and updates remain conservative. On NAVSIM, CLOVER achieves 94.5 PDMS and 90.4 EPDMS, establishing a new state of the art. On the more challenging NavHard split, it obtains 48.3 EPDMS, matching the strongest reported result. On supplementary nuScenes open-loop evaluation, CLOVER achieves the lowest L2 error and collision rate among compared methods. Code data will be released at https://github.com/WilliamXuanYu/CLOVER.

</details>


### [73] [Pelican-Unified 1.0: A Unified Embodied Intelligence Model for Understanding, Reasoning, Imagination and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15153)
*Yi Zhang,Yinda Chen,Che Liu,Zeyuan Ding,Jin Xu,Shilong Zou,Junwei Liao,Jiayu Hu,Xiancong Ren,Xiaopeng Zhang,Yechi Liu,Haoyuan Shi,Zecong Tang,Haosong Sun,Renwen Cui,Kuishu Wu,Wenhai Liu,Yang Xu,Yingji Zhang,Yidong Wang,Senkang Hu,Jinpeng Lu,Nga Teng Chan,Yechen Wu,Yong Dai,Jian Tang,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pelican-Unified 1.0 是首个基于统一化原则训练的具身基础模型，通过单一VLM同时实现理解、推理、想象和动作，在多个基准上达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身模型通常将理解、推理、想象和动作分为三个孤立的专家系统，缺乏统一框架，导致各模块间信息共享不足。本文旨在验证统一化原则在不牺牲专项能力的前提下能否实现多能力的协同优化。

Method: 使用单一VLM作为统一理解模块（映射场景、指令、视觉上下文和动作历史到共享语义空间）和统一推理模块（自回归生成任务、动作和面向未来的思维链，并将最终隐藏状态投影为稠密潜变量）；统一未来生成器（UFG）基于该潜变量通过两个模态特定输出头在同一个去噪过程中联合生成未来视频和未来动作；语言、视频和动作损失均反向传播到共享表示中，实现端到端联合优化。

Result: 单个检查点在八个VLM基准上平均64.7（同规模最佳），WorldArena上66.03（排名第一），RoboTwin上93.5（动作方法中第二）。

Conclusion: 统一化原则在保持专项能力优势的同时，成功将理解、推理、想象和动作集成到单一模型中，表明统一化并不意味着性能妥协。

Abstract: We present Pelican-Unified 1.0, the first embodied foundation model trained according to the principle of unification. Pelican-Unified 1.0 uses a single VLM as a unified understanding module, mapping scenes, instructions, visual contexts, and action histories into a shared semantic space. The same VLM also serves as a unified reasoning module, autoregressively producing task-, action-, and future-oriented chains of thought in a single forward pass and projecting the final hidden state into a dense latent variable. A Unified Future Generator (UFG) then conditions on this latent variable and jointly generates future videos and future actions through two modality-specific output heads within the same denoising process. The language, video, and action losses are all backpropagated into the shared representation, enabling the model to jointly optimize understanding, reasoning, imagination, and action during training, rather than training three isolated expert systems.
  Experiments demonstrate that unification does not imply compromise. With a single checkpoint, Pelican-Unified 1.0 achieves strong performance across all three capabilities: 64.7 on eight VLM benchmarks, the best among comparable-scale models; 66.03 on WorldArena, ranking first; and 93.5 on RoboTwin, the second-best average among compared action methods. These results show that the unified paradigm succeeds in preserving specialist strength while bringing understanding, reasoning, imagination, and action into one model.

</details>


### [74] [Hand-in-the-Loop: Improving Dexterous VLA via Seamless Interventional Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15157)
*Zhuohang Li,Liqun Huang,Wei Xu,Zhengming Zhu,Nie Lin,Xiao Ma,Xinjun Sheng,Ruoshi Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HandITL方法，通过融合人类纠正意图与自主策略执行，解决灵巧操作中VLA模型因接管时刻手势突变导致的复合错误问题，将接管抖动降低99.8%，抓取失败减少87.5%，并提升下游策略性能19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在高维动作空间和接触丰富动力学场景下易累积复合错误，而现有IIL方法在灵巧手操作中存在遥操作与策略执行之间的指令不匹配，导致接管时刻出现"手势突变"问题。

Method: HandITL（Hand-in-the-Loop）——一种无缝的人机循环干预方法，通过混合人类纠正意图与自主策略执行，避免双手灵巧操作中的手势突变。

Result: 相比直接遥操作接管，接管抖动降低99.8%，抓取失败减少87.5%，平均完成时间减少19.1%；收集的干预数据训练的策略在三个长程灵巧任务上平均性能提升19%。

Conclusion: HandITL有效解决了灵巧操作中的人机干预冲突，在双手协调、工具使用和细粒度长程操作任务中均表现优异，为IIL在灵巧操作中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are prone to compounding errors in dexterous manipulation, where high-dimensional action spaces and contact-rich dynamics amplify small policy deviations over long horizons. While Interactive Imitation Learning (IIL) can refine policies through human takeover data, applying it to high-degree-of-freedom (DoF) robotic hands remains challenging due to a command mismatch between human teleoperation and policy execution at the takeover moment, which causes abrupt robot-hand configuration changes, or "gesture jumps". We present Hand-in-the-Loop (HandITL), a seamless human-in-the-loop intervention method that blends human corrective intent with autonomous policy execution to avoid gesture jumps during bimanual dexterous manipulation. Compared with direct teleoperation takeover, HandITL reduces takeover jitter by 99.8% and preserves robust post-takeover manipulation, reducing grasp failures by 87.5% and mean completion time by 19.1%. We validate HandITL on tasks requiring bimanual coordination, tool use, and fine-grained long-horizon manipulation. When used to collect intervention data for policy refinement, HandITL yields policies that outperform those trained with standard teleoperation data by 19% on average across three long-horizon dexterous tasks.

</details>

### [75] [BiSpikCLM: A Spiking Language Model integrating Softmax-Free Spiking Attention and Spike-Aware Alignment Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13859)
*Sihang Guo,Chenlin Zhou,Jiaqi Wang,Kehai Chen,Qingyan Meng,Zhengyu Ma*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出BiSpikCLM，首个全二值脉冲因果语言模型，通过Softmax-Free Spiking Attention (SFSA)消除浮点运算，并引入Spike-Aware Alignment Distillation (SpAD)实现高效训练，以极低计算成本达到可比性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脉冲大语言模型仍依赖大量浮点矩阵乘法和非线性操作，或面临复杂时空动态导致的训练困难，亟需更高效的全二值化方案。

Method: 提出BiSpikCLM，包含两个核心组件：1) Softmax-Free Spiking Attention (SFSA)，消除自回归语言建模中的softmax和浮点运算；2) Spike-Aware Alignment Distillation (SpAD)，在嵌入、注意力图、中间特征和输出logits四个层面对齐ANN教师与SNN学生。

Result: 在1.3B模型上仅需5.6%的训练token即可达到与ANN相当的性能；计算成本仅为ANN的4.16%-5.87%。

Conclusion: 验证了全二值脉冲驱动LLM的可行性和有效性，确立了蒸馏方法作为脑启发脉冲NLP的 promising 路径。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer promising energy-efficient alternatives to large language models (LLMs) due to their event-driven nature and ultra-low power consumption. However, to preserve capacity, most existing spiking LLMs still incur intensive floating-point matrix multiplication (MatMul) and nonlinearities, or training difficulties arising from the complex spatiotemporal dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose BiSpikCLM, the first fully binary spiking MatMul-free causal language model. BiSpikCLM introduces Softmax-Free Spiking Attention (SFSA), eliminating softmax and floating-point operations in autoregressive language modeling. For efficient training, we introduce Spike-Aware Alignment Distillation (SpAD), which aligns ANN teacher and SNN student across embeddings, attention maps, intermediate features, and output logits. SpAD framework allows BiSpikCLM to reach comparable performance to ANN counterparts using substantially fewer training tokens (e.g., only 5.6% of the tokens for the 1.3B model). As a result, BiSpikCLM achieves competitive performance at only 4.16% - 5.87% of the computational cost on natural language generation tasks. Our results highlight the feasibility and effectiveness of fully binary spike-driven LLMs and establish the distillation as a promising pathway for brain-inspired spiking NLP.

</details>


### [76] [Indian Wedding System Optimization (IWSO): A Novel Socially Inspired Metaheuristic with Operational Design and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13871)
*Deepika Saxena,Kishu Gupta,Jitendra Kumar,Jatinder Kumar,Sakshi Patni,Vinaytosh Mishra,Niharika Singh,Ashutosh Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出了一种受印度婚礼社会文化动态启发的新型元启发式算法IWSO，通过媒人引导策略和自适应淘汰重组机制解决复杂优化问题，在基准测试中表现出优于GA、PSO、DE、CS等算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受传统印度婚礼中家庭、候选人和媒人之间的协作匹配过程启发，开发一种新的种群元启发式优化算法，旨在提供一种无需外部参数的引导式选择性搜索框架。

Method: IWSO包含两项关键创新：(i) 媒人引导影响策略——精英解引导弱候选解的进化，无需外部参数即可增强收敛性；(ii) 自适应淘汰与重组机制——通过替换表现不佳的个体来维持种群多样性并防止早熟收敛。采用加权多目标适应度函数，并进行了理论时间和空间复杂度分析。

Result: 在高维和多模态基准测试函数上的大量实验表明，IWSO在收敛速度、解质量和鲁棒性方面均优于GA、PSO、DE、CS等现有优化方法。

Conclusion: IWSO作为一种新颖的种群元启发式算法，有效模拟了印度婚礼的匹配过程，通过其特有的引导和自适应机制在复杂优化问题上展现了更优的搜索性能。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel population-based metaheuristic, Indian Wedding System Optimization (IWSO), inspired by the socio-cultural dynamics of traditional Indian weddings. IWSO models the matchmaking process driven by collaboration among families, candidates, and matchmakers as a guided, selective search framework for solving complex optimization problems. The algorithm introduces two key innovations: (i) a matchmaker-guided influence strategy, where elite solutions direct the evolution of weaker candidates, enhancing convergence without external parameters; and (ii) an adaptive elimination and reinitialization mechanism that maintains diversity and prevents premature convergence by replacing underperforming individuals. IWSO employs a weighted multi-objective fitness function and analytically derived time and space complexity, benchmarked against existing optimization approaches such as Genetic Algorithm (GA), Partical Swarm Optimization (PSO), Differential Evolution (DE), Cuckoo Search (CS), etc. Extensive experiments on benchmark high-dimensional and multimodal test functions demonstrate superior performance of IWSO in terms of convergence speed, solution quality, and robustness.

</details>


### [77] [S-AI-Recursive: A Bio-Inspired and Temporal Sparse AI Architecture for Iterative, Introspective, and Energy-Frugal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13872)
*Said Slaoui*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出S-AI-Recursive架构，将推理建模为受两种拮抗激素（Clarifine和Confusionin）调控的递归闭环过程，通过迭代状态精化实现认知均衡，在SAI-UT+测试集上以不到千万参数取得竞争性推理性能，验证了“时间简约性”原则：迭代认知深度可替代架构宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统前馈式AI架构缺乏迭代自校正机制，推理过程无法动态收敛到稳定认知状态，且过度依赖参数规模扩展来提升性能。受生物体内激素调节闭环系统的启发，本文旨在构建一个具备递归认知循环的稀疏AI架构，通过激素调控的迭代精化机制实现参数高效的推理能力。

Method: 提出递归推理循环（RRC）作为动力系统模型，引入两种激素信号：Clarifine（收敛信号）和Confusionin（不确定性检测器），二者通过拮抗调控驱动迭代状态精化；建立了完整的数学框架，包括递归状态动力学、Lyapunov稳定性证明、熵收缩定理、激素驱动的有限时间终止准则、Euler-Maruyama离散化投影、迭代预算下的原始-对偶智能体选择以及带热启动加速的递归记忆机制。

Result: 在SAI-UT+测试基准上的实验表明，S-AI-Recursive以低于一千万参数的规模，在抽象和符号推理任务上取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 验证了“时间简约性”（temporal parsimony）核心原则：迭代认知深度可以替代架构宽度，即通过递归时间维度的深化而非参数量维度的扩展来实现高效推理。

Abstract: This article introduces S-AI-Recursive, a bio-inspired Sparse Artificial Intelligence architecture in which reasoning is operationalized as a hormonal closed-loop iteration rather than a single feed-forward pass. Building upon the S-AI foundational framework [1], the hormonal-probabilistic unification doctrine [2], and the formal mathematical methodology established in S-AI-IoT [3], the present work formalizes the Recursive Reasoning Cycle (RRC) as a dynamical system governed by two novel hormones: Clarifine, a convergence signal, and Confusionin, an uncertainty detector, whose antagonistic regulation drives iterative state refinement toward a stable cognitive equilibrium. The complete mathematical framework is developed, including recursive state dynamics, Lyapunov stability proof, entropic contraction theorem, hormonal stopping criterion with finite-time termination guarantee, Euler-Maruyama discretization with projection, primal-dual agent selection under iteration budget, and recursive engram memory with warm-start acceleration. Experimental validation on the SAI-UT+ testbench demonstrates that S-AI-Recursive achieves competitive reasoning performance on abstract and symbolic benchmarks with fewer than ten million parameters, confirming the central principle of temporal parsimony: iterative cognitive depth substitutes for architectural width.

</details>


### [78] [ARES-LSHADE: Autoresearch-Enhanced LSHADE with Memetic Polish for the GNBG Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13877)
*Abdullah Naeem,Md Wasi Ul kabir,Manish Bhatt,Ayon Dey,Anav Katwal,Md Tamjidul Hoque*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: ARES-LSHADE 是一种基于LLM设计的记忆差分进化算法，在GECCO 2026的GNBG基准测试中获得了510/744胜场，在18/24个函数上达到机器精度。论文还报告了LLM驱动研究循环中的两个方法论发现：仅使用算子编辑面和适应度观测空间会收敛到特征平台，而扩展观测空间虽能解决所有函数但违反了黑箱规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与GECCO 2026的LLM设计进化算法竞赛，在LLM-LSHADE 2025获胜者的基础上进一步提升算法在GNBG基准测试上的性能，并探索LLM驱动自动化算法设计的边界与约束。

Method: 在LLM-LSHADE基础上引入两个新组件：(a) 通过约30次LLM驱动设计实验的自主动研究循环产生的侦察增强变异算子（含自适应CMA-ES集成）；(b) 多起点L-BFGS-B精炼阶段，且严格遵循基准的黑箱处理规则。

Result: 在官方31次运行/函数的评估中，ARES-LSHADE获得510/744胜场（函数间差距低于1e-8），在24个函数中的18个上达到机器精度。剩余6个函数呈现与GNBG组合结构一致的特征平台特征。

Conclusion: LLM驱动研究循环在仅使用算子编辑面和适应度观测空间时会收敛到特征平台；扩展观测空间（包含基准的组合元数据）虽能解决所有函数但违反了黑箱规则。论文讨论了LLM能力与基准完整性之间的权衡，作为未来LLM驱动优化算法研究的设计考量。

Abstract: We present ARES-LSHADE, a memetic differential-evolution variant submitted to the GECCO 2026 competition on LLM-designed evolutionary algorithms for the Generalized Numerical Benchmark Generator (GNBG). The algorithm builds on the LLM-LSHADE 2025 winner, contributing two new components: (a) a scout-augmented mutation operator with adaptive CMA-ES integration, produced by an autonomous research loop across approximately thirty LLM-driven design experiments, and (b) a multi-start L-BFGS-B polish phase that respects strict blackbox treatment of the benchmark. On the official 31-run-per-function evaluation with the competition-specified function-evaluation budgets, ARES-LSHADE obtains 510 of 744 wins (per-function gap below 1e-8), reaching machine precision on 18 of 24 functions. The remaining six functions exhibit characteristic plateau signatures consistent with GNBG's compositional structure, and were independently identified by the autoresearch loop as the hardest of the suite. Beyond the result itself, this report documents two methodological observations: (i) an LLM-driven research loop with operator-only edit surface and fitness-only observation space converges to a characteristic plateau on this benchmark; (ii) when we initially widened the observation space to include the benchmark's compositional metadata, the resulting algorithm trivially solved all 24 functions but violated the competition's blackbox rule, which we identified before submission. We discuss this tension between LLM capability and benchmark integrity as a design consideration for future LLM-driven optimization-algorithm research. Code and reproducibility artifacts are available at https://github.com/anaeem1/ARES-LSHADE.

</details>


### [79] [Darwin Family: MRI-Trust-Weighted Evolutionary Merging for Training-Free Scaling of Language-Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14386)
*Taebong Kim,Youngsik Hong,Minsik Kim,Sunyoung Choi,Jaewon Jang,Junghoon Shin,Minseo Kim*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: Darwin Family提出了一种无需训练的进化式大模型合并框架，通过梯度无关的权重空间重组，在不进行额外训练的情况下提升模型推理能力。其旗舰模型Darwin-27B-Opus在GPQA Diamond上达到86.9%，在1252个模型中排名第6，超越了原始基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否在不进行额外训练的前提下，通过重组已有检查点中编码的潜在能力来提升前沿级别的推理性能，以替代成本高昂的后训练流程。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：(i) 14维自适应合并基因组，支持细粒度的组件级和块级重组；(ii) MRI-Trust Fusion，通过可学习的信任参数自适应平衡诊断性层重要性信号与进化搜索；(iii) Architecture Mapper，实现异构模型家族间的跨架构杂交。

Result: 旗舰模型Darwin-27B-Opus在GPQA Diamond上达到86.9%，在1252个评估模型中排名第6，超越了其完全训练的基础模型。在4B到35B参数规模上，Darwin模型均持续优于其父模型，并支持递归的多代进化。

Conclusion: 诊断引导的进化式合并是推理密集型语言模型的一种实用且可复现的替代方案，可替代成本高昂的后训练流程，且支持Transformer与Mamba组件的跨架构合并。

Abstract: We present Darwin Family, a framework for training-free evolutionary merging of large language models via gradient-free weight-space recombination. We ask whether frontier-level reasoning performance can be improved without additional training, by reorganizing latent capabilities already encoded in existing checkpoints. Darwin introduces three key ideas: (i) a 14-dimensional adaptive merge genome enabling fine-grained component- and block-level recombination; (ii) MRI-Trust Fusion, which adaptively balances diagnostic layer-importance signals with evolutionary search through a learnable trust parameter; and (iii) an Architecture Mapper that enables cross-architecture breeding between heterogeneous model families. Empirically, the flagship Darwin-27B-Opus achieves 86.9% on GPQA Diamond, ranking #6 among 1,252 evaluated models, and outperforming its fully trained foundation model without any gradient-based training. Across scales from 4B to 35B parameters, Darwin models consistently improve over their parents, support recursive multi-generation evolution, and enable a training-free evolutionary merge that combines Transformer- and Mamba-based components. Together, the Darwin Family demonstrates that diagnostic-guided evolutionary merging is a practical and reproducible alternative to costly post-training pipelines for reasoning-centric language models.

</details>


### [80] [First Mathematical Runtime Analyses of Multi-Objective Evolutionary Algorithms for Multi-Valued Decision Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14836)
*Mingfeng Li,Zheng Cheng,Weijie Zheng,Benjamin Doerr*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文首次对多值决策空间（变量取值$r>2$）下的多目标进化算法进行数学运行时分析。针对$r$值版本的OneMinMax基准问题，证明了经典SEMO算法覆盖Pareto前沿的期望函数评估次数上界为$O(n^2 r^2 \log n)$，下界为$\Omega(n^2 r (r + \log n))$，并通过仅接受严格改进解的变体算法将上界改进至$O(n^2 r (r + \log n))$，与下界匹配。结果表明经典MOEA处理多值变量时不会遇到显著额外困难，但更复杂种群动态的算法需要更高级分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标进化算法在二元决策空间中的理论分析已较为成熟，但实际应用中决策变量常取多个离散值（$r>2$），然而针对此类多值决策空间的MOEA数学运行时分析完全空白。本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 以经典SEMO算法为分析对象，采用单位强度局部变异算子，研究其在$r$值OneMinMax基准问题上的性能。分别推导了标准SEMO和仅接受严格改进解的变体SEMO的期望运行时间上下界，运用概率分析和鞅方法进行论证。

Result: 标准SEMO上界$O(n^2 r^2 \log n)$，下界$\Omega(n^2 r (r + \log n))$；严格改进变体上界$O(n^2 r (r + \log n))$，与下界匹配。上下界之间的微小差距通过变体算法得以消除。

Conclusion: 经典MOEA在处理多值决策变量时不会遇到显著额外困难，算法性能主要受变量取值数$r$和问题规模$n$的多项式影响。但对于具有更复杂种群动态的算法，需要开发更高级的分析工具才能获得紧界。

Abstract: Problems defined on binary decision spaces have been intensively studied in the theory of multi-objective evolutionary algorithms (MOEAs). In contrast, no mathematical runtime analyses exist so far for MOEAs dealing with decision variables that take a finite number $r > 2$ of values, despite the prevalence of such problems in practice. In this work, we begin to fill this research gap. We analyze how the classic SEMO algorithm with unit-strength local mutation computes the Pareto front of an $r$-valued counterpart of the classic \oneminmax benchmark. For the expected number of function evaluations until the Pareto front is covered by the population of this MOEA, we prove an upper bound of $O(n^2 r^2 \log n)$ and a near-tight lower bound of $Ω(n^2 r (r + \log n))$. We can close the small remaining gap between these two bounds by considering a variant of the algorithm that accepts only strictly better solutions; for this variant, we show an upper bound of $O(n^2 r (r + \log n))$, matching our lower bound (which also holds for this variant). Our results suggest that classic MOEAs encounter no significant additional difficulties when dealing with multi-valued decision variables. However, significantly more advanced tools may be required to obtain tight bounds for algorithms with more complex population dynamics.

</details>

### [81] [Time Domain Near Memory Computing Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14162)
*Sarthak Antal,Steve Enosh*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于低精度乘累加运算的时域近存计算架构，通过电流舵DAC和N脉冲生成器实现时域乘法，并对比了延迟单元型和计数器型两种累加方案。在40 MHz、1V供电下实现了7.62 TOPS/W的能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工作负载的计算需求日益增长，数据搬运能耗远超计算能耗。全数字架构虽稳健但能效不足，而模拟存内计算依赖外围DAC/ADC带来额外开销。现有方案难以在能效、精度和设计复杂度之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 提出时域近存计算架构，将数字权重位通过电流舵DAC转换，输入向量由N脉冲生成器编码，在时域完成乘法。对比了两种累加方案：延迟单元型架构和计数器型架构。N脉冲生成器和计数器采用RTL综合实现以提升技术可移植性，电流舵DAC保留在模拟域。

Result: 在1V供电下，4x4 MAC原型实现40 MHz工作频率、42 uW功耗、7.62 TOPS/W能效。

Conclusion: 所提时域近存计算架构在保持数字友好接口的同时实现了高能效，两种累加方案在线性度、可扩展性和功耗效率方面各有权衡，RTL综合实现的数字部分提升了技术可移植性。

Abstract: The increasing computational demand of AI workloads has intensified the need for energy-efficient in-memory and near-memory computing architectures, particularly because data movement often consumes significantly more energy than computation itself. While fully digital architectures provide robust scalability and support higher-resolution computation, analog in-memory computing has demonstrated improved energy efficiency for low-precision workloads. However, its reliance on peripheral DACs and ADCs introduces additional power, area, and design overhead. To address these challenges, this work presents a time-domain near-memory computing architecture for low-precision multiply-and-accumulate (MAC) operations. In the proposed approach, digital weight bits stored in SRAM are converted using a current-steering DAC, while the digital input vector is encoded by an N-pulse generator. This enables multiplication to be performed in the time domain while maintaining a digital-friendly interface. Two accumulation schemes, a delay-cell-based architecture and a counter-based architecture, are investigated and compared in terms of design trade-offs, linearity, scalability, and power efficiency. To improve technology portability, the N-pulse generator and counters are implemented using RTL synthesis, while the current-steering DAC remains in the analog domain. A 4 x 4 MAC prototype is implemented with a 1 V supply, achieving an operating frequency of 40 MHz, power consumption of 42 uW, and energy efficiency of 7.62 TOPS/W.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

### [82] [Memory Forensics Techniques for Automated Detection and Analysis of Go Malware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14020)
*Hala Ali,Andrew Case,Irfan Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对Go二进制文件运行时分析的记忆取证框架，通过解析Go内部结构、ABI感知的逆向分析和协程栈分析，能够恢复类型/函数元数据、堆/静态字符串、执行路径和运行时参数值。作为Volatility 3插件实现，在BRICKSTORM后门、Obscura勒索软件和Pantegana RAT等恶意样本中成功恢复了C2端点、持久化机制、加密密钥、赎金笔记和执行状态等运行时证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Go语言因其静态链接、跨平台编译和激进优化等特点，使得传统分析技术难以有效分析Go恶意软件。现有静态分析和逆向工程工具仅能处理编译时产物，无法恢复仅存在于内存中的运行时执行状态和证据，存在分析盲区。

Method: 1) 解析Go内部结构重建类型和函数元数据；2) 恢复堆分配和静态字符串；3) 区分应用层函数；4) 通过ABI感知的逆向分析从调用点推导执行路径和参数值；5) 分析goroutine栈以识别活跃执行函数并恢复运行时参数值；6) 所有功能作为Volatility 3插件实现。

Result: 在BRICKSTORM后门、Obscura勒索软件和Pantegana RAT等真实恶意样本及开源样本上验证，成功恢复了C2端点、持久化机制、加密密钥、赎金笔记和执行状态，包括已发布威胁情报中缺失的关键运行时证据。

Conclusion: 提出的记忆取证框架有效填补了Go恶意软件运行时分析的技术空白，能够恢复静态分析无法获取的内存运行时状态，对Go恶意软件的取证调查具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: The Go programming language has become increasingly popular among malware developers due to its ability to produce statically linked, cross-platform executables that challenge traditional analysis techniques. These binaries embed a substantial runtime and compiler-generated metadata and are compiled with aggressive optimizations that discard type information for function parameters and local variables. Go's design further complicates analysis by representing strings as pointer-length pairs rather than null-terminated sequences, employing a caller-allocated stack model that obscures argument boundaries, and fragmenting program state across concurrent goroutines. Although existing static analysis and reverse engineering tools provide Go-specific support, they remain limited to compile-time artifacts and cannot recover runtime execution state and artifacts that persist solely in memory. To address this gap, we present the first memory forensics framework for runtime analysis of Go binaries. By parsing Go's internal structures, our framework reconstructs type and function metadata, recovers heap-allocated and static strings, and distinguishes application-level functions. Through ABI-aware backward analysis, it derives execution paths and argument values from call sites. To capture runtime state beyond what static analysis reveals, it analyzes goroutine stacks to identify actively executing functions and recover their runtime argument values. We implemented all capabilities as Volatility 3 plugins and evaluated them against malware seen in recent incidents, such as the BRICKSTORM backdoor, Obscura ransomware, and Pantegana RAT, as well as open-source samples for reproducibility. The framework successfully recovered C2 endpoints, persistence mechanisms, encryption keys, ransom notes, and execution state, including critical runtime artifacts that were absent from published threat intelligence.

</details>


### [83] [ExploitBench: A Capability Ladder Benchmark for LLM Cybersecurity Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14153)
*Seunghyun Lee,David Brumley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ExploitBench，一个将漏洞利用能力分解为16个可衡量标志的分级基准，用于评估LLM在V8引擎漏洞上的真实利用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全基准将崩溃视为利用成功，但实际利用是从触发bug到构建原语再到完全控制的分级过程。需要更细粒度的评估来测量模型真实利用能力。

Method: 设计16级能力标志（覆盖、崩溃、沙箱原语、任意读写、控制流劫持、任意代码执行）；使用确定性预言机（随机挑战-响应、差分执行、信号处理证明）；在41个V8漏洞上测试，设置三组实验：<模型,环境>、<模型,环境,自适应指导>、<模型,环境,工具>。

Result: 公开前沿模型能常规触发漏洞和崩溃，但无法实现任意代码执行；私有模型在约一半漏洞上实现了任意代码执行。表明针对加固目标的利用构造是新兴的前沿能力。

Conclusion: 漏洞利用并非二元事件，而是渐进能力阶梯；现有基准低估了利用难度；ExploitBench提供了更精确的评估框架；私有模型在利用能力上显著领先公开模型。

Abstract: Exploitation is not a binary event. It is a ladder of acquiring progressive capabilities, from executing a single buggy line of code to taking full control of the target. However, existing LLM security benchmarks treat a crash as exploitation success. That single binary outcome collapses the hard parts of exploitation: the transition from triggering a bug to constructing reusable primitives and control.
  We present ExploitBench, a capability-graded benchmark that decomposes exploitation into 16 measurable flags, from coverage and crash through sandbox primitives, arbitrary read/write, control-flow hijack, and arbitrary code execution. Each capability is verified by a deterministic oracle that uses a per-run randomized challenge-response for primitives, differential execution against ground-truth binaries to measure progress, and a signal-handler proof for code execution.
  We instantiate ExploitBench on 41 V8 bugs because V8 is both widely deployed and exploitation-hardened. We report three arms: <model,env> as the primary measurement of model-environment capability, <model,env, adaptive coaching> as a secondary arm that adds adaptive coaching to test whether targeted feedback shifts outcomes, and <model,env,harness> as an ablation that swaps in the model's native CLI to check whether vendor-side optimizations increase exploitation capabilities.
  Our results show a sharp capability split between publicly deployed frontier models and the private frontier. Across the 8 publicly deployed models tested, reaching the vulnerable code and triggering a crash is routine, but arbitrary code execution is not. The private model shows arbitrary code execution on approximately half. Overall, results suggest that exploit construction against hardened targets is an emerging frontier capability.

</details>


### [84] [DSTAN-Med: Dual-Channel Spatiotemporal Attention with Physiological Plausibility Filtering for False Data Injection Attack Detection in IoT-Based Medical Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14165)
*Md Mehedi Hasan,Rafiqul Islam,Md Zakir Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DSTAN-Med框架，通过双通道自注意力机制（DAM）分离传感器间空间相关性与时间依赖性，并引入生理合理性过滤器（PPF）抑制违反领域知识的FDI攻击。在三个IoMT数据集上，相比最强Transformer基线TranAD，灵敏度提升7.4-8.3个百分点（p<0.01），PPF独立贡献3.1-4.2个百分点精度增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法将传感器间空间相关性与时间依赖性混淆在共享隐空间中，无法解耦FDI攻击同时印刻的独特空间和时间特征；且没有方法利用领域知识约束输出以排除生理上不可能的攻击模式。

Method: 提出DSTAN-Med监督框架，包含：(1) 双通道自注意力机制（DAM），通过独立的传感器级（SWA）和时间级（TWA）自注意力路径在正交张量轴上处理多变量传感器窗口；(2) 残差1D-CNN模块用于局部时间特征提取；(3) 零参数的生理合理性过滤器（PPF），抑制违反领域知识界限的攻击特征。

Result: 在PhysioNet/CinC 2012、MIMIC-III Waveform和WESAD三个数据集上，相比最强Transformer基线TranAD，灵敏度提升7.4-8.3个百分点（p<0.01，McNemar检验，Holm-Bonferroni校正）；PPF独立贡献3.1-4.2个百分点精度增益且灵敏度损失极小；去除残差连接导致灵敏度下降14.0个百分点。

Conclusion: DSTAN-Med通过解耦空间和时间注意力路径并引入生理合理性先验，有效增强了IoMT系统对FDI攻击的检测能力，各组件均被消融实验证明不可或缺。

Abstract: False data injection (FDI) attacks on Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) sensor streams falsify vital signs in transit, threatening patient safety and defeating clinical monitoring systems that lack cyber-physical anomaly detection capability. Existing deep learning detectors conflate inter-sensor spatial correlations with temporal dependencies in a shared latent space, preventing disentanglement of the distinct spatial and temporal signatures that FDI attacks imprint simultaneously; no current method exploits domain knowledge to constrain outputs against physiologically impossible attack patterns. We propose DSTAN-Med, a supervised framework comprising a Dual-channel Attention Mechanism (DAM) that routes multivariate sensor windows through independent sensor-wise (SWA) and time-wise (TWA) self-attention pathways operating on orthogonal tensor axes, a residual 1D-CNN block for local temporal feature extraction, and a zero-parameter Physiological Plausibility Filter (PPF) that suppresses attack signatures violating domain-knowledge bounds. Evaluated across three IoMT sensor datasets - PhysioNet/CinC 2012 (ICU vital signs), MIMIC-III Waveform (continuous ICU waveforms), and WESAD (wearable biosensor signals) - DSTAN-Med achieves mean sensitivity gains of 7.4-8.3 percentage points over the strongest Transformer baseline (TranAD), with improvements significant at p < 0.01 (McNemar's test, Holm-Bonferroni correction). The PPF contributes independent precision gains of 3.1-4.2 percentage points at negligible sensitivity cost across all three corpora. Ablation studies confirm that each component is individually necessary; removal of residual connections alone reduces sensitivity by 14.0 percentage points. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/mehedi93hasan/DSTAN-MED.

</details>


### [85] [Web Agents Should Adopt the Plan-Then-Execute Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14290)
*Julien Piet,Annabella Chow,Yiwei Hou,Muxi Lyu,Sylvie Venuto,Jinhao Zhu,Raluca Ada Popa,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证ReAct（推理-行动循环）不适用于网页智能体，应改用"规划-执行"模式：在观察网页内容前先承诺一个任务专用程序，随后执行。原因是网页内容混合多方输入，ReAct模式下所有内容都影响模型决策，为提示注入提供直接路径。而"规划-执行"改变边界，不可信数据只能在预定义执行图内影响值或分支，不能重新定义用户任务或生成新操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct架构已成为LLM智能体的默认方案，但作者认为对网页智能体而言这是错误选择。网页内容混合了卖家列表、客户评论、赞助广告等多方输入，在ReAct模式下这些内容全部流入模型影响下一步决策，为提示注入攻击提供了直接路径。需要更安全的架构来隔离不可信内容对控制流的影响。

Method: 提出"规划-执行"范式：在观察运行时网页内容之前，先承诺一个任务专用程序（计划），然后执行该程序。通过分析WebArena基准测试，验证所有任务都与该范式兼容，其中80%可仅用纯程序化计划完成，无需运行时LLM子程序。同时识别出主要障碍：工具需要干净地映射到语义动作，且效果在执行前可知；而浏览器工具（点击、输入、滚动）具有页面依赖含义，规划层面是短视的。

Result: WebArena中所有任务都与plan-then-execute兼容，80%可仅用纯程序化计划完成。但当前网页基础设施缺乏类型化接口，浏览器工具（点击、输入、滚动）的页面依赖含义导致规划短视——智能体只能看到当前页面操作，后续操作只有在行动后才显现。

Conclusion: 网页任务默认不需要反应式（Reactive）架构。它们需要类型化、完整、可审计的网站API。这是基础设施问题，而非建模问题。缩小差距需要将网站交互从点击和按键转变为任务级操作的类型化接口。

Abstract: ReAct has become the default architecture across LLM agents, and many existing web agents follow this paradigm. We argue that it is the wrong default for web agents. Instead, web agents should default to plan-then-execute: commit to a task-specific program before observing runtime web content, then execute it. The reason is that web content mixes inputs from many parties. An e-commerce product page may combine a seller's listing, customer reviews and sponsored advertisements. Under ReAct, all of this content flows into the model when deciding on the next action, creating a direct path for prompt injections to steer the agent's control flow. Plan-then-execute changes this boundary: untrusted data may influence values or branches inside a predefined execution graph, but it cannot redefine the user task or cause the model to synthesize new actions at runtime. We analyze WebArena, a popular web agent benchmark, and find that all tasks are compatible with plan-then-execute, while 80% can be completed with a purely programmatic plan, without any runtime LLM subroutine. We identify the main barrier to adopting plan-then-execute on the web: For it to work well, tools must map cleanly to semantic actions, with effects known before execution, so agents have enough information to plan. The web does not naturally expose that interface. Browser tools such as click, type, and scroll have page-dependent meanings. Planning at this layer is near-sighted: the agent can only see actions on the current page, and later actions appear only after it acts.
