Closing this gap requires typed interfaces that turn website interactions from clicks and keystrokes to task-level operations. This is an infrastructure problem, not a modeling problem. Web tasks do not need reactivity by default; they need typed, complete, auditable website APIs.

</details>


### [86] [To See is Not to Learn: Protecting Multimodal Data from Unauthorized Fine-Tuning of Large Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14291)
*Chengshuai Zhao,Zhen Tan,Dawei Li,Zhiyuan Yu,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MMGuard是一种主动保护多模态数据免受未经授权LVLM微调的方法，通过注入人眼不可感知的扰动生成不可学习样本，利用跨模态绑定破坏和集成学习策略，在9个开源LVLM和6个数据集上实现了有效、隐蔽且鲁棒的保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LVLM数据盗用的防御方法（如机器遗忘、水印）均为事后补救措施，只能在知识产权侵权发生后生效，缺乏主动预防机制。

Method: MMGuard通过三个核心机制实现防御：(1) 注入人眼不可感知的扰动，最小化训练损失形成优化捷径，使模型过拟合噪声；(2) 跨模态绑定破坏策略，理论保证地强制噪声与训练目标间产生虚假关联；(3) 集成学习策略增强跨模型迁移能力。

Result: 在白盒、灰盒和黑盒威胁模型下，MMGuard在9个开源LVLM和6个数据集上均实现了有效、隐蔽且鲁棒的保护效果。

Conclusion: MMGuard建立了机制层面的优势，能够主动防御针对LVLM的激进微调攻击，为多模态数据版权和隐私保护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) is increasingly accompanied by unauthorized scraping and training on multimodal web data, posing severe copyright and privacy risks to data owners. Existing countermeasures, such as machine unlearning and watermarks, are inherent post-hoc approaches that act only after intellectual property infringement has already occurred. In this work, we propose MMGuard to empower data owners to proactively protect their multimodal data against unauthorized LVLM fine-tuning. MMGuard generates unlearnable examples by injecting human-imperceptible perturbations that actively exploit the learning dynamics of LVLMs. By minimizing the training loss, the perturbation creates an optimization shortcut, causing the model to overfit to the noise and thereby degrading downstream performance when the perturbation is absent during inference. To further strengthen this defense, MMGuard introduces a cross-modal binding disruption, strategically shifting LVLM attention to enforce a spurious correlation between the noise and the training target with theoretical guarantees. Enhanced by an ensemble learning strategy for cross-model transferability, MMGuard is evaluated against nine open-source LVLMs across six datasets. Our comprehensive results demonstrate effective, stealthy, and robust protection under white-box, gray-box, and black-box threat models, establishing a mechanistic advantage in proactively defending against aggressive fine-tuning exploitation.

</details>


### [87] [The Great Pretender: A Stochasticity Problem in LLM Jailbreak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14418)
*Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Cong Chen,Jonathan Petit*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了当前大语言模型越狱攻击研究中的核心问题：攻击成功率（ASR）指标不稳定且被系统性高估。研究发现，即使越狱提示词在单次评估中表现良好，多次运行时成功率可能下降高达30个百分点。作者提出新指标和新框架CAS-eval（评估）与CAS-gen（生成），前者用于稳定评估，后者帮助恢复越狱攻击损失的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱攻击方法的ASR指标不稳定、不可比、被系统性高估；成功的越狱提示词无法在目标模型上稳定复现性能。

Method: 系统研究了攻击评估和攻击生成过程中的随机性影响；提出新指标；设计CAS-eval评估框架和CAS-gen攻击生成框架。

Result: 越狱攻击在需要多次成功时ASR可下降高达30个百分点；CAS-gen帮助现有方法恢复该性能损失。

Conclusion: ASR作为LLM越狱攻击的主要指标不可靠；需要更稳定的评估方法和生成框架来保证攻击的鲁棒性和可重复性。

Abstract: "Oh-Oh, yes, I'm the great pretender. Pretending that I'm doing well. My need is such, I pretend too much..." summarizes the state in the area of jailbreak creation and evaluation. You find this method to generate adversarial attacks proposed by a reputable institution (e.g., BoN from Anthropic or Crescendo from Microsoft Research). However, this method does not deliver on the promise claimed in the paper despite having top ASR scores against industry-grade LLMs. You successfully generate the jailbreak prompts against your target (open) model. However, the generated jailbreak prompt works against the target model with a 50% consecutive success rate (5 out of 10 attempts) despite having an 80% ASR (on paper) on the latest closed-source model (with a guardrail system)! This observation leads us to think. First, Attack Success Rate (ASR), the primary metric for LLM jailbreak benchmarking, is not a stable quantity. Second, published ASR numbers are therefore systematically inflated and incomparable across papers. Therefore, we wonder "Why a successful jailbreak prompt does not perform consistently well against a target model on which the prompts have been optimized?". To answer this question, we study the impact of stochasticity not only during attack evaluation but also during attack generation. Our evaluation includes several jailbreak attacks, models (different sizes and providers), and judges. In addition, we propose a new metric and two new frameworks (CAS-eval and CAS-gen). Our evaluation framework, CAS-eval, shows that an attack can have an ASR drop of up to 30 percentage points when a jailbreak prompt needs to succeed on more than one attempt. Thankfully, our attack generation framework (CAS-gen) improves previous jailbreak methods and helps them recover this loss of 30 percentage points!

</details>


### [88] [Privacy Auditing with Zero (0) Training Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14591)
*Tudor Cebere,Mathieu Even,Linus Bleistein,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Zero-Run隐私审计框架，在不需要重新训练或控制数据插入的情况下，利用两个固定数据集（已知成员和非成员）对模型进行事后差分隐私审计，并通过因果推断方法校正分布偏移带来的混淆效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私审计方法需要对训练流程进行干预（如多次重新训练或随机化数据包含），这对于已部署的大规模系统（如基础模型）往往不可行。因此，亟需一种无需干预的、事后审计框架。

Method: 提出Zero-Run隐私审计框架，采用事后观察方式，利用两个固定数据集（已知成员和非成员）。借鉴因果推断思想，将分布偏移和算法泄露的联合效应建模为自适应组合（全局校正），或通过条件化观测数据调整逐点成员猜测（实例级校正），从而纠正混淆效应。

Result: 在合成数据和大型模型上的实验表明，Zero-Run审计能够在无法重新训练或控制数据插入的情况下，实现实用的隐私评估。

Conclusion: Zero-Run框架为大规模已部署模型提供了一种无需干预的、有效的事后差分隐私审计方法，弥补了现有方法在应用场景上的局限。

Abstract: Privacy auditing provides empirical lower bounds on the differential privacy parameters of learning algorithms. Existing methods, however, require interventional access to the training pipeline, either to retrain multiple times or to randomize data inclusion. This is often infeasible for large deployed systems such as foundation models. We introduce Zero-Run privacy auditing, a post-hoc framework for auditing models using two fixed datasets: examples known to be training-set members and examples known to be non-members. In this observational regime, membership is no longer randomized; instead, member and non-member data often differ in distribution, so membership inference scores may reflect a distribution shift rather than algorithmic leakage. Drawing on ideas from causal inference, we formalize this confounding effect and propose two complementary corrections that yield valid privacy audits. Our first approach models the combined effect of distribution shift and algorithmic leakage as an adaptive composition, producing conservative global corrections. Our second approach conditions on observed data and adjusts pointwise membership guesses, yielding sharper instance-dependent bounds. Experiments on synthetic data and large-scale models show that Zero-Run auditing enables practical privacy evaluation when retraining or controlled data insertion is infeasible.

</details>


### [89] [One Step to the Side: Why Defenses Against Malicious Finetuning Fail Under Adaptive Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14605)
*Itay Zloczower,Eyal Lenga,Gilad Gressel,Yisroel Mirsky*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文调查了15种近期针对大模型微调攻击的防御方法，发现这些防御存在共同弱点：它们只是遮蔽或误导通向有害行为的路径，而非真正移除有害行为本身。作者开发了一种统一的适应性攻击方法，成功突破了所有这些防御机制，表明当前方法无法提供可靠的安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型提供者发布开源权重或允许用户通过API微调基础模型，虽然模型在发布前经过安全对齐，但微调有害数据往往能移除这些安全防护。现有防御方法仅在固定攻击下评估，未考虑对抗性攻击，因此需要检验这些防御的真实鲁棒性。

Method: 调查了15种近期防御方法，识别出若干防御机制；然后开发了一种统一的适应性攻击方法，针对所有防御机制的共同弱点进行攻击。

Result: 所有被调查的防御方法均被统一的适应性攻击所突破，说明这些防御并未真正移除有害行为，而只是阻碍了它们原本设计针对的攻击。

Conclusion: 当前的防御方法无法提供可靠的安全保障，作者希望其提出的统一适应性攻击方法能帮助未来研究者和实践者在部署前对新防御进行压力测试。

Abstract: Model providers increasingly release open weights or allow users to fine-tune foundation models through APIs. Although these models are safety-aligned before release, their safeguards can often be removed by fine-tuning on harmful data. Recent defenses aim to make models robust to such malicious fine-tuning, but they are largely evaluated only against fixed attacks that do not account for the defense. We show that these robustness claims are incomplete. Surveying 15 recent defenses, we identify several defense mechanisms and show that they share a single weakness: they obscure or misdirect the path to harmful behavior without removing the behavior itself. We then develop a unified adaptive attack that breaks defenses across all defense mechanisms. Our results show that current approaches do not provide robust security; they mainly stop the attacks they were designed against. We hope that our unified adaptive adversary for this domain will help future researchers and practitioners stress-test new defenses before deployment.

</details>


### [90] [Known By Their Actions: Fingerprinting LLM Browser Agents via UI Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14786)
*William Lugoloobi,Samuelle Marro,Jabez Magomere,Joss Wright,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于LLM的智能体在浏览网页时，能否被网站通过被动JavaScript追踪器识别出其底层模型。实验表明，通过智能体的交互行为和时序特征，可以高达96% F1分数识别出14种前沿LLM模型，且分类器在不同模型规模和家族间具有泛化能力。注入随机时序延迟会降低分类性能，但无法提供鲁棒保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究网站能否被动识别驱动智能体的底层LLM模型，因为这种识别能力会带来严重安全风险，使攻击者能够针对已知模型漏洞发起定向攻击。

Method: 通过被动JavaScript追踪器捕获智能体的交互行为（action）和时序特征（interaction timings），训练分类器对14种前沿LLM模型进行识别；在信息检索和购物任务等四种网页环境中进行实验；评估分类器在不同模型大小和家族间的泛化能力、少量交互轨迹下的训练效果、以及注入随机时序延迟作为防御手段的有效性。

Result: 分类器识别底层模型的F1分数高达96%；分类器在不同模型大小和家族间具有泛化能力；少量交互轨迹即可训练出强分类器；智能体身份可在episode早期被推断；注入随机时序延迟会显著降低分类器性能，但重新训练后性能基本恢复。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的智能体在网页交互过程中，其底层的模型身份可以通过被动监控的方式被高精度识别，这构成了新的攻击面。简单的时序随机化防御手段不足以提供鲁棒保护，需要更有效的防护策略。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents increasingly browse the web on users' behalf, a natural question arises: can websites passively identify which underlying model powers an agent? Doing so would represent a significant security risk, enabling targeted attacks tailored to known model vulnerabilities. Across 14 frontier LLMs and four web environments spanning information retrieval and shopping tasks, we show that an agent's actions and interaction timings, captured via a passive JavaScript tracker, are sufficient to identify the underlying model with up to 96\% F1. We formalise this attack surface by demonstrating that classifiers trained on agent actions generalise across model sizes and families. We further show that strong classifiers can be trained from few interaction traces and that agent identity can be inferred early within an episode. Injecting randomised timing delays between actions substantially degrades classifier performance, but does not provide robust protection: a classifier retrained on delayed traces largely recovers performance. We release our harness and a labelled corpus of agent traces \href{https://github.com/KabakaWilliam/known_actions}{here}.

</details>


### [91] [Capacitive Touchscreens at Risk: A Practical Side-Channel Attack on Smartphones via Electromagnetic Emanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14633)
*Yukun Cheng,Changhai Ou,Shiyu Zhu,Jinyuan Zhang,Zhenfang Qiu,Xingshuo Han,Tianwei Zhang,Yuan Li,Shihui Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TESLA是一种利用电容触摸屏电磁辐射进行无接触侧信道攻击的新型方法，可从手机电磁泄漏中高精度恢复PIN码、键盘输入、应用类别和手写轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电容触摸屏侧信道攻击往往需要限制性条件或侵入式测量，缺乏一种在真实场景下可行、非接触且能全面恢复多种敏感信息的高效攻击手段。

Method: 将EM探头秘密放置于受害者设备附近，捕获触摸屏扫描过程中固有的电磁辐射，利用这些辐射信号编码了触摸交互的时空演化信息，形成一个统一的泄漏基础，从而提取多种敏感信息。

Result: 在iPhone X、小米10 Pro、三星S10和华为Mate 30 Pro上验证，PIN码识别率99.3%，键盘输入重建率97.6%，应用推断率95.0%，手写轨迹字符识别率76.8%，2D轨迹重建Jaccard指数0.74。

Conclusion: TESLA相较现有攻击具有更广泛的攻击目标、更高效的样本采集以及在真实攻击场景中的可操作性，对现代智能手机安全构成严重威胁。

Abstract: Capacitive touchscreens in modern smartphones introduce severe side-channel vulnerabilities. However, existing attacks often require restrictive conditions or invasive measurements. This paper presents TESLA, a novel, contactless electromagnetic (EM) side-channel attack that exploits inherent EM emanations during touchscreen scanning. We demonstrate that these emanations encode the spatiotemporal evolution of touch interactions, forming a unified leakage basis. By secretly placing an EM probe near the victim's device, TESLA enables attackers to extract highly sensitive information, including screen-unlocking PIN codes, keyboard inputs, interacting application categories, and continuous handwriting trajectories. Compared to existing attacks, TESLA offers a broader range of attack targets, more efficient sample acquisition, and operations in practical attack scenarios. Extensive evaluations on popular commercial smartphones, specifically the iPhone X, Xiaomi 10 Pro, Samsung S10, and Huawei Mate 30 Pro, validate the effectiveness of TESLA. It achieves remarkable inference accuracy in diverse settings such as private meeting rooms and public libraries, with success rates of 99.3% for PIN code recognition, 97.6% for keyboard input reconstruction, and 95.0% for application inference, respectively. Simultaneously, it attains a 76.8% character recognition accuracy and a high geometric similarity (Jaccard index of 0.74) for 2D handwriting trajectory reconstruction.

</details>


### [92] [Adapting AlphaEvolve to Optimize Fully Homomorphic Encryption on TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14718)
*Shruthi Gorantala,Jianming Tong,Asra Ali,Baiyu Li,Jonathan Katz,Jeremy Kun,Thomas Steinke,Abhradeep Thakurta,Julian Walker,Amir Yazdanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaEvolve，利用LLM驱动的进化搜索自动探索FHE在TPU上的硬件感知优化，在24小时内实现了TFHE bootstrap延迟提升2.5倍、CKKS旋转和乘法延迟分别提升1.31倍和1.18倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHE部署因计算开销大而受阻，专用硬件加速器（如TPU）虽能缓解但映射复杂密码学内核困难，现有编译器栈难以充分利用底层硬件，开发者需手动试错导致资源碎片化和利用率低下。

Method: 将优化建模为进化搜索问题，利用AlphaEvolve闭环系统结合LLM驱动代码生成，通过真实硬件执行反馈和正确性测试指导进化过程，自动探索硬件感知的密码学内核优化。

Result: 在Google Cloud TPUv5e上，TFHE bootstrap延迟提升2.5倍，CKKS旋转和乘法延迟分别提升1.31倍和1.18倍。

Conclusion: AlphaEvolve能够帮助研究人员在密码学、编译器和硬件加速器之间导航优化权衡，自动化探索过程显著优于人工工程实现。

Abstract: The deployment of Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) at scale is hindered due to its heavy computational overhead. While specialized hardware accelerators like Google Tensor Processing Units (TPUs) can help, mapping complex cryptographic kernels onto such architectures remains a challenge. Efficient execution requires co-optimization between the systolic array-based Matrix Multiplication Unit (MXU) and Vector Processing Units (VPUs), as well as the orchestration of data movement across the vector register files. Existing compiler stacks often abstract low-level hardware utilization, requiring developers to adopt a manual trial-and-error process that often results in fragmented execution and underutilized resources. To accelerate this development process, we use AlphaEvolve to automate the exploration of hardware-aware cryptographic-kernel optimizations. We frame optimization as an evolutionary search problem, utilizing the closed-loop system provided by AlphaEvolve, that leverages LLM-driven code generation. We use real-world feedback from hardware execution and rigorous correctness testing to guide the evolution process. We evaluate AlphaEvolve optimization on primitives for both the TFHE (Jaxite) and CKKS (CROSS) FHE schemes on Google Cloud TPUv5e, a contemporary TPU architecture. Within 24 hours of automated exploration, AlphaEvolve discovered implementation-level optimizations that improve TFHE bootstrap latency by 2.5x and CKKS rotation and multiplication latency by 1.31x and 1.18x, respectively, relative to human-engineered state of the art. These results demonstrate that AlphaEvolve can be used to enable researchers to navigate the optimization trade-offs between cryptography, compilers, and hardware accelerators.

</details>


### [93] [EVA: Editing for Versatile Alignment against Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14750)
*Yi Wang,Hongye Qiu,Yue Xu,Sibei Yang,Zhan Qin,Minlie Huang,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EVA是一个通过直接模型编辑实现安全对齐的框架，它通过精确识别和编辑对有害指令敏感的神经元来防御越狱攻击，在保持模型通用推理能力的同时有效消除有害行为，并在LLMs和VLMs上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全防御方法（安全微调或外部过滤器）存在计算开销大、安全性-效用性权衡等问题，会降低模型在良性任务上的性能，亟需一种更精确高效的解决方案。

Method: 将安全对齐重构为精确的知识修正任务，通过定位并手术式编辑对有害指令敏感的特定神经元来中和有害行为，同时保持大部分模型参数不变。

Result: 在LLMs和VLMs上，EVA在缓解越狱攻击方面均优于基线方法，且不损害模型的通用推理能力。

Conclusion: EVA提供了一种精确且高效的部署后安全对齐方案，能够有效平衡安全性与实用性，是模型编辑在安全对齐领域的开创性应用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities but remain vulnerable to jailbreaking attacks, where adversaries exploit textual or visual triggers to bypass safety guardrails. Recent defenses typically rely on safety fine-tuning or external filters to reduce the model's likelihood of producing harmful content. While effective to some extent, these methods often incur significant computational overheads and suffer from the safety utility trade-off, degrading the model's performance on benign tasks. To address these challenges, we propose EVA (Editing for Versatile Alignment against Jailbreaks), a novel framework that pioneers the application of direct model editing for safety alignment. EVA reframes safety alignment as a precise knowledge correction task. Instead of retraining massive parameters, EVA identifies and surgically edits specific neurons responsible for the model's susceptibility to harmful instructions, while leaving the vast majority of the model unchanged. By localizing the updates, EVA effectively neutralizes harmful behaviors without compromising the model's general reasoning capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EVA outperforms baselines in mitigating jailbreaks across both LLMs and VLMs, offering a precise and efficient solution for post-deployment safety alignment.

</details>


### [94] [Do Coding Agents Understand Least-Privilege Authorization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14859)
*Zheng Yan,Jingxiang Weng,Charles Chen,Dengyun Peng,Ethan Qin,Jiannan Guan,Jinhao Liu,Qiming Yu,Yixin Yuan,Fanqing Meng,Carl Che,Mengkang Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出权限边界推理任务和AuthBench基准，发现现有模型在最小权限授权上存在固有缺陷，并提出充分性-紧致性分解方法，通过先模拟任务生成覆盖策略再审计权限，显著提升安全性与效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在访问shell、仓库和用户文件时，需要最小权限授权以确保安全部署，但目前模型无法自主推断任务所需的权限边界，导致要么权限不足无法完成任务，要么过度授权暴露敏感面。

Method: 提出Sufficiency-Tightness Decomposition（充分性-紧致性分解）方法：先通过前向模拟任务生成覆盖导向的策略（确保所有必要权限），再审计每个授权条目是否具有充分依据且不涉及敏感资源。

Result: 在AuthBench的120个真实终端任务上，该方法对紧致性偏好的模型在敏感任务成功率上提升最高达15.8%，并在所有评估模型上降低了攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 直接生成权限策略是瓶颈所在，因为单次生成需同时发现所有必要权限并拒绝所有非必要权限；分解策略可有效缓解这一矛盾。模型存在特定"授权吸引子"，增加推理时间只会让模型在其固有失败模式上更加一致。

Abstract: As coding agents gain access to shells, repositories, and user files, least-privilege authorization becomes a prerequisite for safe deployment: an agent should receive enough authority to complete the task, without unnecessary authority that exposes sensitive surfaces.To study whether current models can infer this boundary themselves, we first introduce permission-boundary inference, where a model maps a task instruction and terminal environment to a file-level read/write/execute policy, and AuthBench, a benchmark of 120 realistic terminal tasks with human-reviewed permission labels and executable validators for utility and attack outcomes.AuthBench shows that authorization is not a simple conservative-versus-permissive calibration problem: frontier models often omit permissions required by the execution chain while also granting unused or sensitive accesses.Increasing inference-time reasoning does not resolve this mismatch. Instead, each model moves toward a model-specific authorization attractor: more reasoning makes it more consistent in its own failure mode, whether broad-but-exposed or tight-but-brittle.This suggests that direct policy generation is the bottleneck, because a single generation must both discover all necessary accesses and reject all unnecessary ones.We therefore propose Sufficiency-Tightness Decomposition, which first generates a coverage-oriented policy by forward-simulating the task and then audits each granted entry for grounding and sensitivity.Across tested models, this decomposition improves sensitive-task success by up to 15.8% on tightness-biased models while reducing attack success across all evaluated models.

</details>


### [95] [Toward Securing AI Agents Like Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14932)
*Lukas Pirch,Micha Horlboge,Patrick Großmann,Syeda Mahnur Asif,Klim Kireev,Thorsten Holz,Konrad Rieck*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从操作系统安全视角审视基于LLM的自主智能体安全，通过统一架构分析和案例研究发现现有智能体存在严重安全漏洞，但许多问题可通过操作系统安全技术缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自主智能体（如OpenClaw）通过工具使用和第三方技能扩展能力的同时，引入了重大安全风险，需要系统性地研究其安全问题。

Method: 以操作系统安全视角，调研开源智能体现状，推导统一智能体架构，系统分析潜在攻击向量，并对四个OpenClaw类智能体进行案例研究评估。

Result: 即使攻击者能力有限，现有智能体的多种保护机制在实践中失效；安全运行需要详细的系统知识和仔细配置；部分智能体能力在设计上就不安全。

Conclusion: 许多漏洞可通过操作系统安全领域成熟技术缓解，并提出了智能体系统安全设计建议。

Abstract: Autonomous agents based on large language models (LLMs) are rapidly emerging as a general-purpose technology, with recent systems such as OpenClaw extending their capabilities through broad tool use, third-party skills, and deeper integration into user environments. At the same time, these agentic systems introduce substantial security risks by combining unconstrained capabilities with access to sensitive user data. In this work, we investigate the security of LLM-based agents through the lens of operating systems. We argue that both face strikingly similar challenges in isolating resources, separating privileges, and mediating communication.
  Guided by this perspective, we survey the current landscape of open-source agents, derive a unified agent architecture, and systematically analyze potential attack vectors. To validate this analysis, we conduct a case study evaluating four widely used OpenClaw-like agents. Even under modest attacker capabilities, we find that several protection mechanisms fail in practice and that secure operation requires detailed system knowledge and careful configuration. However, we also observe that while some agentic capabilities remain insecure by design, many vulnerabilities can be mitigated using well-established techniques from operating system security. We conclude with a set of recommendations for the secure design of agentic systems.

</details>


### [96] [PickleFuzzer: A Case Study in Fuzzing for Discrepancies Between Python Pickle Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15084)
*Justin Applegate,Andreas Kellas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PickleFuzzer，一种基于生成的模糊测试工具，用于发现Python不同pickle实现之间的不一致性。该工具通过语法生成pickle对象并比较各实现的行为差异，成功检测到14个新不一致性，其中4个可绕过Hugging Face等平台的安全扫描工具，获得了$750漏洞奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python的pickle序列化协议虽然强大但不安全，现有防御（限制导入、静态/动态分析）依赖对PVM操作码的准确解释，而Python三个原生PVM模块之间存在不一致性，可能导致恶意载荷被错误检测，削弱现有防御。

Method: 提出PickleFuzzer，一种基于生成（generation-based）的模糊测试工具。它使用自定义语法生成pickle对象，将生成的pickle对象传递给各pickle实现，比较执行过程中抛出的异常或关键内部状态的变化。由于缺乏完整的pickle规范，作者开发了相应语法；通过比较各实现的行为差异来判断不一致性，无需规范导出的oracle。

Result: 检测到14个新不一致性，其中4个是关键性的，可被用于绕过Hugging Face等模型托管平台部署的安全扫描工具。相关发现已报告给Python软件基金会，安全漏洞向漏洞赏金平台报告并获得$750奖金。

Conclusion: 差异测试（differential testing）是识别重要pickle实现中安全相关不一致性的可行方法，该工作可为未来通过更定向的模糊测试发现更深层pickle漏洞提供有前景的方向。

Abstract: Python's native serialization protocol, pickle, is a powerful but insecure format for transferring untrusted data. It is frequently used, especially for saving machine learning models, despite known security challenges. While developers sometimes mitigate this risk by restricting imports during unpickling or using static and dynamic analysis tools, these approaches are error-prone and depend heavily on accurate interpretations of the Pickle Virtual Machine (PVM) opcodes. Discrepancies across Python's three native PVM modules can lead to incorrect detection of malicious payloads and undermine existing defenses.
  To efficiently and scalably identify discrepancies, we present PickleFuzzer, a custom generation-based fuzzer that identifies inconsistencies across pickle implementations. PickleFuzzer generates pickle objects, passes them to each implementation, and detects differences in thrown exceptions or changes to key internal states. It generates pickle objects using a grammar, which we developed to account for the missing pickle specification. It determines discrepancies by comparing the execution behaviors of each test implementation, rather than requiring a specification-derived oracle. PickleFuzzer detected 14 new discrepancies between the pickle implementations. Four discrepancies are critical and can be used to bypass security-critical scanning tools like those deployed on the popular model hosting platform, Hugging Face. We disclosed all findings to the Python Software Foundation for remediation, and additionally disclosed the security issues to a bug bounty platform and were awarded a $750 bounty. We demonstrate that differential testing is a viable approach for identifying security-relevant discrepancies in important pickle implementations, and our work can lead to promising future directions for finding deeper pickle bugs with more directed fuzzing.

</details>


### [97] [Talk is (Not) Cheap: A Taxonomy and Benchmark Coverage Audit for LLM Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15118)
*Karthik Raghu Iyer,Yazdan Jamshidi,Nicholas Bray,Alexey A. Shvets*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于STRIDE模型的4×6目标-技术矩阵审计框架，用于评估LLM攻击基准的覆盖率。通过对932篇安全研究论文的507叶分类法分析，发现主流基准（HarmBench、InjecAgent、AgentDojo）仅覆盖矩阵最多25%的区域，服务中断和模型内部等威胁类别缺乏标准化评估，且存在严重的命名碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM攻击基准各自独立评估，缺乏统一框架来审计它们是否共同覆盖了威胁面，导致某些威胁类别被系统性忽略。

Method: 构建了基于STRIDE的4×6目标-技术矩阵，从932篇arXiv安全研究（2023-2026）中提取507叶分类法（401个数据填充+106个威胁模型推导），对六个公开基准进行覆盖审计。

Result: 三大主流基准（HarmBench、InjecAgent、AgentDojo）占据互不重叠的单元格，最多覆盖矩阵25%；服务中断和模型内部威胁类别缺乏标准化评估，尽管已发表攻击实现46×令牌放大和96%攻击成功率；攻击命名存在高达29种表面形式的碎片化问题，主要集中于安全与对齐绕过。

Conclusion: 提出可扩展的分类法、攻击记录和覆盖映射，使新基准可映射到同一矩阵，帮助社区追踪评估缺口是否正在缩小。

Abstract: We introduce a reusable framework for auditing whether LLM attack benchmarks collectively cover the threat surface: a 4$\times$6 Target $\times$ Technique matrix grounded in STRIDE, constructed from a 507-leaf taxonomy -- 401 data-populated and 106 threat-model-derived leaves -- of inference-time attacks extracted from 932 arXiv security studies (2023--2026). The matrix enables benchmark-external validation -- auditing collective coverage rather than individual benchmark consistency. Applying it to six public benchmarks reveals that the three primary frameworks (HarmBench, InjecAgent, AgentDojo) occupy non-overlapping cells covering at most 25\% of the matrix, while entire STRIDE threat categories (Service Disruption, Model Internals) lack any standardized evaluation, despite published attacks in these categories achieving 46$\times$ token amplification and 96\% attack success rates through mechanisms which no benchmark tests. The corpus of 2,521 unique attack groups further reveals pervasive naming fragmentation (up to 29 surface forms for a single attack) and heavy concentration in Safety \& Alignment Bypass, structural properties invisible at smaller scale. The taxonomy, attack records, and coverage mappings are released as extensible artifacts; as new benchmarks emerge, they can be mapped onto the same matrix, enabling the community to track whether evaluation gaps are closing.

</details>


### [98] [MetaBackdoor: Exploiting Positional Encoding as a Backdoor Attack Surface in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15172)
*Rui Wen,Mark Russinovich,Andrew Paverd,Jun Sakuma,Ahmed Salem*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaBackdoor，一种利用位置信息（如序列长度）而非文本内容作为触发条件的后门攻击方法。实验表明，该攻击可在语义清洁的输入上激活后门，泄露系统提示、在多轮对话中自我触发恶意工具调用，并能与基于内容的后门组合使用，更难被检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后门攻击主要依赖内容触发器（如特定词语），需要显式修改输入文本。作者认为这一假设不必要且限制了威胁模型的广度，因此探索利用Transformer固有的位置编码作为非内容触发信号的可能性。

Method: 利用Transformer架构中位置编码的必要性，设计基于序列长度的位置触发器。后门在输入长度满足特定条件时自动激活，无需修改文本内容。该方法可单独使用，也可与内容后门组合形成更精确的激活条件。

Result: 1）简单长度位置触发器即可激活后门；2）能诱导模型泄露系统提示等内部敏感信息；3）多轮对话可自然进入触发区域，实现自我激活并执行恶意工具调用；4）与内容后门正交，可组合使用增强隐蔽性。

Conclusion: 揭示位置编码是此前被忽视的攻击面，LLM后门的威胁模型需要重新审视。现有基于文本检测的防御措施不足以防御此类攻击，亟需针对位置触发器的新的防御策略。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks pose a serious security threat to large language models (LLMs), which are increasingly deployed as general-purpose assistants in safety- and privacy-critical applications. Existing LLM backdoors rely primarily on content-based triggers, requiring explicit modification of the input text. In this work, we show that this assumption is unnecessary and limiting. We introduce MetaBackdoor, a new class of backdoor attacks that exploits positional information as the trigger, without modifying textual content. Our key insight is that Transformer-based LLMs necessarily encode token positions to process ordered sequences. As a result, length-correlated positional structure is reflected in the model's internal computation and can be used as an effective non-content trigger signal.
  We demonstrate that even a simple length-based positional trigger is sufficient to activate stealthy backdoors. Unlike prior attacks, MetaBackdoor operates on visibly and semantically clean inputs and enables qualitatively new capabilities. We show that a backdoored LLM can be induced to disclose sensitive internal information, including proprietary system prompts, once a length condition is satisfied. We further demonstrate a self-activation scenario, where normal multi-turn interaction can move the conversation context into the trigger region and induce malicious tool-call behavior without attacker-supplied trigger text. In addition, MetaBackdoor is orthogonal to content-based backdoors and can be composed with them to create more precise and harder-to-detect activation conditions.
  Our results expand the threat model of LLM backdoors by revealing positional encoding as a previously overlooked attack surface. This challenges defenses that focus on detecting suspicious text and highlights the need for new defense strategies that explicitly account for positional triggers in modern LLM architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Vision-Based Runtime Monitoring under Varying Specifications using Semantic Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13923)
*Bardh Hoxha,Oliver Schön,Hideki Okamoto,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在部分可观测条件下，基于视觉观测对过去时间信号时态逻辑(ptSTL)进行认证运行时监测。提出可重用的监测器，一次性训练和校准后即可认证目标片段中的任意公式，并引入语义基和滚动预测两种监测器。在行人过街基准和Waymo真实驾驶数据上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的认证运行时监测方法通常需要针对每个公式重新训练，缺乏可重用性。同时，在部分可观测的视觉环境下，如何为时态逻辑公式提供有限样本保证是一个挑战。

Method: 1) 提出语义基方法，证明原子鲁棒性分数向量是单调、1-Lipschitz可重用接口的最小预测目标，通过语法树的确定性解码器评估任意公式，单次保形校准即可认证整个片段。2) 提出滚动预测监测器，仅预测当前谓词值并在线重建时态历史。3) 在行人过街基准和Waymo驾驶数据上验证。

Result: 滚动预测监测器在短时域上获得更紧的认证界，而语义基监测器在长时域上比滚动预测监测器紧4倍。在Waymo数据上，两种监测器均满足保形覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 语义基方法为ptSTL公式的认证监测提供了可重用的高效方案，在不同时域长度下两种监测器各有优势，均能提供有限样本的统计保证。

Abstract: We study certified runtime monitoring of past-time signal temporal logic (ptSTL) from visual observations under partial observability. The monitor must infer safety-relevant quantities from images and provide finite-sample guarantees, while being \emph{reusable}: once trained and calibrated, it should certify any formula in a target fragment without per-formula retraining. For fragments induced by a finite dictionary of temporal atoms, we prove that the \emph{semantic basis}, the vector of atom robustness scores, is the minimum prediction target within the class of monotone, 1-Lipschitz reusable interfaces: any formula is evaluated by a deterministic decoder derived from the parse tree, and a single conformal calibration pass certifies the entire fragment with no union bound. We also introduce a \emph{rolling prediction monitor} that predicts only current predicate values and reconstructs temporal history online; this is easier to learn but grows conservative at long horizons. On a pedestrian-crossroad benchmark, rolling achieves tighter certified bounds at short horizons while the semantic-basis monitor is up to 4-times tighter at long horizons. We validate the presented monitors on real-world Waymo driving data, where both monitors satisfy the conformal coverage guarantee empirically.

</details>


### [100] [Forgetting That Sticks: Quantization-Permanent Unlearning via Circuit Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15138)
*Saisab Sadhu,Pratinav Seth,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有研究忽视了大模型实际部署前会被量化压缩的事实。本文发现，基于梯度的机器遗忘在4-bit量化后会失效，而能够抵抗量化的方法几乎不改变模型。作者提出MANSU方法，通过因果电路归因、零空间投影和参数幅度保证，首次同时实现了有意义遗忘、保留集保持、非正量化差距和结构性擦除四个目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准遗忘评估仅在全精度、训练后立即进行，但所有部署的语言模型都经过量化，现有方法在量化环境下表现出的系统性双重失效未被充分认识。

Method: MANSU包含三个组件：(1) 因果电路归因定位最小遗忘子图；(2) 电路限制的零空间投影结合对角Fisher保留约束；(3) 每参数幅度下限保证量化后参数不变。

Result: MANSU首次同时满足所有四个性质（有意义遗忘、保留集保持、非正PTQ差距、结构性擦除），而梯度方法在压缩后准确率回升高达+0.05。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了遗忘-量化之间的根本矛盾（参数更新远小于量化bin宽度），并提供了理论形式化（稀疏性-持久性权衡）和实际解决方案。

Abstract: Standard unlearning evaluations measure behavioral suppression in full precision, immediately after training, despite every deployed language model being quantized first. Recent work has shown that 4-bit post-training quantization can reverse machine unlearning; we show this is not a tuning artefact but a systematic dual failure: gradient-based methods that achieve meaningful forgetting lose it under compression, while methods that survive quantization barely change the model. Both failures trace to the same root cause: across all baselines, per-parameter updates lie 47-828x below the NF4 quantization bin width; updates diffused across billions of parameters cannot clear quantization bin boundaries, a consequence we formalize as a sparsity-permanence tradeoff. We present MANSU (Mechanistic-Aligned Null-Space Unlearning), which resolves both modes by combining causal circuit attribution to isolate the minimal forget-set subgraph, circuit-restricted null-space projection with a diagonal-Fisher retain bound, and a per-parameter magnitude floor guaranteeing quantization survival by construction. We additionally introduce Circuit Attribution Divergence (CAD), a mechanistic verification metric distinguishing structural erasure from behavioral suppression, a distinction existing metrics cannot make. Across multiple model families and hazard benchmarks, MANSU is the first method to jointly satisfy all four properties with margin on each (meaningful forgetting, retain preservation, non-positive PTQ gap, and structural erasure), while gradient-based baselines recover up to +0.05 accuracy under compression.

</details>


### [101] [PreFT: Prefill-only finetuning for efficient inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14217)
*Andrew Lanpouthakoun,Aryaman Arora,Zhengxuan Wu,Dhruv Pai,Ben Keigwin,Dan Jurafsky,Christopher Potts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreFT（仅预填充微调），仅在预填充阶段应用适配器层，在解码阶段丢弃，相比传统PEFT在多用户个性化服务场景下实现1.9倍吞吐量提升，且通过增加秩可补偿性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的参数高效微调方法（PEFT）在服务多用户个性化模型时，预填充（prefill）和解码（decode）阶段存在吞吐量不匹配问题：解码阶段处理多个适配器时吞吐量远低于预填充阶段，导致整体服务效率低下。

Method: 提出PreFT（Prefill-only Finetuning），仅在预填充阶段对输入token应用适配器（LoRA/ReFT），解码生成阶段则丢弃适配器，使用基础模型参数。在vLLM推理引擎上实现了高效的多适配器服务方案。

Result: 在Llama 3.1 70B服务512个适配器时，PreFT吞吐量达到传统PEFT的1.9倍。在SFT任务中，PreFT的评估损失略高于PEFT，但可通过增加秩补偿且几乎不降低吞吐量。在RL任务中，PreFT性能与标准PEFT接近。

Conclusion: PreFT在准确率-吞吐量的权衡上优于现有PEFT方法，为大规模个性化LLM服务提供了更高效的选择。

Abstract: Large language models can now be personalised efficiently at scale using parameter efficient finetuning methods (PEFTs), but serving user-specific PEFTs harms throughput, even with specialised kernels and memory management techniques. This is because, theoretically and empirically, a mismatch exists between prefill (processing a large number of tokens at once) and decode (generating a single token autoregressively): the latter has far lower throughput when serving multiple adapters. Rather than optimising performance relative to parameter count, for efficient multi-adapter serving, we instead ought to optimise performance relative to serving throughput. We therefore propose PreFT (Prefill-only Finetuning), wherein we only apply the adapter to prefill tokens and discard it afterwards. PreFT significantly increases throughput with minimal effect on performance. We develop and release an efficient implementation of two prefill-only PEFTs, LoRA and ReFT, on the vLLM inference engine. We first show that serving multi-user PreFTs is more efficient than traditional PEFTs ($1.9\times$ the throughput when serving $512$ adapters on Llama 3.1 70B). Then, we compare the performance of prefill-only vs. all-token adapters on a variety of supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning tasks with LMs at varying scales. On SFT, we observe that the evaluation loss of PreFTs is higher than PEFTs, but can be compensated by increasing rank with nearly no reduction in throughput. On RL, we consistently find that PreFTs approach parity with standard PEFTs. Together, this work validates prefill-only adaptation of LLMs as a more favourable accuracy-throughput tradeoff than existing PEFTs for personalised serving.

</details>


### [102] [CSI-JEPA: Towards Foundation Representations for Ubiquitous Sensing with Minimal Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14171)
*Xuanhao Luo,Zhizhen Li,Yuchen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CSI-JEPA，一种自监督预测表示学习框架，用于标签高效的Wi-Fi感知。它从未标注的CSI样本中学习可复用的时频谱表示，通过预测掩码信道区域的潜在特征实现。在7个真实世界Wi-Fi感知任务上，相比最先进的监督Transformer模型，平均准确率提升高达10.64个百分点，在匹配预算下最多节省98.0%的标签数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CSI感知模型依赖特定任务的监督训练，需要大量标注数据，且泛化能力差，难以在实际部署中扩展。虽然未标注的CSI数据丰富，但标注数据收集成本高昂。

Method: CSI-JEPA采用自监督预测表示学习，将CSI信道响应幅度窗口沿时间和子载波维度进行标记化，提出信道变化感知掩码策略，从局部时域和子载波域变化更强的区域采样预测目标。预训练后冻结编码器作为骨干，添加轻量级任务专用适配器用于下游感知任务。

Result: 在7个真实世界Wi-Fi感知任务上，CSI-JEPA相比最先进的监督Transformer模型，平均准确率提升高达10.64个百分点，在匹配预算下最多节省98.0%的标签数据。

Conclusion: CSI-JEPA通过自监督预测表示学习有效解决了CSI感知中标签稀缺和跨任务泛化问题，显著提升了多任务Wi-Fi感知的性能和数据效率。

Abstract: Channel state information (CSI) provides a widely available sensing modality for human and environment perception, but existing CSI sensing models usually rely on task-specific supervised training and require substantial labeled data for each task, device, user, or environment. This limits their scalability in practical deployments where unlabeled CSI is abundant but labeled data is costly to collect. In this paper, we present CSI-JEPA, a self-supervised predictive representation learning framework for label-efficient, multi-task Wi-Fi sensing. CSI-JEPA learns reusable temporal-spectral representations from unlabeled CSI samples by predicting latent features of masked channel regions from visible context. To better match the physical structure of CSI, CSI-JEPA tokenizes channel-response amplitude windows along the time and subcarrier dimensions. It then introduces a channel variation-aware masking strategy that samples predictive targets from regions with stronger local temporal and subcarrier-domain variations. After pretraining, the encoder is frozen and used as a backbone, with lightweight task-specific adapters added for downstream sensing tasks. We evaluate CSI-JEPA on seven real-world Wi-Fi sensing tasks spanning diverse objectives and deployment settings. The results show that CSI-JEPA improves downstream sensing performance over competitive baselines, achieving up to 10.64 percentage points mean accuracy gain over state-of-the-art supervised Transformer and matched-budget label savings of up to 98.0%.

</details>


### [103] [Quantum Advantage in Multi Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14235)
*Simranjeet Singh Dahia,Claudia Szabo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对量子多智能体强化学习（QMARL）中的量子纠缠在智能体协作中的作用进行了实证评估。在CHSH游戏中，纠缠的QMARL智能体达到0.854胜率（接近Tsirelson极限），超越经典上限0.75；在CoopNav任务中，无纠缠的QMARL仍比经典方法提升约2倍成功率（0.85 vs 0.40），且混合架构（量子actor+经典critic）表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QMARL研究缺乏可证明的基线，无法严格区分量子优势与算法巧合，亟需通过有理论界限的任务（如CHSH游戏）来量化验证量子纠缠带来的真实协作增益。

Method: 采用去中心化QMARL框架，使用变分量子电路（VQC）作为actor，共享纠缠态；在CHSH游戏和CoopNav两个任务上进行实验，对比纠缠/无纠缠量子电路、经典MAA2C及混合架构（量子actor+经典centralized critic）。

Result: 1) CHSH游戏中，纠缠QMARL达到0.854胜率（Tsirelson极限），无纠缠量子电路仅为0.75（匹配经典基线）；2) 不同Bell态对协作效果影响差异显著；3) CoopNav中无纠缠QMARL成功率0.85，经典MAA2C约0.40；4) 混合架构（量子actor+经典critic）优于纯经典和纯量子方案。

Conclusion: 明确验证了量子纠缠是QMARL中实现量子优势的核心机制，而非量子电路本身；混合架构展现出实用潜力；未来需进一步探索纠缠结构设计与可扩展性。

Abstract: We present an empirical evaluation of quantum entanglement in agent coordination within quantum multi agent reinforcement learning (QMARL). While QMARL has attracted growing interest recently, most prior work evaluates quantum policies without provable baselines, making it impossible to rigorously distinguish quantum advantage from algorithmic coincidence. We address this directly by evaluating a decentralized QMARL framework with variational quantum circuit (VQC) actors with shared entangled states. In the CHSH game, which has a mathematically proven classical performance ceiling of 0.75 win rate, we show that entangled QMARL agents approach the Tsirelson limit of 0.854, providing clear evidence of their quantum advantage. We show that unentangled quantum circuits match the classical baseline, confirming that entanglement and not the quantum circuit itself is the active coordination mechanism. We also explore the effect of specific entanglement structures, as some Bell states enable coordination gains while others actively harm performance. On cooperative navigation (CoopNav), QMARL without entanglement achieves $\sim2\times$ improvement in success rate over classical MAA2C ($\sim$0.85 versus $\sim$0.40), with the hybrid configuration, quantum actor paired with a classical centralised critic, outperforming both fully classical and fully quantum solutions. We present our experimental analysis and discuss future work.

</details>


### [104] [Data-Augmented Game Starts for Accelerating Self-Play Exploration in Imperfect Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14379)
*JB Lanier,Nathan Monette,Pierre Baldi,Roy Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DAGS（数据增强游戏起始状态采样策略），利用离线人类演示数据初始化强化学习的状态分布，以加速双人零和博弈中正则化策略梯度方法的探索，并在多个博弈环境中验证了其在固定计算预算下降低可剥削性的效果，同时提出了多任务观测标志以缓解状态分布偏移导致的均衡偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模不完美信息博弈（如星际争霸、Dota、CS）因稀疏奖励和长时域探索而难以计算近似均衡，现有方法面临计算不可行的挑战。

Method: 提出Data-Augmented Game Starts (DAGS)，从离线人类演示中采样中间状态作为强化学习数据收集的初始状态，结合正则化策略梯度方法，并引入多任务观测标志来校正均衡偏差。

Result: 在合成数据集、Kuhn扑克、Goofspiel及反例博弈上的实验表明，DAGS在固定计算预算下显著降低了可剥削性，尤其在长时域探索困难的环境中效果突出。

Conclusion: DAGS能有效加速不完美信息博弈中的均衡求解，但需注意起始状态分布偏移可能导致均衡偏差；多任务观测标志是一种可行的修正手段。同时，论文发布了新的基准环境以推动相关研究。

Abstract: Finding approximate equilibria for large-scale imperfect-information competitive games such as StarCraft, Dota, and CounterStrike remains computationally infeasible due to sparse rewards and challenging exploration over long horizons. In this paper, we propose a multi-agent starting-state sampling strategy designed to substantially accelerate online exploration in regularized policy-gradient game methods for two-player zero-sum (2p0s) games. Motivated by an assumption that offline demonstrations from skilled humans can provide good coverage of high-level strategies relevant to equilibrium play, we propose the initialization of reinforcement learning data collection at intermediate states sampled from offline data to facilitate exploration of strategically relevant subgames. Referring to this method as Data-Augmented Game Starts (DAGS), we perform experiments using synthetic datasets and analytically tractable, long-horizon control variants of two-player Kuhn Poker, Goofspiel, and a counterexample game designed to penalize biased beliefs over hidden information. Under fixed computational budgets, DAGS enables regularized policy gradient methods to achieve lower exploitability in games with significantly more challenging exploration. We show that augmenting starting state distributions when solving imperfect information games can lead to biased equilibria, and we provide a straightforward mitigation to this in the form of multi-task observation flags. Finally, we release a new set of benchmark environments that drastically increase exploration challenges and state counts in existing OpenSpiel games while keeping exploitability measurements analytically tractable.

</details>


### [105] [Mechanistic Interpretability of EEG Foundation Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13930)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Rahul Thapa,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Anton Storgaard Mosquera,Nick Williams,Radu Gatej,Tue Lehn-Schiøler,Sándor Beniczky,Sadasivan Puthusserypady,James Zou,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用TopK稀疏自编码器对三个EEG基础模型（SleepFM, REVE, LaBraM）进行特征解耦分析，提出基于临床分类法的单义性评估框架，揭示模型存在的表征失败（如"破坏球"干预和临床纠缠），并通过频谱解码器将潜在操作映射为可解释的生理频率特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型在临床任务上表现优异，但其内部计算机制不透明，阻碍了临床信任；现有方法缺乏对模型隐层表征的可解释性分析框架。

Method: 在三个架构不同的EEG Transformer（SleepFM, REVE, LaBraM）的嵌入层上应用TopK稀疏自编码器提取稀疏特征字典；基于临床分类学（异常、年龄、性别、用药）进行单义性和纠缠度的基准测试；提出单一超参数流程（基于字典健康审计）跨架构迁移；通过概念引导引入"目标vs.脱靶"探针面积指标量化引导选择性；最后通过频谱解码器将干预映射回幅度谱。

Result: 发现了三种操作状态：可选择引导、已编码但纠缠、未编码；揭示了"破坏球"干预导致全局性能崩溃以及临床纠缠（如年龄-病理混杂）等关键表征失败；频谱解码成功将潜在干预转化为可解释的频率特征（如病理性慢波抑制和α频带恢复）。

Conclusion: TopK SAE结合临床分类学评估和频谱解码为EEG基础模型提供了有效的可解释性分析框架，能够识别表征缺陷并实现生理上有意义的干预。

Abstract: EEG foundation models achieve state-of-the-art clinical performance, yet the internal computations driving their predictions remain opaque: a barrier to clinical trust. We apply TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers: SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM to extract sparse feature dictionaries from their embeddings. By grounding these features in a clinical taxonomy (abnormality, age, sex, and medication), we benchmark monosemanticity and entanglement across architectures. A single hyperparameter procedure, driven by an intrinsic dictionary health audit, transfers robustly across all three architectures. Via concept steering, we introduce a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric to quantify steering selectivity and reveal three operational regimes: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded. This framework exposes critical representational failures: "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse global model performance, and clinical entanglements, such as age-pathology confounding, where it is impossible to suppress one concept without corrupting the other. Finally, a spectral decoder maps these interventions back to the amplitude spectrum, translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, such as pathological slow-wave suppression and $α$-band restoration.

</details>


### [106] [Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13933)
*Gaurav Rudravaram,Lianrui Zuo,Karthik Ramadass,Elyssa McMaster,Jongyeon Yoon,Aravind R. Krishnan,Adam M. Saunders,Chenyu Gao,Nancy R. Newlin,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Lori L. Beason Held,Murat Bilgel,Laura A. Barquero,Micah DArchangel,Tin Q. Nguyen,Laurie B. Cutting,Derek Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Daniel C. Moyer,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需手动调参的联合变分自编码器（Joint-VAE），通过架构退火（architectural annealing）在dMRI结构连接组中自适应分离采集相关变异与生物学变异，在7416个样本上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同站点、扫描仪和协议在dMRI中引入的采集差异导致结构连接组分析中的变异性，现有降维方法将变异性建模为连续变量导致采集效应被吸收，而混合潜空间模型需要手动容量调参。

Method: 提出Joint-VAE框架，通过对编码器输出进行架构退火（architectural annealing）而非损失函数退火，自适应平衡离散和连续潜变量，无需手动调参。

Result: 在N=7,416个结构连接组（年龄2-102岁，13项研究，25种采集参数组合）上，架构退火产生更强的站点学习效果（ARI=0.53, p<0.05），优于标准VAE、PCA+k-means及仅通过损失退火的混合模型。

Conclusion: 混合连续-离散潜空间配合架构退火（而非基于损失的退火）能有效无监督地捕获dMRI中的采集变异性，恢复与扫描仪和协议差异对齐的聚类。

Abstract: Acquisition differences across sites, scanners, and protocols in dMRI introduce variability that complicates structural connectome analysis. This motivates deep learning models that can represent high-dimensional connectomes in a low-dimensional space while explicitly separating acquisition-related effects from biological variation. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods model all variance as continuous, so acquisition effects often get absorbed into a continuous latent space. Recent hybrid latent-space models combine discrete and continuous components to address this, but typically require manual capacity tuning to ensure the discrete component captures the intended variability. We introduce an unsupervised framework that removes this manual tuning by architecturally annealing encoder outputs before decoding, allowing the model to adaptively balance discrete and continuous latent variables during training. To evaluate it, we curated a dataset of N=7,416 structural connectomes derived from dMRI, spanning ages 2 to 102 and 13 studies with 25 unique acquisition-parameter combinations. Of these, 5,900 are cognitively unimpaired, 877 have mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and 639 have Alzheimer's disease (AD). We compare against a standard VAE, PCA with k-means clustering, and hybrid models that anneal only through the loss function. Our architectural annealing produces stronger site learning (ARI=0.53, p<0.05) than these baselines. Results show that a hybrid continuous-discrete latent space, with architectural rather than loss-based annealing, provides a useful unsupervised mechanism for capturing acquisition variability in dMRI: by jointly modeling smooth and categorical structure, the Joint-VAE recovers clusters aligned with scanner and protocol differences.

</details>


### [107] [Controlling Logical Collapse in LLMs via Algebraic Ontology Projection over F2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12968)
*Hisashi Miyashita,Mgnite Inc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出代数本体投影（AOP）方法，将LLM隐藏状态投影到伽罗瓦域F₂中，仅用42个关系对作为代数键，在零样本包含性预测上达到93.33%准确率。同时提出语义结晶度（SC）指标量化F₂约束满足度，发现系统提示充当代数边界条件，且后层存在逻辑一致性系统性退化（后层崩塌），揭示了前向计算是迭代的代数组织过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型内部是否以形式可验证的代数结构编码本体论关系，而非仅近似学习逻辑关系。

Method: 提出代数本体投影（AOP），将LLM隐藏状态投影到伽罗瓦域F₂，基于里氏替换原则约束，仅用42个关系对作为代数键。提出语义结晶度（SC）指标量化F₂约束满足度，通过提示工程而非模型微调实现。

Result: AOP在Gemma-2 Instruct上达93.33%零样本包含性准确率，多模型家族一致达86.67%。发现后层崩塌现象（7/10条件下），后层逻辑一致性系统性退化。

Conclusion: LLM前向计算可视为迭代的代数组织过程，系统提示充当代数边界条件，仅其与指令微调的组合可防止后层崩塌。为LLM逻辑结构的形式化可访问性开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Do large language models internally encode ontological relations in a formally verifiable algebraic structure? We introduce Algebraic Ontology Projection (AOP), which projects LLM hidden states into the Galois Field F2 under Liskov Substitution Principle constraints, using only 42 relational pairs as algebraic keys. AOP achieves up to 93.33% zero-shot inclusion accuracy on unseen concept pairs (Gemma-2 Instruct with optimized prompt), with consistent 86.67% accuracy observed across multiple model families -- with no model tuning, but through prompt alone.
  This algebraic structure is strongly layer-dependent. We introduce Semantic Crystallisation (SC), a metric that quantifies F2 constraint satisfaction relative to a random baseline and predicts zero-shot accuracy without held-out data. System prompts act as algebraic boundary conditions: only their combination with instruction tuning prevents Late-layer Collapse -- a systematic degradation of logical consistency in the final layers, observed in 7 of 10 conditions. These findings reframe forward computation as an iterative process of algebraic organisation, and open a path toward LLMs whose logical structure is not merely approximated, but formally accessible.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond Mode-Seeking RL: Trajectory-Balance Post-Training for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13935)
*Saba Ahmadi,Prasanna Parthasarathi,Yufei Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TraFL方法，通过轨迹平衡目标解决扩散语言模型后训练中的轨迹锁定问题，在数学推理和代码生成任务上一致优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型的后训练方法多采用奖励最大化目标，这会导致"轨迹锁定"问题：奖励驱动的更新将概率质量过度集中到少数去噪路径上，降低了重复采样时替代正确解的覆盖范围。

Method: 提出TraFL（Trajectory Flow baLancing），采用轨迹平衡目标，将策略训练引导至以冻结参考模型为锚点的奖励倾斜目标分布。通过扩散兼容的序列级替代函数和学习的提示依赖归一化使其在扩散语言模型中实用化。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上，TraFL是唯一在所有基准长度设置下均优于基线的后训练方法，且收益随采样预算增加而持续。在Minerva Math的留出评估中保持优于基线，并在LiveCodeBench所有难度级别上取得最强结果。

Conclusion: 轨迹平衡目标能有效缓解扩散语言模型后训练中的轨迹锁定问题，实现更稳定且可扩展的性能提升。

Abstract: Diffusion language models are a promising alternative to autoregressive models, yet post-training methods for them largely adapt reward-maximizing objectives. We identify a central failure mode in this setting we call trajectory locking: sampled reward-driven updates over-concentrate probability mass onto a narrow set of denoising paths, reducing coverage of alternative correct solutions under repeated sampling. To address this, we propose TraFL (Trajectory Flow baLancing), a trajectory-balance objective that trains the policy toward a reward-tilted target distribution anchored to a frozen reference model. We make this practical for diffusion language models with a diffusion-compatible sequence-level surrogate and a learned prompt-dependent normalization. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, TraFL is the only evaluated post-training method that improves over the base model in every benchmark-length setting, with gains that persist as the sampling budget increases. The improvements transfer to held-out evaluations: TraFL stays above the base model on Minerva Math and is the strongest method on every LiveCodeBench difficulty split.

</details>


### [109] [Towards the Next Frontier of LLMs, Training on Private Data: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13936)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于联邦学习的框架，使多个数据孤岛能够在不共享私有数据的情况下联合微调共享的大语言模型，并在医疗和金融领域的四个数据集上验证了其有效性，结果表明联邦微调性能接近集中式训练且优于单机构独立学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的成功主要依赖公开数据，但医疗、金融等高监管领域中大量有价值的私有数据因隐私、法规和组织壁垒而无法共享，且这些数据在机构间呈非独立同分布（non-IID）特性。解锁这些私有分布式数据是LLM发展的下一个前沿。

Method: 基于Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台构建框架，使各节点在不交换私有数据的情况下联合微调共享LLM。采用三种参数高效微调（PEFT）策略（LoRA、QLoRA、IA3），在非IID设置下评估跨领域基准（医疗：MedQA、MedMCQA；金融：FPB、FiQA-SA）。

Result: 联邦微调性能接近集中式训练，优于单机构独立学习；从绿色AI角度，QLoRA和IA3在有限精度损失下提高了效率。

Conclusion: 联邦PEFT是一种可行的LLM适应方法，适用于数据无法共享的场景，能够兼顾隐私保护、跨机构协作和模型性能。

Abstract: The recent success of large language models (LLMs) has been largely driven by vast public datasets. However, the next frontier for LLM development lies beyond public data. Much of the world's most valuable information is private, especially in highly regulated sectors such as healthcare and finance, where data include patient histories or customer communications. Unlocking this data could represent a major leap forward, enabling LLMs with deeper domain expertise and stronger real-world utility. Yet, these data cannot be shared because they are distributed across institutions and constrained by privacy, regulatory, and organizational barriers. Moreover, institutional datasets are typically non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID), differing across sites in population characteristics, data modalities, documentation patterns, and task-specific label distributions.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a practical approach to unlocking private and distributed institutional data for LLM adaptation through federated collaboration across data silos. Built on the Sherpa.ai Federated Learning platform, our framework enables nodes to jointly fine-tune a shared LLM without exchanging private data. We evaluate this approach through a cross-domain benchmark in healthcare and finance, using four closed-ended question answering and classification datasets: MedQA, MedMCQA, FPB, and FiQA-SA. We compare three parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategies-LoRA, QLoRA, and IA3-across pretrained backbones under non-IID settings reflecting institutional data heterogeneity. Our results show that federated fine-tuning performs close to centralized training and outperforms isolated single-institution learning. From a Green AI perspective, QLoRA and IA3 improve efficiency with limited accuracy degradation, supporting federated PEFT as a viable approach for adapting LLMs where data cannot be shared.

</details>


### [110] [An Amortized Efficiency Threshold for Comparing Neural and Heuristic Solvers in Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14624)
*Sohaib Afifi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判了“神经组合优化求解器训练成本高→整体效率低”这一常见论断，指出其忽略了部署规模的影响。作者定义了**摊销效率阈值（AET）**，即神经求解器在总能耗/碳排放上与启发式基线打平所需的部署实例数。实验表明，在n=20的MTVRP环境下，交叉点约为1.58×10^5次部署，单实例能耗比为0.41，证明了神经求解器在足够大规模部署下的能效优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对“神经组合优化求解器因GPU训练能耗高而整体不如CPU元启发式高效”这一常见但未经验证的批评，指出其逻辑跳跃在于忽略了训练成本是固定的一次性投入，而启发式求解的成本随部署实例数线性增长，因此需要引入部署量这一变量来公平比较。

Method: 定义了**摊销效率阈值（AET）**，即在给定解质量约束下，神经求解器与启发式基线在总能耗/碳排放上打平所需的部署实例数。证明了当神经求解器单实例能耗占优时，两者累积能耗比趋向一个小于1的常数，且该极限不依赖于训练成本的度量方式。在MTVRP环境（n=20，19个变体，5个训练种子）上以HGS（PyVRP）为基线进行实验测量。

Result: 实验测得的交叉点约为1.58×10^5次部署；单实例能耗比为0.41（神经求解器更低）。该结果反映了小规模实例（n=20）下的情况，更大规模问题的结构收敛性留待未来研究。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于提出了AET框架、开放了实验工具链和测量协议。核心结论是：神经求解器的能效批评忽略了摊销效应，在足够大规模的部署下神经方法可以更节能；但大问题尺寸下结论是否成立仍需实证验证。

Abstract: A common critique of neural combinatorial-optimization solvers is that they are less energy-efficient than CPU metaheuristics, given the operational energy cost of training them on GPUs. This paper examines the inferential step from "training is expensive" to "neural solvers are net-inefficient", which is where the critique actually goes wrong. Training the network costs a large fixed amount of GPU energy; running the metaheuristic costs a small amount of CPU energy on every instance, repeated as long as the solver is deployed. The two are not commensurable until a deployment volume is fixed. We define the Amortized Efficiency Threshold (AET) as the deployment volume above which a neural solver breaks even with a heuristic baseline in total energy or carbon, under an explicit constraint on solution quality. We show that the cumulative-energy ratio between the two solvers tends to a constant strictly below one whenever the network wins per-instance, and that this limit does not depend on how the training cost was measured. An embodied-carbon term amortizes hardware fabrication symmetrically on both sides. We instantiate the framework on the Multi-Task VRP (MTVRP) environment at n=20 customers across 19 problem variants and five training seeds, with HGS via PyVRP as the heuristic baseline. The measured crossover sits near $1.58 \times 10^5$ deployed instances; the per-instance ratio is 0.41, reflecting the moderate size of the instances tested. The contribution is the framework, the open instrumentation, and the measurement protocol; structural convergence of the ratio at larger problem sizes is left to future empirical work.

</details>


### [111] [EvolveMem:Self-Evolving Memory Architecture via AutoResearch for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13941)
*Jiaqi Liu,Xinyu Ye,Peng Xia,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Mingyu Ding,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvolveMem提出一种自进化记忆架构，不仅存储内容会更新，检索机制（评分函数、融合策略、答案生成）也会通过LLM驱动的诊断模块自动调优，形成闭环自研究过程。在LoCoMo和MemBench上分别比最强基线提升25.7%和18.9%，且进化策略可跨基准迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆系统将检索基础设施视为固定组件，存储内容可演化但检索机制的评分函数、融合策略和答案生成策略在部署后冻结，导致记忆系统缺乏真正的自适应性。

Method: 将完整检索配置暴露为结构化动作空间，由LLM驱动的诊断模块读取每问题失败日志、识别根本原因并提议配置调整，配合带自动回退和探索机制的元分析器，形成闭环自进化过程（AutoResearch）。

Result: LoCoMo上相比最强基线提升25.7%（相对值），相比最简基线提升78.0%；MemBench上相比最强基线提升18.9%（相对值）。进化配置可跨基准正迁移。

Conclusion: 自进化过程能自主发现通用检索原理而非基准特定启发式，实现从手动调参到自动化架构研究的转变。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that operate across multiple sessions, yet existing memory systems treat retrieval infrastructure as fixed: stored content evolves while scoring functions, fusion strategies, and answer-generation policies remain frozen at deployment. We argue that truly adaptive memory requires co-evolution at two levels: the stored knowledge and the retrieval mechanism that queries it. We present EvolveMem, a self-evolving memory architecture that exposes its full retrieval configuration as a structured action space optimized by an LLM-powered diagnosis module. In each evolution round, the module reads per-question failure logs, identifies root causes, and proposes targeted configuration adjustments; a guarded meta-analyzer applies them with automatic revert-on-regression and explore-on-stagnation safeguards. This closed-loop self-evolution realizes an AutoResearch process: the system autonomously conducts iterative research cycles on its own architecture, replacing manual configuration tuning. Starting from a minimal baseline, the process converges autonomously, discovering effective retrieval strategies including entirely new configuration dimensions not present in the original action space. On LoCoMo, EvolveMem outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.7% relative and achieves a 78.0% relative improvement over the minimal baseline. On MemBench, EvolveMem exceeds the strongest baseline by 18.9% relative. Evolved configurations transfer across benchmarks with positive rather than catastrophic transfer, indicating that the self-evolution process captures universal retrieval principles rather than benchmark-specific heuristics. Code is available at https://github.com/aiming-lab/SimpleMem.

</details>


### [112] [Beyond Binary: Reframing GUI Critique as Continuous Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14311)
*Yuchen Sun,Pei Fu,Shaojie Zhang,Anan Du,Xiuwen Xi,Ruoceng Zhang,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan,Chongyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BBCritic，将GUI操作评估从二分类范式转变为基于功能等价假设的层次化对比学习，通过恢复被二分类压平的层级化可行空间，显著提升了细粒度排序能力；同时构建了包含4级层次化分类的基准BBBench。BBCritic-3B无需额外标注即超越7B SOTA二分类模型，并展现出强零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI批评模型统一采用二分类，导致有效操作与看似合理但无效的干扰项得分严重混淆。作者将问题归结为两个结构性缺陷：可行空间坍塌（层次化可行空间被压缩为0/1标签）和噪声敏感性（二分类目标过拟合到噪声决策边界）。

Method: 提出BBCritic，基于功能等价假设，通过两阶段对比学习将指令和操作对齐到共享的可行空间中，恢复被二分类压平的层次化结构。同时构建BBBench基准，包含密集操作空间和四级层次化分类体系。

Result: BBCritic-3B在无需额外标注的情况下，超越了7B参数的SOTA二分类模型，并在跨平台和跨任务场景中展现出强大的零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: GUI操作批评本质上是一个度量学习问题，而非分类问题。从二分类转向基于对比学习的层次化可行空间建模，能够显著提升细粒度排序能力。

Abstract: Test-Time Scaling (TTS), which samples multiple candidate actions and ranks them via a Critic Model, has emerged as a promising paradigm for generalist GUI agents. Its efficacy thus hinges on the critic's fine-grained ranking ability. However, existing GUI critic models uniformly adopt binary classification. Our motivational analysis of these models exposes a severe entanglement: scores for valid actions and plausible-but-invalid distractors become indistinguishable. We attribute this failure to two structural defects: Affordance Collapse--the hierarchical affordance space is compressed into 0/1 labels; and Noise Sensitivity--binary objectives overfit to noisy decision boundaries. To resolve this, we introduce BBCritic (Beyond-Binary Critic), a paradigm shift grounded in the Functional Equivalence Hypothesis. Through two-stage contrastive learning, BBCritic aligns instructions and actions in a shared Affordance Space, recovering the hierarchical structure that binary supervision flattens. We also present BBBench (Beyond-Binary Bench), the first GUI critic benchmark that pairs a dense action space with a hierarchical four-level taxonomy, enabling fine-grained ranking evaluation. Experimental results show that BBCritic-3B, trained without any extra annotation, outperforms 7B-parameter SOTA binary models. It demonstrates strong zero-shot transferability across platforms and tasks, supporting our methodological view: GUI critique is fundamentally a metric-learning problem, not a classification one.

</details>


### [113] [A Unified Geometric Framework for Weighted Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13943)
*Raphael Vock,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了对比学习中的加权InfoNCE目标可被解释为距离几何问题，权重方案决定了目标几何能否被实现。研究分析了多种监督/弱监督场景下的最优嵌入几何：平衡SupCon恢复经典正则单纯形，类不平衡导致非均匀类间相似度，而Soft SupCon保持单纯形几何；连续标签下y-Aware CL存在几何不匹配问题，只有几何一致的设计（如欧几里得-欧几里得加权或X-CLR）才具有唯一最优嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习旨在通过表示学习保留样本间的关系结构，但其嵌入结果的几何性质尚不明确。作者希望从距离几何视角统一理解加权对比学习目标的优化行为与几何特性。

Method: 将加权InfoNCE目标函数重新解释为距离几何问题，通过理论分析刻画不同权重方案下最优嵌入的几何结构。对监督对比学习(SupCon)、Soft SupCon、类平衡/不平衡场景、连续标签场景(y-Aware CL)及X-CLR等进行了系统分析。

Result: (1) 平衡SupCon恢复正则单纯形，类不平衡打破对称性；(2) Soft SupCon无论类别平衡与否均保持正则单纯形；(3) y-Aware CL在连续标签场景中除非标签位于超球面，否则无法达到熵最优；(4) 欧几里得-欧几里得加权和X-CLR可得到唯一最优嵌入。

Conclusion: 对比学习的权重方案决定了其几何可实现性：有些方案退化为退化结构，有些存在几何不匹配。该框架为设计几何一致的对比学习目标提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Contrastive learning (CL) aims to preserve relational structure between samples by learning representations that reflect a similarity graph. Yet, the geometry of the resulting embeddings remains poorly understood. Here we show that weighted InfoNCE objectives can be interpreted as Distance Geometry Problems, where the weighting scheme specifies the target geometry to be realized by the representation. This viewpoint yields exact characterizations of the optimal embeddings for several supervised and weakly supervised objectives. In supervised classification, both SupCon and Soft SupCon (a dense relaxation of it where pairs from distinct classes have small non-zero similarity) collapse samples within each class to a single prototype. However, while balanced SupCon recovers the classical regular simplex geometry, class imbalance breaks this symmetry: SupCon induces non-uniform inter-class similarities depending on class sizes, whereas Soft SupCon preserves a regular simplex geometry regardless of class imbalance. In continuous-label settings, our framework reveals a different failure mode: y-Aware CL generally cannot attain its entropic optimum unless the labels lie on a hypersphere, exposing a mismatch between Euclidean label weights and spherical latent similarity. By contrast, geometrically consistent choices such as Euclidean-Euclidean weighting or X-CLR admit unique optimal embeddings. Our results show that the choice of weighting scheme determines whether contrastive learning is geometrically realizable, degenerate, or inconsistent, providing a principled framework for designing contrastive objectives.

</details>


### [114] [WarmPrior: Straightening Flow-Matching Policies with Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13959)
*Sinjae Kang,Chanyoung Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WarmPrior，将生成式机器人控制中的标准高斯源分布替换为基于近期动作历史构建的时间接地先验分布，通过产生更直的路径提升操作任务成功率，并在先验空间强化学习中改善探索分布与样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于扩散和流匹配的生成式策略在视觉运动机器人控制中占据主导地位，但源分布（标准高斯分布）这一设计维度尚未被充分探索。作者发现源分布的选择对生成路径的曲率和任务成功率有显著影响，因此希望通过引入更好的先验分布来提升性能。

Method: 提出WarmPrior，一种时间接地的先验分布，其构建方式是利用近期可用的动作历史信息。将标准高斯源分布替换为WarmPrior，并将其应用于行为克隆和先验空间强化学习中。

Result: 在机器人操作任务上，WarmPrior一致性地提升了成功率。其效果归因于产生了显著更直的概率路径，类似于Rectified Flow中最优传输耦合的作用。在先验空间强化学习中，WarmPrior重塑了探索分布，同时提升了样本效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: 源分布是生成式机器人控制中一个重要但被忽视的设计轴，WarmPrior作为一种简单有效的改进方法，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching have become a dominant paradigm for visuomotor robotic control. We show that replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution with WarmPrior, a simple temporally grounded prior constructed from readily available recent action history, consistently improves success rates on robotic manipulation tasks. We trace this gain to markedly straighter probability paths, echoing the effect of optimal-transport couplings in Rectified Flow. Beyond standard behavior cloning, WarmPrior also reshapes the exploration distribution in prior-space reinforcement learning, improving both sample efficiency and final performance. Collectively, these results identify the source distribution as an important and underexplored design axis in generative robot control.

</details>


### [115] [Towards Resource-Efficient LLMs: End-to-End Energy Accounting of Distillation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13981)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个全面的能源核算框架，用于测量蒸馏管道的完整计算成本，通过分阶段跟踪GPU功耗，揭示了传统知识蒸馏和合成数据微调两种方法中此前被忽略的能源与排放成本，并给出了能源约束下的方法选择和超参数设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型部署导致GPU需求和数据中心规模激增，引发电力消耗和电网压力问题。蒸馏常被宣传为获得更便宜、更高效模型的有效途径，但这些说法很少考虑完整的端到端能源和资源成本，包括教师端的数据生成、logit缓存和评估等关键负载。

Method: 提出了一个全面的能源核算框架，通过详细的阶段跟踪GPU设备功耗来测量蒸馏管道的完整计算成本。在实验中分离并记录不同阶段的实测能耗，系统测量了两种常见蒸馏方法（基于logit的知识蒸馏和合成数据监督微调）的能耗与排放，构建了能量-质量帕累托前沿。

Result: 构建了能量-质量帕累托前沿，揭示了此前被忽略的蒸馏成本，并基于测量和分析得出了在能源和预算约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数的实用设计规则。

Conclusion: 发布了开源测量工具和核算协议，为可比较、可复现的蒸馏研究提供标准化基础，明确考虑完整管道的能源影响。

Abstract: The rise in deployment of large language models has driven a surge in GPU demand and datacenter scaling, raising concerns about electricity use, grid stress, and the impacts of modern AI workloads. Distillation is often promoted as one of the most effective paths to obtain cheaper, more efficient models, yet these claims rarely account for the full end-to-end energy and resource costs, including crucial teacher-side workloads such as data generation, logit caching, and evaluation. We present a comprehensive energy accounting framework that measures the complete computational cost of distillation pipelines via detailed stage-wise tracking of GPU device power consumption. In our experiments, we separate and log empirical energy use across distinct phases and systematically measure the energy and emissions of two common distillation methods: the classic logit-based knowledge distillation and synthetic-data supervised fine-tuning, constructing energy-quality Pareto frontiers that expose the previously ignored costs. From these measurements and analyses, we derive practical design rules for selecting distillation methods and hyperparameters under energy and budget constraints, and release an open-source measurement harness and accounting protocol to provide a standardized foundation for comparable, reproducible distillation research, explicitly accountable for complete pipeline energy impact.

</details>


### [116] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin,Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3是面向表格数据的下一代基础模型，支持100万训练样本，在标准表格基准TabArena上以单次前向传播超越所有调优和集成基线，通过测试时计算扩展进一步领先200-420 Elo，同时比前代快20倍，比AutoGluon快10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据是科学和工业中最具价值的高维预测问题的基础，TabPFN已推动该模态的基座模型变革。为满足用户需求，需要将SOTA性能扩展到100万训练行级数据集，并大幅降低训练和推理时间。

Method: 完全使用来自先验分布的合成数据预训练；引入测试时计算扩展（Test-Time Compute Scaling），即TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking)版本；采用缩减KV缓存和行分块（row-chunking）技术以在单张H100上扩展到100万行；提供专门的时间序列检查点TabPFN-TS-3。

Result: TabArena基准上以单次前向传播超越所有调优和集成基线，帕累托支配速度/性能前沿；TabPFN-3-Plus在TabArena上超越所有非TabPFN模型200+ Elo，最大数据子集上领先420 Elo；比AutoGluon 1.5 extreme快10倍；比TabPFN-2.5快20倍；RelBenchV1上达到新SOTA；TabSTAR上达到SOTA；fev-bench上排名第2；SHAP计算加速120倍。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3通过合成数据预训练、测试时计算扩展和工程优化，在表格、时间序列、关系型及表格-文本混合数据上均实现SOTA，标志着表格基础模型在规模、速度和性能上的重大突破。

Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

</details>


### [117] [HodgeCover: Higher-Order Topological Coverage Drives Compression of Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13997)
*Tao Zhong,Dongzhe Zheng,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了稀疏MoE层压缩中存在的三元组不可合并障碍（harmonic kernel），提出HodgeCover方法通过Hodge分解识别并覆盖这些关键瓶颈，在激进压缩场景下达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于成对信号（如KL散度）的专家压缩方法存在结构性盲区：三个专家两两兼容但三者合并时形成不可约环，导致任何成对评分都无法判断三元组是否可联合合并。这种障碍阻碍了无重训练压缩方法的有效性。

Method: 1) 将障碍形式化为2-复形上单纯拉普拉斯算子的调和核（harmonic kernel）；2) 对边携带的KL屏障信号进行Hodge分解，隔离调和核；3) 提出HodgeCover算法，贪婪覆盖调和关键边和三元组关键三角形；4) 混合变体将HodgeCover与现有权重剪枝结合。

Result: 在三个开源稀疏MoE模型上进行激进的专家缩减，HodgeCover在专家缩减轴上匹配SOTA无重训练基线，在混合轴（激进压缩前沿）上领先，且唯一地在所有四个Hodge分量上保持质量平衡。

Conclusion: 揭示并利用MoE结构的调和核改变了压缩器的性能格局，在最重要的激进压缩场景下取得最优结果。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers route tokens through a handful of experts, and learning-free compression of these layers reduces inference cost without retraining. A subtle obstruction blocks every existing compressor in this family: three experts can each be pairwise compatible yet form an irreducible cycle when merged together, so any score that ranks experts on pairwise signals is structurally blind to which triples are jointly mergeable. We show the obstruction is a precise mathematical object, the harmonic kernel of the simplicial Laplacian on a 2-complex whose vertices are experts, whose edges carry KL merge barriers, and whose faces carry triplet barriers; Hodge-decomposing the edge-barrier signal isolates the kernel exactly. We turn the diagnostic into a selection objective: HodgeCover greedily covers the harmonic-critical edges and triplet-critical triangles, and a hybrid variant of HodgeCover pairs it with off-the-shelf weight pruning on survivors. On three open-weight Sparse MoE backbones under aggressive expert reduction, HodgeCover matches state-of-the-art learning-free baselines on the expert-reduction axis, leads on the aggressive-compression frontier of the hybrid axis, and uniquely balances retained mass across all four Hodge components. These results show that exposing the harmonic kernel of a learned MoE structure changes which compressor wins at the regime that matters most.

</details>


### [118] [Support Before Frequency in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13999)
*Adrian Müller,Antoine Gonon,Zebang Shen,Ya-Ping Hsieh,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现离散扩散模型在去噪过程中会自然形成层次结构：先学习数据的支撑集（如语法有效句），再学习支撑集内的频率分布。均匀扩散和吸收扩散在机制上存在差异，前者存在三种编辑类型（改善/保持/恶化有效性），后者主导项集中在改善有效性的移动上。实验验证了支撑定位早于频率排序的学习顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在语言建模中表现日益出色，但其去噪目标函数如何组织学习过程尚不清楚。研究者希望揭示这些模型学习数据分布的内在机制和层次结构。

Method: 通过理论分析证明在均匀扩散和吸收扩散的小噪声区域（最终去噪步骤）中，每个单token反向编辑可分解为尺度主导项（决定是否向数据支撑集移动）和精细系数（决定同一尺度内的相对概率）。实验采用掩码语言扩散模型和合成正则语言任务进行验证。

Result: 支撑定位（区分语法有效性）比支撑内频率排序更早出现；均匀扩散与吸收扩散之间的差异符合理论预测的速率分离；均匀扩散呈现三分性（有效性改善/保持/恶化），吸收扩散主导项为有效性改善移动。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型在去噪过程中优先学习数据支撑集（如语法结构），然后才学习支撑集内的频率分布，揭示了模型学习的层次化组织规律。不同扩散机制（均匀vs吸收）在编辑类型分布上有本质差异。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are increasingly competitive for language modeling, yet it remains unclear how their denoising objectives organize learning. Although these objectives target the full data distribution, we show that the exact reverse process induces a hierarchy between coarse support information and finer frequency information. For uniform and absorbing (a.k.a. masking) diffusion, we prove that, in the small-noise regime of the final denoising steps, each single-token reverse edit decomposes into a leading scale, determined by whether it moves toward the data support (e.g., grammatically valid sentences), and a finer coefficient, determining relative probabilities within the same scale. Thus, recovering validity structure only requires learning the correct order of magnitude of reverse probabilities, whereas recovering data frequencies requires coefficient-level estimation. The separation is mechanism-dependent: uniform diffusion exhibits a trichotomy into validity-improving, validity-preserving, and validity-worsening edits, while absorbing diffusion places its leading-order mass on validity-improving moves. Experiments on a masked language diffusion model and synthetic regular-language tasks support these predictions: support-localization emerges earlier than within-support frequency ranking, and the contrast between uniform and absorbing diffusion matches the predicted rate separation. Together, our results suggest that discrete diffusion models learn data support before data frequencies.

</details>


### [119] [Dywave: Event-Aligned Dynamic Tokenization for Heterogeneous IoT Sensing Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14014)
*Tomoyoshi Kimura,Denizhan Kara,Jinyang Li,Hongjue Zhao,Yigong Hu,Yizhuo Chen,Xiaomin Ouyang,Shengzhong Liu,Tarek Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dywave是一种面向IoT传感信号的动态分词框架，利用小波层次分解识别语义事件边界，自适应压缩冗余区间。在五个真实数据集上，相比SOTA方法准确率提升最高12%，输入token长度减少最高75%，同时提升了对域偏移和变长序列的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT系统持续采集异构传感信号以支持智能应用（如人类活动分析、情绪监测、环境感知），但这些信号具有非平稳和多尺度的特性，标准分词技术难以有效处理。

Method: 提出Dywave动态分词框架，采用基于小波的层次化分解，识别与底层语义事件对齐的有意义时间边界，自适应压缩冗余区间同时保持时间连贯性。

Result: 在五个真实IoT传感数据集（涵盖活动识别、压力评估、近场物体检测）上，准确率比SOTA方法提升最高12%，主流序列模型的输入token长度减少最高75%，并展现出更好的域偏移和变长序列鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Dywave通过动态分词有效提升了IoT传感信号处理的准确性和计算效率，并增强了模型对域偏移和序列长度变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems continuously collect heterogeneous sensing signals from ubiquitous sensors to support intelligent applications such as human activity analysis, emotion monitoring, and environmental perception. These signals are inherently non-stationary and multi-scale, posing unique challenges for standard tokenization techniques. This paper proposes Dywave, a dynamic tokenization framework for IoT sensing signals that constructs compact input representations aligned with intrinsic temporal structures and underlying physical events. Dywave leverages wavelet-based hierarchical decomposition, identifies meaningful temporal boundaries corresponding to underlying semantic events, and adaptively compresses redundant intervals while preserving temporal coherence.
